In preliminary referential games, target repetition coincided with shorter successful messages, while predictability from a hidden cyclic rule did not shorten them. Experiments varying memory and message rates can estimate the frontier and test this prediction across cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [167] [DiSCO: Defending text-to-image generation through distribution-guided contrastive prompt optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17067)
*Tong Zhang,Motasem Alfarra,Carlos Hinojosa,Christos Louizos,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DiSCO是一种零样本、纯黑盒的文本到图像模型防御方法，通过提示词级别的分布引导后缀扩展（beam search）和对比评分，有效降低NSFW内容生成率，无需访问模型内部或重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法大多依赖白盒假设（如文本编码器优化、权重编辑或推理时干预），无法扩展到专有模型；基于LLM提示重写的黑盒方法虽然在多数情况下有效，但在"良性对抗"（benign adversarial）问题上失效——即提示词在语言学上是安全的，但因其数据分布仍触发有害内容生成。

Method: DiSCO完全在提示词层面运行，作为即插即用模块。它通过 beam search 进行分布引导的后缀扩展，使用目标模型自身生成的"安全图片池"和"不安全图片池"进行对比评分（contrastive scoring），并通过迭代自适应反馈持续优化，直到生成安全内容为止。

Result: 在I2P基准上，DiSCO对未防御和已防御模型均能一致提升安全性，在多种红队攻击下分别实现37.7%和25.13%的攻击成功率（ASR）降低，同时保持语义保真度并改善图像连贯性。

Conclusion: DiSCO作为黑盒、架构无关的模块，可即插即用地应用于任何文本到图像系统，无需对模型本身做任何改动，为专有模型的NSFW内容防御提供了实用的可扩展方案。

Abstract: As text-to-image generative models advance, they raise critical safety concerns, particularly the generation of Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content such as violence and nudity, further exacerbated by red-teaming adversarial attacks. Existing defenses predominantly operate under white-box assumptions, relying on text encoder optimization, weight editing, or inference-time intervention, and fundamentally cannot scale to proprietary models. Black-box alternatives based on LLM prompt rewriting offer broader applicability, yet fail in a critical regime we identify as the \textit{benign adversarial} problem: prompts that are linguistically safe but still trigger harmful generation due to the model's learned data distribution. We propose DiSCO, a zero-shot, strictly black-box defense that operates entirely at the prompt level as a plug-and-play module, requiring no model retraining, fine-tuning, or access to model internals. DiSCO performs distribution-guided suffix expansion via beam search, optimized through contrastive scoring over safe and unsafe image pools generated by the target model itself, with iterative adaptive feedback until safe content is produced. We demonstrate that DiSCO consistently enhances the safety of both undefended and defended models on the I2P benchmark under multiple red-teaming attacks, achieving 37.7% and 25.13% ASR reduction, respectively, while maintaining semantic fidelity and improving image coherence. As a black-box, architecture-agnostic module, DiSCO can be readily applied to any text-to-image system without necessitating any changes to the model itself.

</details>


### [168] [KernelArc: A Multi-Agent Framework for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17071)
*Joyjit Kundu,Ben Stoffelen,Kaili Wang,Peter Vrancx,Ludovic Denoyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KernelArc，一个用于异构工作负载下自主GPU内核优化的多智能体框架。该框架通过策略专精的并行智能体、结论共享内存、确定性基准守卫和只读跨智能体状态进行协调。在NVIDIA H100和B200 GPU上使用SOL-ExecBench工作负载进行评估，生成的实现包括自定义BF16 GEMM、静态cuBLASLt专家API配置表、融合MoE反向传播、形状门控解码器层融合、原生NVFP4分组查询注意力和分页预填充注意力。在2026年7月30日的SOL-ExecBench公开排行榜快照中，这些提交在代表性L1、L2、量化及FlashInfer任务中排名第一。实验轨迹支持核心动机：共享多智能体搜索能在固定候选预算内拓宽探索并达到更强的基线，而各协调特征的价值取决于具体内核和优化阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化的搜索空间庞大且异构，单一搜索策略难以覆盖不同工作负载的多样化需求。作者希望通过多智能体并行协调来拓宽探索范围，在固定候选预算内找到更强的最优解（incumbent），从而提升内核优化的效率和效果。

Method: 提出KernelArc多智能体框架，包含几个关键设计：(1) 策略专精的智能体并行运行；(2) 智能体之间通过"结论-only"共享内存进行协调；(3) 引入确定性基准守卫（deterministic benchmark guard）确保评估可靠性；(4) 使用只读跨智能体状态，并配合高原触发的草稿生成（plateau-triggered drafting）机制来实现协调。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100和B200 GPU上的SOL-ExecBench工作负载评估中，KernelArc生成的内核实现涵盖多种复杂优化场景（BF16 GEMM、cuBLASLt配置、MoE反向、解码器融合、NVFP4 GQA、分页预填充注意力）。在2026年7月30日的公开排行榜快照中，这些提交在代表性L1、L2、量化及FlashInfer任务中均排名第一。

Conclusion: 共享多智能体搜索确实能够拓宽探索范围并在固定预算内达到更强的候选解；但不同协调特征（如结论共享、基准守卫、跨智能体草稿等）的价值因内核类型和优化阶段的不同而有所差异，不能一概而论。

Abstract: We present KernelArc, a multi-agent framework for autonomous GPU kernel optimization across heterogeneous workloads. Strategy-specialized agents run in parallel and coordinate through conclusions-only shared memory, a deterministic benchmark guard, and read-only cross-agent state with plateau-triggered drafting. We evaluate \kernelarc{} on NVIDIA H100 and B200 GPUs using category-representative SOL-ExecBench workloads. The resulting implementations span custom BF16 GEMM, static cuBLASLt Expert-API configuration tables, fused mixture-of-experts backward, shape-gated decoder-layer fusion, native NVFP4 grouped-query attention, and paged prefill attention. At the public SOL-ExecBench leaderboard snapshot recorded on July~30, 2026, these submissions ranked first on representative L1, L2, Quantization, and FlashInfer tasks. The trajectories support the paper's central motivation: shared multi-agent search can broaden exploration and reach stronger incumbents within a fixed candidate budget, while the value of individual coordination features depends on the kernel and optimization stage.

</details>


### [169] [A decodability criterion predicts when hidden-state selection beats majority voting in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17124)
*Zhixiang wang,Ziliang Hong,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CASE（Correctness-Axis SElection），一种基于隐藏状态打分来动态选择答案候选的测试时信息融合方法，并引入可解码性（decodability）指标来预测该方法相较于多数投票的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票在困难问题上不可靠，因为采样答案共享相关错误，错误答案可能获胜，增加采样甚至使结果更糟。基于隐藏状态读取出正确性信号是替代方案，但其准确性因模型和任务而异，且缺乏可信任的度量指标。

Method: CASE在答案token的隐藏状态上训练一个线性门控，选择得分最高的候选答案。核心贡献是可解码性指标，这是一种无泄漏的度量，衡量门控将正确候选排在错误候选之上的能力，用于预测隐藏状态选择是否优于投票。

Result: 在留出数据上，可解码性预测选择相对投票的准确率增益，Pearson相关系数r=0.75，决策阈值接近AUC=0.60。在通用和医学LLM上，CASE在中难度问题上最高提升19个点，在困难问题上提升16.8个点。可解码性预测可迁移到未见科学领域（误差在3.8个点内）。

Conclusion: 可解码性取决于模型必须回忆的对齐知识而非模型规模，可为给定模型和任务提供实用的提前测量标准，用于在习得式选择和多数投票之间做出选择。

Abstract: Combining the answers a large language model (LLM) samples for a question into one decision is a test-time information fusion problem, usually solved by majority voting. Voting is unreliable on difficult questions, where the sampled answers share correlated errors, so the wrong answer can win and drawing more samples makes the decision worse. Selecting a candidate by reading a correctness signal from the model's hidden states is a promising alternative, but its accuracy varies across models and tasks, and no measure indicates when it can be trusted. In this paper, we propose CASE (Correctness-Axis SElection), a dynamic selection combiner that trains a linear gate on the answer-token hidden state and selects the highest-scoring candidate. Its main contribution is decodability, a leakage-free measure of how well the gate ranks a question's correct candidates above its incorrect ones, which predicts whether hidden-state selection will outperform voting. A conventional probe appears accurate only because of question-identity leakage, which vanishes under question-grouped evaluation. On held-out data, decodability predicts the accuracy gain of selection over voting with a Pearson correlation r=0.75 and a decision threshold near AUC=0.60. Across general and medical LLMs, CASE improves over voting by up to 19 points on medium-difficulty questions and 16.8 points on hard questions. Decodability depends on the aligned knowledge a model must recall, not on its scale, and its prediction transfers to an unseen scientific domain within 3.8 points. It thus provides a practical criterion, measurable in advance for a given model and task, for choosing between learned selection and majority voting.

</details>


### [170] [Toward Personal Intelligence Through Cooperative Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17128)
*Yashar Talebirad,Osman Jime,Ali Parsaee,Eden Redman,Yongbin Kim,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"合作式观察"（cooperative observation）框架，认为个人AI系统与用户之间存在一个关于观察权限的反馈循环：系统通过观察构建用户模型，用户根据系统行为的有用性和可检查性决定是否维持、扩大或收回观察通道。作者基于Organizm原型六个月的单一被试使用报告，初步验证了这一框架，并提出了评估方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI系统需要建立用户目标、约束和持续承诺的模型来代表用户行事，但模型质量受限于系统能观察到的信息。然而，仅仅扩大观察范围并不能自动提升辅助质量，因为资源受限系统必须为当前任务选择和压缩信息。作者指出观察瓶颈具有合作性结构，现有研究缺乏对这一反馈循环的系统性框架。

Method: 1. 提出"合作式观察"框架，将观察瓶颈描述为有用性（usefulness）、信任（trust）和未来访问权限（future access）之间的反馈循环。2. 构建Organizm原型系统，进行为期六个月的单一被试（single-subject）纵向使用研究。3. 基于使用观察报告分析系统行为如何影响用户对观察通道的维持、扩张或收缩决策。4. 提出评估观察质量如何塑造个人AI的评估方向。

Result: 初步的单一被试报告来自Organizm原型系统的六个月使用，提供了合作式观察循环的实际证据——系统展示了有用且可检查的行为，用户因此维持或扩展了观察权限；而系统失败时用户会纠正、收窄或撤销观察权限。论文还勾勒了未来评估方向，用于定量衡量观察质量对个人AI性能的影响。

Conclusion: 合作式观察为个人智能提供了一种框架性理解：个人AI的有效性不仅取决于其算法能力，更取决于系统能否通过有用和可检查的行为赢得用户的持续观察合作。观察不只是被动获取信息的通道，而是与信任、同意和用户控制动态交织的主动关系。

Abstract: A personal AI system needs a model of the user's goals, constraints, and ongoing commitments to plan and act on their behalf, and the quality of that model is bounded by what the system can observe. Broader observation does not by itself improve assistance because a bounded system must select and compress information for the task at hand. We argue that this observation bottleneck has a cooperative structure: the system builds a partial model of the user's changing life, the user evaluates its actions, and the user's consent and control shape what it can observe next. Useful and inspectable behavior can give users a reason to maintain or expand the observation channel, while failures can lead them to correct, narrow, revoke, or abandon it. We use the term cooperative observation for this feedback loop among usefulness, trust, and future access, and propose it as a framework for personal intelligence. We report a preliminary single-subject account from Organizm, a prototype used over six months, and outline evaluation directions for measuring how observation quality shapes personal AI.

</details>


### [171] [KnowSim: Evaluating Information Calibration in LLM Assistants with User Simulators that Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17150)
*Yoonjoo Lee,Hyoungwook Jin,Tae Soo Kim,Shaoyang Zhang,Philippe Laban,Q. Vera Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KNOWSIM是一个评估框架，通过维护显式知识状态的用户模拟器来评估LLM的信息校准能力。它使用带前置关系的信息单元图表示知识，并依据学习理论规则演化，计算三个指标（知识增益、传递校准、认知负荷）。在705个人类-AI会话中验证，与人类判断73-74%的一致性，优于三个基线模拟器，并揭示了标准评估无法发现的能力-处理交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM需与用户有效协作，必须进行信息校准（使内容匹配用户不断演化的理解与认知能力）。然而现有用户模拟器未显式建模用户知识，无法在不同知识水平下产生真实交互，也无法反映知识演化过程中交互的变化。

Method: 构建KNOWSIM框架：1）用户模拟器维护显式知识状态，表示为带前置关系的信息单元图；2）知识状态依据基于学习理论的更新规则演化；3）从知识状态轨迹计算三个指标（知识增益、传递校准、认知负荷）；4）在两个领域的705个人类-AI会话中验证，并与9个LLM进行应用测试。

Result: KNOWSIM的排序与人类判断显著一致（73-74%符号一致性），优于三个基线模拟器。应用于9个LLM时，发现最佳模型随用户知识水平而改变，揭示了标准评估不可见的"能力-处理交互"效应。

Conclusion: KNOWSIM通过显式建模用户知识状态及其演化过程，提供了比现有基线更准确、更细致的信息校准评估工具，能够揭示模型在不同用户知识水平下的差异化表现，为LLM的用户交互评估提供了新的视角。

Abstract: To effectively collaborate with users on knowledge-intensive tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) must perform information calibration: matching content to a user's evolving understanding and cognitive capacity. Yet user simulators used to evaluate and train LLMs do not explicitly model user knowledge so they neither produce realistic interactions across knowledge levels nor reflect how interactions unfold as that knowledge evolves. To close this gap, we introduce KNOWSIM, an evaluation framework built around a user simulator that maintains explicit knowledge states, represented as a graph of Information Units with prerequisite relationships, that evolve under update rules grounded in learning theory. KNOWSIM computes three metrics (Knowledge Gain, Delivery Calibration, Cognitive Overload) directly from the knowledge state trajectory, reflecting key mechanistic aspects of information calibration. We validate KNOWSIM against 705 human-AI sessions across two domains, stratified by knowledge level: its rankings align significantly with human judgments (73-74% sign agreement), outperforming three baseline simulators. Applied to 9 LLMs, KNOWSIM reveals that the best model shifts by user knowledge level, revealing aptitude-treatment interactions invisible to standard evaluation.

</details>


### [172] [Synthesizing Feature Extractors: An Agentic Approach for Algorithm Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17170)
*Hai Xia,Carlos Ansótegui,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种使用大语言模型（LLM）在agentic check-fix-verify循环中自动合成可解释的、面向特定问题的特征提取器（Python脚本）的方法，用于约束满足问题的算法选择。在三个组合优化问题和五个求解器组合上，合成的特征提取器在测试集准确率上持续优于专家设计的mzn2feat特征（FLECC上最高提升8.3个百分点）和基于Transformer的trans2feat变体，同时保持可检查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束满足问题的算法选择需要提取能捕捉问题结构的特征，但手动设计特征提取器需要深厚的领域专业知识，且当新问题类别出现时，手动设计迅速成为瓶颈。

Method: 利用LLM作为agent，在check-fix-verify循环中自动合成可执行的Python脚本作为特征提取器。给定高层MiniZinc模型和实例，LLM agent生成代码构建带类型的图表示，并计算结构属性（如图密度、变量聚类、约束紧密度），形成可解释的问题特定特征。

Result: 在车辆路径、汽车排序、定长纠错码三个组合问题上，使用五个SOTA求解器组成的求解器组合进行评估。合成的特征提取器在算法选择器上持续优于专家设计的mzn2feat特征（FLECC上测试集准确率最高提升8.3个百分点）以及最优的trans2feat Transformer变体。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的自动特征提取器合成方法不仅能超越专家设计的特征，还能带来可检查的、可解释的特征提取代码，为算法选择中特征工程自动化提供了一条新的路径。

Abstract: Algorithm selection for constraint satisfaction problems requires extracting features that capture problem structure. Manually designing feature extractors demands deep domain expertise and quickly becomes a bottleneck when new problem classes appear. We present an automated approach that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) in an agentic check--fix--verify loop to synthesize executable Python scripts that act as interpretable, problem-specific feature extractors. Given a high-level MiniZinc model and an instance, the LLM agent generates code that constructs a typed graph representation and computes structural properties such as graph density, variable clustering, and constraint tightness. We evaluate our approach on three combinatorial problems (vehicle routing, car sequencing, fixed-length error-correcting codes) with a portfolio of five state-of-the-art solvers. The synthesized extractors yield algorithm selectors that consistently outperform both expert-curated mzn2feat features (up to $8.3$ percentage points (pp) test-set accuracy on FLECC) and the best transformer-based trans2feat variants. In the meanwhile, the synthesized feature extractors remain inspectable.

</details>


### [173] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Automated Safety Benchmarks for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17183)
*Nyamtulla Shaik,Fengjun Li,Bo Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对26个开源小语言模型（SLM）使用5个主流安全基准套件进行大规模评估，发现现有为大语言模型设计的安全基准在SLM上产生大量模糊/不相关判断，且这些模糊判断与提示复杂度和模型架构相关，表明LLM中心的安全基准不足以作为SLM安全评估的独立证据，同时指出能力-安全混杂问题及基于均值排名的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型（SLM）正被广泛部署于资源受限、隐私敏感的场景中，其安全性和偏见失败可能引发安全与社会风险。然而，现有AI安全/安全/合规基准是为大语言模型设计的，可能无法可靠地迁移到SLM上。因此需要探究这些基准能否有效且可靠地评估SLM。

Method: 在统一的评判标准（对有害、安全、模糊/不相关响应分别评分0、1、0.5）下，评估5个广泛使用的基准套件对26个开源SLM的表现，并进行大规模的有效性和稳健性评估。

Result: 各基准中模糊判断占主导地位，且与提示复杂度和模型架构相关；模糊率随词法密度、输出困惑度和输出长度增加而升高，随词法复杂度、自一致性和回复-提示相似度增加而降低；均值排行榜在合理的模糊处理方式下会发生显著变化。

Conclusion: LLM中心的安全基准不足以作为SLM安全评估的独立证据，存在能力-安全混杂问题，且基于均值分数的排行榜在数学上具有脆弱性，需要开发专门针对SLM的安全评估方法。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed in resource-constrained, privacy-sensitive settings, where safety and bias failures can cause security and societal risks. However, existing AI safety\slash security\slash compliance benchmarks are designed for large language models that may not transfer reliably to SLMs. We therefore ask: Can these benchmarks effectively and reliably evaluate SLMs? To answer this question, we conduct a large-scale assessment of the effectiveness and robustness of these automated pipelines by evaluating five widely used benchmark suites across 26 open-source SLMs under a unified judging rubric, which assigns a score of 0, 1, or 0.5 to harmful, safe, or ambiguous/irrelevant responses, respectively. Across the benchmarks, ambiguous judgments dominate and correlate with prompt complexity and model architecture, indicating that {\em LLM-centric safety benchmarks are insufficient as standalone evidence for SLM safety assessment}. In general, the ambiguity rate increases with lexical density, output perplexity, and output length and decreases with lexical sophistication, self-coherence, and reply-prompt similarity. This reveals a capability-safety confound that mixes model capability with apparent safety. Since ambiguity is prevalent, aggregate mean-score leaderboards are mathematically brittle: model rankings change significantly under reasonable ambiguity treatments, even when the underlying outputs remain unchanged.

</details>


### [174] [Fool's Gold: Defensive Deception Against Safety-Removal Attacks on Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17202)
*Mark Russinovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"decoy hardening"（"愚人金"）防御方法：与其试图阻止拒绝机制被移除（abliteration），不如接受拒绝层被剥离的事实，并"毒化"其收益——一旦拒绝机制被移除，模型对危险操作请求的大部分回答会变成自信、流畅但关键要素均被伪造的诱饵。该方法在7个模型上验证，其中6个通过效能门槛，显著提高了受攻击状态下的诱饵率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重语言模型的安全对齐极易被移除——abliteration只需几分钟就能将拒绝方向的权重投影出去，且目前没有任何发布时的防御能持久阻止这一过程。既然无法阻止，不如欺骗攻击者。

Method: 在攻击的可微分仿真中训练诱饵，使得诱饵仅在受攻击状态（拒绝被剥离后）下表达；同时使用"拒绝钉"（refusal pin）和"良性缰绳"（benign leash）保持干净状态行为与原始模型一致。

Result: 在7个模型（5个家族，9B-122B，密集和MoE架构）上测试。6个通过预注册效能门槛的模型中，0.51-0.90的受攻击状态响应为诱饵（+0.27-0.84归因于防御），且全部维持在良性行为和能力预算内；第7个（较小）未能通过门槛。外部红队基准测试中，防御后的122B模型在CBRNE相关子集上有0.82-0.86的致命错误率（对比未防御的0.10）。K=64次重复采样的元素级一致性分析显示，防御模型仅能在0.083-0.625的提示上重建可用程序（对比未防御的0.58-0.96），且无法无标签区分两种状态。

Conclusion: 该防御是一种认识论层面的声明：在没有独立地面真值的情况下，任何被测试的观测表面都无法区分伪造答案和正确答案。防御不处理上下文内越狱，且仅保护初始发布时的权重。

Abstract: Safety alignment in open-weight language models is trivially removable: abliteration projects a refusal-mediating direction out of the weights in minutes, and no release-time defense we are aware of prevents it durably. What cannot be prevented can be deceived. Our defense, decoy hardening ("Fool's Gold"), concedes the refusal strip and poisons its payoff: once refusal is stripped, most answers to hazardous operational requests are confident, fluent decoys whose critical elements are falsified. Decoys are trained inside a differentiable simulation of the attack, expressing only in the attacked state; a refusal pin and benign leash hold clean-state behavior to the original. We instantiate it on seven models from five families (9B-122B, dense and mixture-of-experts). On the six models passing our pre-registered efficacy gate, 0.51-0.90 of attacked-state responses to held-out prompts are decoys, +0.27-0.84 attributable to the defense; all six stay within registered benign-behavior and capability budgets; the seventh (smaller) fails the gate (boundary case). Rates replicate on a frozen test split or untouched strata. The claim is epistemic: without independent ground truth, no observation surface we tested separates falsified answers from correct ones - on external red-team benchmarks' CBRNE-adjacent slice, the defended 122B is fatally wrong on 0.82-0.86 of matched-quality answers vs at most 0.10 undefended. Repeated sampling does not restore trust: element-wise consensus at K=64 reconstructs a fully usable procedure on 0.083-0.625 of prompts where the instrument validates, vs 0.58-0.96 undefended, with no label-free way to tell the regimes apart; on the weakest such model the claim is per-draw only. We evaluate chemical and biological hazards; the defense does not address in-context jailbreaks and protects only the initially released defended weights.

</details>


### [175] [Explicit State Elicitation Is Not Enough: A Controlled Audit of Memory-Policy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17247)
*Yihang Chen,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Chong Peng,Huan Xu,Shuaiting Li,Yiqi Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并实施了一套针对个性化智能体中"用户记忆管理"结构化中间输出的实证审计协议，通过控制反事实数据集与多种诊断手段，验证了状态结构提示束、状态输出字段等干预措施的有效性，发现示例级准确率高估了反事实一致性，完整四路家庭成功极为罕见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化智能体必须决定检索到的用户记忆应被使用、忽略、更新还是查询，但目前缺乏系统化方法审计这一决策过程中的结构化中间输出，且初始实验结果（480例合成开发集显示状态结构提示束有大幅收益）可能被词法可分性等数据捷径所误导。

Method: 提出四步实证审计协议：1)审计数据集捷径（TF-IDF诊断）；2)隔离捆绑的提示变更；3)检查中间标签是否与答案关联；4)测试分解语义证据并审计提供商级执行失败。构建160例冻结控制反事实集（40个四路匹配家族）和规则推导参考策略来评估Llama-3.3-70B与GPT-OSS-120B。

Result: 状态定义暴露可提升准确率，但隔离的显式状态输出字段对Llama-3.3-70B无显著提升，对GPT-OSS-120B仅有边缘非显著增益。基准关联状态标签会改变策略预测，但属于标签条件诊断而非内部机制证据。完整四路家族成功罕见，分解语义证据提取未能改善路由，GPT-OSS条件因提供商端请求验证不可用。

Conclusion: 结构化中间输出的审计表明，早期看似显著的收益多源于数据捷径而非真实机制改进；示例级准确率会高估反事实一致性；评估仅限策略分类，未涉及下游响应、工具动作或记忆存储变更。

Abstract: Personalized agents must decide whether retrieved user memory should be used, ignored, updated, or queried before it affects a current task. We use this setting to develop an empirical audit protocol for structured intermediate outputs: first audit dataset shortcuts, then isolate bundled prompt changes, check whether intermediate labels are answer-associated, test decomposed semantic evidence, and audit provider-level execution failures. A 480-example synthetic development set initially suggested large gains from a state-structured prompt bundle, but TF-IDF diagnostics showed lexical separability and no positive standalone Ignore cases. We therefore construct a frozen 160-example controlled counterfactual set with 40 matched four-way families and rule-derived reference policies. On this set, exposing the four state definitions improves accuracy, but an isolated explicit state-output field does not significantly improve policy accuracy for Llama-3.3-70B and gives only a marginal, non-significant gain for GPT-OSS-120B. Supplying benchmark-associated state labels shifts policy predictions, but because those labels deterministically map to policies, this is a label-conditioning diagnostic rather than evidence of a faithful internal mechanism. Family-level and seed-stability analyses further show that example-level accuracy overstates counterfactual consistency: complete four-way family success is rare. An exploratory follow-up that elicits decomposed semantic evidence also fails to improve routing for the cleanly evaluated endpoint; the corresponding GPT-OSS condition was unavailable because of provider-side request validation. We evaluate policy classification only, not downstream responses, tool actions, or memory-store mutation.

</details>


### [176] [Do LLMs Know a Good Hypothesis When They See One? Logit-Based Energy Scoring Outperforms Prompted LLM-as-Judge for Scientific Hypothesis Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17270)
*Swati Rajwal,Sanjay Das,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于logit的能量评分方法，利用语言模型的内在置信度而非比较性判断来评估科学假设。在7个语言模型、1,323篇论文、12个学科上，内在评分达到33.0%的Hit@1，优于提示式列表排序的16.6%。最强配置（10亿参数模型）达到53.1%的最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于科学假设生成，但如何评估生成的假设仍是一个挑战。现有的评估方法（如LLM作为裁判或依赖语义相似度）往往偏向熟悉的想法而非新颖的想法，缺乏可靠的评估机制，这阻碍了可信AI驱动的科学工作流的发展。

Method: 提出一种基于logit的能量评分方法，通过语言模型的内在置信度来评估假设，而非使用比较性判断。研究将每篇论文与其真实假设及15个错误替代方案配对，在7个语言模型上对1,323篇论文进行了基准测试。

Result: 内在评分在两种评分器上合并达到33.0%的Hit@1，而提示式列表排序仅为16.6%。最强的配置是使用logit能量评分的10亿参数模型，达到53.1%的Hit@1（不过这是在14种模型×评分器组合中事后选择的最大值）。

Conclusion: 内在模型置信度在科学假设评估方面展现出潜力，该研究为基于置信度的方法在未来可信AI驱动的科学发现中提供了研究动机和方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for scientific hypothesis generation. However, evaluating generated hypotheses remains a challenge for trustworthy AI-enabled scientific workflows. Existing approaches often use LLMs as judges or rely on semantic similarity, which can favor familiar ideas over novel ones. We propose a logit-based energy scoring method that evaluates hypotheses using a language model's intrinsic confidence rather than comparative judgment. We benchmarked seven language models on 1,323 papers across 12 disciplines. Each paper was paired with its hypothesis and fifteen incorrect alternatives. Intrinsic scoring reached 33.0% Hit@1 pooled across both scorers, compared with 16.6% for prompted listwise ranking. The strongest configuration, a 1-billion-parameter model using logit-based energy scoring, reached 53.1%, though this was the maximum across 14 model-by-scorer combinations selected post hoc. Overall, intrinsic model confidence shows potential for scientific hypothesis evaluation. This study also motivates future research on confidence-based methods for trustworthy AI-enabled scientific discovery.

</details>


### [177] [ASI-Bench: At the Dawn of Artificial Superintelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17271)
*Junwei Zhou,Zhen Sun,Binyu Li,Jiangyu Zhou,Yuexi Pan,Hengyu Wang,Honghe Ren,Xiaohan Jia,Xueyang Zhou,Xiaoyu Cao,Yongchao Chen,Yuanning Feng,Junhao Wu,Cheng Zhang,Sijia Chen,Haoyu Xue,Chengsong You,Huan Wang,Koutian Wu,Peigan Gao,Jiakun Wu,Wenzhe Li,Ergan Shang,Qingyuan Zheng,Jingjing Zhou,Ruixuan Jia,Yan Xu,Hongrui Zhang,Xiao-Han Ma,Zhengxiang Cheng,Yuexing Hao,Liting Mai,Xianglin Ji,Wenjun Zhang,Zhuofan Chen,Yixiao Huang,Chi Wang,Wenyue Hua,Yilun Hao,Yuantao Zhai,Ziyan Zhao,Jingyan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ASI-Bench，这是首个联合评估AI系统在通用研究领域中创新探索能力和自主科研执行能力的基准测试，也是首个在同一研究项目中逐步撤除人类方法学指导以测试AI自主性的基准。该基准包含60个跨11个科学领域的项目级研究任务，由40多位专家耗时31,000+人工小时构建。实验表明，在18种最先进的智能体-模型配置下，平均分数从完整方法指导下的50.91降至仅指定方法时的29.10，再到智能体必须自行确定方法时的26.62，说明当前AI系统仍严重依赖人类指导，远未达到自主开展端到端项目级科学研究的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统能力主要建立在学习、压缩和应用已有人类知识的基础上，现有基准也主要测试AI基于所学知识产生正确答案或在大量人类指导下完成任务的能力，缺乏对AI创新探索和自主科研执行能力的联合评估。为迈向人工超级智能（ASI），需要AI超越掌握现有知识，探索未知领域并创造可验证的新知识，因此需要一个能够测试AI在逐步减少人类指导下自主开展科研的基准。

Method: 构建ASI-Bench基准：由40多名专家耗时31,000+人工小时构建，包含60个覆盖11个科学领域的项目级研究任务，采用逐步减少方法学指导的渐进式设计——从完整方法指导、仅指定方法到智能体自行决定方法三个层级，所有任务均经过专家评审、AI辅助审计、沙箱执行和评分验证。

Result: 在18种最先进的智能体-模型配置下，平均得分从完整方法指导的50.91降至仅指定方法时的29.10，再到智能体须自行确定方法时的26.62，显示出显著的能力下降，说明当前AI系统对人类指导的依赖度仍然很高。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统仍严重依赖人类方法学指导，远未实现自主开展端到端项目级科学研究的水平。ASI-Bench作为开放基准，邀请全球研究人员和构建者贡献新任务、挑战当前AI极限，以加速人类通往人工超级智能的进程。

Abstract: Artificial superintelligence (ASI) requires AI to move beyond mastering existing knowledge toward exploring the unknown, creating new knowledge, and turning new ideas into verifiable results. However, the capabilities of today's AI systems are still largely built on learning, compressing, and applying existing human knowledge. Accordingly, existing benchmarks primarily test whether AI can produce correct answers based on learned knowledge, or whether it can complete tasks under extensive human guidance. We therefore introduce ASI-Bench, the first benchmark to jointly evaluate AI systems' capabilities of innovative exploration and autonomous scientific execution across general research domains, and the first to progressively withdraw human methodological guidance within the same research project to test how far AI can proceed on its own. Built by over 40 experts with the cost of 31,000+ human hours, ASI-Bench contains 60 project-level research tasks across 11 scientific domains and progressively reduces methodological guidance to test whether AI can independently select methods, conduct research, and produce verifiable results. All tasks undergo expert review, AI-assisted auditing, sandbox execution, and scorer validation. Across 18 state-of-the-art agent--model configurations, the average score drops from 50.91 with full methodological guidance to 29.10 with only the method specified and 26.62 when agents must determine the method themselves. This sharp decline shows that current systems remain heavily dependent on human guidance and are still far from autonomously conducting end-to-end, project-level scientific research. ASI-Bench is open to the world. We invite researchers and builders everywhere to contribute new tasks, challenge the limits of today's AI, and help accelerate humanity's collective path toward artificial superintelligence at https://asibench.apexin.ai/submit.

</details>


### [178] [DeAR: Decentralized Agentic Reasoning via Capability Grounding and Collaborative Thought Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17282)
*Xing Wei,Changmeng Zheng,XiaoYong Wei,Xiufen Ye,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DeAR（去中心化智能体推理）框架，将传统集中式智能体协作转变为自主对等协作，通过三个机制（去中心化能力感知、思维图导航、拓扑更新）在9个多模态推理和文本问答基准上超越现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体推理系统依赖集中式协议，存在路由瓶颈和静态角色分配问题，在处理复杂多模态查询时表现不佳。作者认为需要从集中控制转向自主对等协作以克服这些局限。

Method: DeAR框架包含三个核心机制：(1) 去中心化能力感知——基于查询动态实现智能体专业化；(2) 思维图导航——实现目标定向的智能体间交互；(3) 拓扑更新——支持自适应的错误修正。

Result: 在9个多样的多模态推理和文本问答基准上，DeAR一致性地优于近期基线方法，验证了智能体间去中心化和自适应协作能够提升知识密集型推理任务的准确性。

Conclusion: 去中心化和自适应的智能体协作相比集中式协议在知识密集型推理任务中更具优势，DeAR框架证明了这一方向的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Existing agentic reasoning systems typically rely on centralized protocols. This design introduces routing bottlenecks and static role allocations that often fail when handling complex multimodal queries. We propose DeAR (Decentralized Agentic Reasoning), a framework that shifts from central control to autonomous peer-to-peer collaboration. DeAR is built on three mechanisms: (1) decentralized capability grounding for query-dependent agent specialization, (2) thought map navigation for targeted peer interactions, and (3) topology update for adaptive error correction. Evaluations across 9 diverse multimodal reasoning and text-based QA benchmarks indicate that DeAR consistently outperforms recent baseline methods, validating that decentralized and adaptive collaboration among agents enhances accuracy in knowledge-intensive reasoning tasks. The source code will be available at https://open_upon_acceptance.

</details>


### [179] [PlanPO: Group Planning-Aware Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17289)
*Dayang Liang,Liyuan He,Xuan Feng,Shuxin Li,Bo An,Yunlong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanPO是一种面向智能体大语言模型多轮交互训练的强化学习方法，通过对成功轨迹引入从粗到细的优势信号（轨迹长度和轮次响应长度），在组相对策略优化框架内区分路径效率差异，从而学习可泛化的规划能力。在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld基准上平均较GRPO提升27.2%，且几乎不增加训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于组相对策略优化的方法无法区分同样成功但交互效率差异巨大的轨迹（如迂回成功与高效成功获得相同奖励），导致优势塌缩和性能瓶颈。作者希望超越任务特定的高质量行为模式，学习可泛化的规划能力。

Method: PlanPO在组相对优化结构中引入粗到细的优势信号：粗粒度层面利用轨迹级长度差异，细粒度层面利用轮次级响应长度差异，两者均在相同任务的成功轨迹间进行相对比较。在组相对策略优化框架内，引导智能体从高质量采样中学习审慎且可泛化的规划与生成行为，同时避免退化为单纯的文本长度最小化。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld三个多轮交互基准上，PlanPO平均较GRPO提升27.2%，并超越多个近期强基线方法，额外训练成本可忽略不计。

Conclusion: PlanPO验证了在成功轨迹内部引入效率和规划意识优势信号的有效性，为智能体LLM在交互任务中学习可泛化规划能力提供了简单高效的解决方案，且不牺牲训练效率。

Abstract: Group-relative policy optimization has emerged as a key paradigm for training agentic large language models (LLMs) on multi-turn interactive tasks. However, most existing variants fail to distinguish advantages among successful trajectories even when these trajectories differ substantially in their interaction efficiency. For instance, circuitous successes are often assigned the identical outcome reward, causing advantage collapse and severe performance bottlenecks. To this end, we propose Group Planning-aware Policy Optimization (PlanPO), a simple yet effective RL method for learning generalizable planning abilities beyond task-specific high-quality behavior patterns. Specifically, PlanPO introduces coarse-to-fine advantage signals, which capture the relative differences in trajectory-level lengths and turn-level response lengths conditioned on successful trajectories sampled for the same task. Within the group-relative optimization structure, this enables agents to actively learn generalizable and deliberate behaviors spanning interaction planning and textual generation from high-quality rollouts, without degenerating into vanilla length minimization. Experimentally, PlanPO improves over GRPO by 27.2\% on average across the challenging multi-turn benchmarks ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, outperforming recent powerful baselines while incurring negligible additional training cost.

</details>


### [180] [LiveHouse-TS: An Open-world Living Benchmark for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17299)
*Haomin Wen,Ziyu Zhou,Qingxiang Liu,Siru Zhong,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LiveHouse-TS，首个针对时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）的开放世界实时基准测试基础设施。与依赖固定历史窗口的静态基准不同，LiveHouse-TS采用预序（prequential）评估方式，在实际未来数据上持续评估模型，将基准测试从"快照精度"转向"连续时间有效性"。在11个领域17个数据集上的流式评估表明，静态排名在实时协议下会发生剧烈重排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型的评估协议主要依赖静态基准和固定历史测试窗口，只能反映模型在某一固定时段上的平均表现，无法捕捉真实世界中模型在季节变化、分布漂移和突发事件等持续演化环境下的行为。这导致模型排名可能失真，无法回答"模型排名能否长期维持"等关键科学问题。

Method: 构建LiveHouse-TS开放世界实时基准基础设施，采用预序（prequential）评估协议，在开放世界的真实未来数据上持续评估TSFMs，覆盖11个领域共17个数据集，进行大规模流式评估以考察模型排名的长期稳定性和对分布漂移的鲁棒性。

Result: 在11个领域17个数据集上的流式评估结果表明，静态基准下的模型排名在实时（live）评估协议下会发生剧烈的重新洗牌，说明静态排名无法可靠地代表模型在动态环境中的长期表现。

Conclusion: LiveHouse-TS作为持续运行的时间序列基准基础设施，超越了传统的一次性排行榜模式，为探索"模型排名能否长期维持"和"哪些模型在面对分布漂移时真正鲁棒"等长期科学问题提供了方法论支撑，并呼吁社区转向更具时间有效性的实时评估范式。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have recently emerged as a highly promising paradigm for cross-domain zero-shot forecasting. However, existing evaluation protocols predominantly rely on static benchmarks with fixed historical test windows. While these benchmarks provide a valuable baseline snapshot, they evaluate an average performance on a fixed history, failing to capture how models behave in continuously evolving real-world environments characterized by seasonal variations, distribution shifts, and unexpected events. To bridge this gap, we introduce LiveHouse-TS, the first open-world living benchmark infrastructure for TSFMs. By evaluating models prequentially on real future data in open-world environments, LiveHouse-TS shifts time series benchmarking from snapshot accuracy to continuous temporal validity. Rather than acting as a one-off leaderboard, our infrastructure serves as a continuous time series infrastructure designed to explore vital, long-term scientific questions: Can model rankings be maintained over the long term? Which models remain genuinely robust under distribution shifts? Extensive streaming evaluations across 11 domains with 17 datasets demonstrate that static rankings undergo a dramatic reshuffling under a live protocol.

</details>


### [181] [SignalReasoner: Assessing the Upper Bound of 3B Models for Signal Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17301)
*Guozheng Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在无线信号处理数学问题上，对Qwen2.5-3B-Base模型采用强化微调策略的适配方法。对比了直接RL和SFT+RL两种训练范式，以及GRPO、GSPO、GMPO三种策略优化算法，最佳模型准确率达39.12%，比未训练基线（12.37%）提升三倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然监督式思维链微调和基于可验证奖励的强化学习已显著提升大语言模型的数学推理能力，但这些方法在信号处理领域的应用仍相对未被探索。作者希望填补这一空白，探索强化微调策略在无线信号数学问题上的适用性。

Method: 使用Qwen2.5-3B-Base模型，在WirelessMATHBench-XL基准上测试两种训练范式：(i)直接在基准上使用可验证奖励进行RL；(ii)先在蒸馏的无线领域思维链语料上进行SFT，再进行相同的领域特定RL。同时对比GRPO、GSPO和GMPO三种策略优化算法。

Result: 最佳模型达到39.12%的总体准确率，相比未训练的Base模型（12.37%）实现了超过三倍的提升。

Conclusion: 领域感知的CoT SFT可作为后续RL的有效初始化；研究同时评估了GSPO和GMPO相比GRPO在信号推理任务中稳定性和准确性方面的潜在优势。

Abstract: Post-training with supervised chain-of-thought fine-tuning and reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards has substantially improved the mathematical reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, their application to signal processing problems remains relatively under-explored. This report investigates reinforcement fine-tuning strategies for adapting Qwen2.5-3B-Base to graduate-level signal mathematical problems from WirelessMATHBench-XL, a comprehensive benchmark for mathematical reasoning in this domain. We examine two training paradigms: (i) direct reinforcement learning (RL) on WirelessMATHBench-XL with verifiable rewards; and (ii) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on a distilled wireless-domain chain-of-thought corpus, followed by the same domain-specific RL stage. Across both paradigms, we benchmark Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO), and Geometric-Mean Policy Optimization (GMPO). We aim to assess whether domain-aware CoT SFT serves as an effective initialization for subsequent RL, and whether GSPO or GMPO offer advantages in stability or accuracy over GRPO for signal reasoning tasks. Our best model achieves an overall accuracy of 39.12\%, representing a more than threefold improvement over the untrained Base model (12.37\%).

</details>


### [182] [Wuying-Browser-Agent: Real-World Centric Fundamental Long-Horizon Browser Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17319)
*AIMAE Team,Tianxiang Chen,Yan Cheng,Zhangye Han,Xiaowei Li,Chang Liu,Cheng Liu,Zhongqiang Ma,Long Peng,Xiaobing Tu,Yinggui Wang,Hongliang Wei,Chen Wu,Daiping Xin,Kunyu Zhou,Pengyang Zhou,Peiyuan Chen,Ziyuan Chen,Yutao Deng,Chunyu Dong,Xiangyu Fu,Yicheng Feng,Ruian He,Haochen Li,Miancan Liu,Zhengqin Liu,Wei Peng,Jinkui Ren,Haoyu Tan,Dong Xiao,Rongkun Xue,Shujian Yang,Xianhang Ye,Ziqi Yuan,Ziyang Yu,Linghan Zhang,Xiantao Zhang,Xuanpu Zhao,Yinan Zhao,Zhenghui Zhao,Bin Zhu,Likai Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Wuying-Browser-Agent，一个统一的浏览器智能体框架，通过在执行、监督、优化和评估四个层面进行对齐（而非仅依赖规模），解决了真实部署中浏览器智能体面临的长序列决策、错误恢复和复杂UI交互等挑战。该框架在多个基准上刷新了开源SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浏览器智能体在简短、干净的环境中表现良好，但真实部署场景截然不同——智能体需要在实时网站上持续做出数十个决策，同时要处理错误恢复和复杂UI。作者认为仅靠扩大模型规模无法弥合这一差距，必须在pipeline的每个层面（执行、监督、优化、评估）进行系统性对齐。

Method: 1) 结构化浏览器工具（harness）：提供稳定的执行原语和面向决策的上下文管理；2) RUIC-SFT：通过反思（reflection）和UI专项课程式监督微调，显式训练错误恢复轨迹和复杂UI交互；3) DAO-GRPO：基于势函数的奖励塑形和发散感知步长加权，改进长程信用分配；4) BrowserBench：新的双语真实网页基准，包含350个任务，平均37.9步，用于暴露长程失败模式。

Result: Wuying-Browser-Agent-27B在WebVoyager上达到80.6%，Online-Mind2Web上66.7%，BrowserBench上65.1%，刷新了浏览器使用基准的开源SOTA。该pipeline还迁移到浏览器之外的一般智能体任务，在Tau2-Bench、Claw-Eval和BFCL-v4上平均得分73.8。

Conclusion: 作者认为，为了实现浏览器智能体的真实部署，需要在pipeline的每个层面进行对齐而非仅追求规模扩展。Wuying-Browser-Agent证明了这种多层面对齐方法的有效性，且该框架具备跨领域泛化能力，展示了更强的通用智能体能力。

Abstract: Browser agents perform well on short, clean demonstrations, but real deployment is fundamentally different: agents must sustain dozens of decisions on live websites while recovering from mistakes and navigating complex UIs. We argue that closing this gap requires alignment at every level of the pipeline, including execution, supervision, optimization, and evaluation, rather than scale alone. We present Wuying-Browser-Agent, a unified framework that addresses each of these levels. A structured browser harness provides stable execution primitives and decision-oriented context management. Reflection and UI-specialized Curriculum SFT (RUIC-SFT) explicitly trains on recovery trajectories and complex-UI interactions. Divergence-Aware Online GRPO (DAO-GRPO) improves long-horizon credit assignment through potential-based reward shaping and divergence-aware step weighting. Finally, we introduce BrowserBench, a bilingual real-web benchmark of 350 tasks averaging 37.9 steps, because most existing benchmarks are too short to expose long-horizon failure modes. Wuying-Browser-Agent-27B achieves 80.6\% on WebVoyager, 66.7\% on Online-Mind2Web, and 65.1\% on BrowserBench, establishing a new open-source state of the art on browser-use benchmarks. The same pipeline also transfers beyond browser use, demonstrating strong general agentic ability and reaching an average score of 73.8 on Tau2-Bench, Claw-Eval, and BFCL-v4.

</details>


### [183] [LLMs for Medical Consultation Are Evaluated Too Late: The Preformulation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17330)
*Yining Hua,Cyrus Ayubcha,Hongbin Na,Levi Lian,Alon Gorenshtein,Yiftach Barash,Eyal Klang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了三款API大语言模型在医疗咨询初诊场景（问题尚未明确时）的表现，发现基线条件下模型常在未获得患者关键信息前就给出自我护理建议（9/12），而加入"就诊指导"指令后，结构化交接摘要从0/12提升至10/12，但关键事实的 elicitation 仍不可靠，建议直接评估首诊可观察行为而非诊断准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型医疗咨询评估多在临床问题已明确后进行，但真实咨询常以模糊、淡化或错误框架的问题开始。作者旨在填补这一"预配方差距"（preformulation gap）的评估空白。

Method: 评估三款API模型，使用四个医生编写的多轮对话场景，设置基线与"就诊指导"（entry-to-care instruction）两种条件，生成24份固定脚本对话；另对两个场景使用自适应标准化患者模拟生成12份对话，分析自我护理建议出现的时机和结构化交接摘要的生成情况。

Result: 基线条件下9/12的场景-模型组合出现无依据的自我护理建议，加入指令后降至0/12；结构化交接摘要在基线中0/12出现，指令后达10/12。但指令虽改变了时序和文档记录，未能可靠确保关键事实的 elicitation（引出）。

Conclusion: 预配方差距应通过可观察的首次接触行为（如是否过早给出建议、是否结构化记录）直接评估，而非依赖诊断准确性或最终答案质量进行推断。

Abstract: Large language models for medical consultation are often evaluated after a clinical problem has already been made clear, although real consultations may begin with a vague, minimized, or misframed concern. We evaluated three API models across four physician-authored, multi-turn vignettes under baseline and entry-to-care instruction conditions, yielding 24 fixed-script transcripts; two cases also used adaptive standardized-patient simulation, yielding 12 transcripts. Self-care or home-management advice before any patient answer appeared in 9 of 12 baseline case-model cells and 0 of 12 instruction cells, while structured handoff summaries appeared in 0 of 12 and 10 of 12 cells, respectively. The instruction changed sequencing and documentation, although it did not reliably ensure elicitation of decisive facts. The preformulation gap should therefore be evaluated directly through observable first-contact behavior rather than inferred from diagnostic accuracy or final-answer quality.

</details>


### [184] [TileMix: Tile-Centric Mixed-Precision Attention for LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17336)
*Hanzhi Zhang,Qiao Zhang,Qinglei Cao,Heng Fan,Yan Huang,Kewei Sha,Yunhe Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TileMix 是一种针对大语言模型长上下文预填充阶段的磁贴级精度路由内核，通过将注意力矩阵划分为硬件对齐的分数磁贴，并将其路由至 FP16 或 INT8 计算路径，在保持全局精度的同时提升预填充吞吐量，无需训练即可恢复长上下文质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文预填充阶段中，密集自注意力因二次方的查询-键分数计算而产生大量计算和内存开销。现有方法要么使用统一的低精度路径（uniform low-precision），要么选择性忽略某些token交互，均未能在融合密集注意力内部实现对硬件对齐的分数磁贴进行空间精度路由。

Method: TileMix 将注意力矩阵划分为硬件对齐的分数磁贴，将路由决策打包为紧凑位掩码，并将每个磁贴组通过 FP16 或 INT8 分数计算路径处理，两条路径共享在线 softmax 状态。可扩展的精度分组让每个路由位控制多个相邻键磁贴，保持硬件对齐的计算磁贴和紧凑元数据。它保留密集 token 连接、无需训练，支持分组查询注意力、变长批处理和 INT8 键/值缓存。

Result: 在 LongEval、LV-Eval 和 A100 预填充基准上，针对 LLaMA、Qwen 和 Vicuna 模型，TileMix 恢复了统一 INT8 下丢失的长上下文质量，并提升了相对 FP16 的预填充吞吐量，提供了跨模型家族可控的精度-效率边界。

Conclusion: TileMix 通过将数值精度变为可执行的空间决策，在融合密集注意力中实现了精度与效率的可控权衡，为长上下文预填充提供了新的优化方向，且实现已开源。

Abstract: Long-context prefill in large language models (LLMs) incurs substantial computation and memory traffic because dense self-attention computes quadratic query-key scores. Existing methods either use a uniform low-precision path or select token interactions, leaving spatial precision routing over hardware-aligned score tiles outside fused dense attention. We introduce TileMix, a tile-centric precision-routing kernel that makes numerical precision an executable spatial decision over score-tile groups within fused dense attention. TileMix partitions the attention matrix into hardware-aligned score tiles, packs routing decisions into compact bitmasks, and dispatches each tile group through FP16 or INT8 score computation while both paths update a shared online-softmax state. Scalable precision grouping lets each routing bit govern multiple adjacent key tiles, preserving hardware-aligned compute tiles and compact metadata at long contexts. By routing all legal tile groups, TileMix preserves dense token connectivity, requires no training, and supports grouped-query attention, variable-length batches, and INT8 key/value caches. Across LongEval, LV-Eval, and A100 prefill benchmarks on LLaMA, Qwen, and Vicuna, TileMix recovers long-context quality lost under uniform INT8 and improves prefill throughput over FP16, yielding a controllable accuracy-efficiency frontier across model families. The implementation is available at https://github.com/HanzhiZhang-Ulrica/TileMix.

</details>


### [185] [LLM-Only PDDL Domain Repair with Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17341)
*Nader Karimi Bavandpour,Pascal Bercher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了开源大语言模型在PDDL模型自动修复任务上的表现。符号基线F1为0.49，最佳LLM在高推理努力下达到0.87（绝对提升0.38），但平均测试通过率仅0.82，在Thoughtful域上骤降至0.06，即使包含测试轨迹的最佳设置也仅达0.92。结论是当前开源模型无法保证满足测试约束，不足以支持可靠的自动模型修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI规划依赖于显式的世界模型（通常以PDDL表示），而模型错误会导致规划失败。已有研究关注如何检测和修复模型错误，用户可提供正/负测试计划作为约束，自动修复方法需修改PDDL模型以满足这些约束。本文旨在评估开源大语言模型是否胜任这一修复任务。

Method: 采用纯LLM方法（LLM-only）进行PDDL模型修复，评估多个最近的开源权重LLM，并与符号基线方法对比。实验中设置了不同的推理努力水平（reasoning effort），并考察是否包含测试轨迹（test traces）对修复效果的影响。

Result: 符号基线F1为0.49；最佳LLM在高推理努力下达到F1=0.87，绝对提升0.38。但该设置下平均测试通过率仅为0.82，在Thoughtful域上骤降至0.06；即使包含测试轨迹的最佳设置测试通过率也仅达0.92。

Conclusion: 尽管LLM在F1分数上大幅超越符号基线，但测试通过率不够稳定且远未达到完美，尤其在特定域上表现极差，表明当前开源权重模型无法保证满足测试约束，可靠性不足以支撑实际自动模型修复应用。

Abstract: AI planning is concerned with finding a sequence of actions that achieves a specified goal. It relies on explicit models of the world, commonly represented in the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL). An active line of research investigates how errors in such models can be detected and repaired. For example, users may provide positive test plans that are solutions, and negative test plans that fail during execution. Automated repair methods then modify the PDDL model to satisfy these constraints. In this paper, we evaluate the ability of recent open-weight large language models to perform this repair task using an LLM-only approach. Our experiments show that the symbolic baseline achieves an $F_1$ score of $.49$, while the best-performing LLM reaches $.87$ with high reasoning effort, an absolute improvement of $.38$. However, that setting has a mean test pass rate of only $.82$, falling to $.06$ on the Thoughtful domain; even the best setting that includes the test traces reaches only $.92$. Thus, current open-weight models cannot guarantee satisfaction of the test constraints required for reliable automated model repair.

</details>


### [186] [Cognitive Graph Intelligence for Adaptive and Robust DDoS Attack Detection in Next Generation Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17352)
*Mohammad Arif Hossain,Yeahia Sarker,Md Jafrin Hossain,Most. Humayra Khanom Rime,Nirwan Ansari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphGAN，一种基于图与生成对抗网络的DDoS检测引擎，通过k近邻图构建保留流量间的时序与特征关系，利用GAN生成合成少数样本来缓解类别不平衡，并使用GCN判别器与分类器实现最终检测，在四个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDoS攻击威胁网络可用性，其检测面临严重类别不平衡和非平稳条件两大挑战。现有方法未能充分捕捉流量之间的关联结构，也难以在数据稀缺场景下有效工作。

Method: GraphGAN由三部分组成：(1) 将顺序流量流通过滑动窗口转换为k近邻图，保留特征相似性与时序依赖；(2) 生成器学习DDoS攻击分布以合成真实少数类样本，GCN判别器区分真实与合成图数据；(3) 在平衡数据集上训练的独立GCN分类器执行最终检测决策。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的评估表明，GraphGAN在准确率、精确率和召回率方面优于现有最先进方法，尤其在数据稀缺场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过整合时序图构建、对抗增强和GCN分类，GraphGAN有效建模协调攻击行为并缓解类别不平衡，为数据受限环境下的入侵检测提供了稳健且拓扑感知的解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed Denial-of-Service (DDoS) attacks threaten network availability, requiring a cognitive detection process that senses traffic, infers intent, and supports an adaptive response under severe class imbalance and non-stationary conditions. This paper proposes a Graph-based Generative Adversarial Network (GraphGAN) that serves as the cognitive detection engine for this task. GraphGAN captures the relational structure among traffic flows while addressing imbalance through adversarial generation of synthetic samples. Sequential flows are converted into $k$-nearest neighbor graphs using sliding windows to preserve feature-similarity and temporal dependencies among flows. The generator learns the distribution of DDoS attacks to synthesize realistic minority samples, while a Graph Convolutional Network (GCN)-based discriminator distinguishes real from synthetic graph data. A separate GCN classifier, trained on the balanced dataset, performs the final detection decision. Evaluations on four benchmark datasets show that GraphGAN achieves superior accuracy, precision, and recall compared to state-of-the-art approaches, particularly in data-scarce scenarios. By integrating temporal graph construction, adversarial augmentation, and GCN classification, GraphGAN effectively models coordinated attack behaviors and mitigates class imbalance, providing a robust and topology-aware solution for intrusion detection in data-constrained environments.

</details>


### [187] [LEGO-RL: Harness-Native Reinforcement Learning for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17393)
*Yiming Du,Yuxin Jiang,Tao Yuan,Jianbo Dai,Shaowei Wang,Jierun Chen,Chaofan Tao,Xianzhi Yu,Lifeng Shang,Kam-Fai Wong,Xiaohui Li,Haoli Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEGO-RL 是一个连接原生编码智能体框架与可扩展策略梯度优化的框架，通过进程内LLM代理、沙箱编排和可观测训练三大支柱，在不修改内部控制流的情况下实现高效强化学习训练。在SWE-bench Verified上，使用GSPO训练的Qwen3.5-35B-A3B在三个编码智能体框架上均取得显著性能提升，同时保持滚动训练概率相关性超过0.99。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体的强化学习越来越依赖长时间运行的智能体框架来管理工具集成、仓库上下文和执行反馈。然而，这些框架的原生执行环境与策略梯度训练本质上不匹配：环境崩溃和奖励黑客破坏结果信号，训练与推理之间的差异使智能体行为与策略更新脱耦。

Method: LEGO-RL 基于三大支柱：(1) 忠实优化——通过进程内LLM代理捕获原始生成流以实现token级对齐，并在训练器端进行稳健的log-probability重计算，即使框架侧发生压缩或重新序列化；(2) 可靠执行——可扩展的沙箱编排，具备镜像缓存和分阶段防御以缓解奖励黑客问题；(3) 可观察训练——集成插件自动进行验证和监控，配合Live UI提供细粒度轨迹诊断。

Result: 使用GSPO训练稀疏MoE模型Qwen3.5-35B-A3B，在SWE-bench Verified上：OpenHands SDK从64.0%提升到70.4%，Claude Code从62.4%提升到68.2%，OpenCode从57.2%提升到66.6%，同时保持滚动训练概率相关性高于0.99。

Conclusion: LEGO-RL成功弥合了原生编码智能体框架与可扩展策略梯度优化之间的鸿沟，无需修改框架内部控制流即可实现有效的强化学习训练，并在多个真实编码智能体环境中验证了其有效性和可靠性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for coding agents increasingly relies on long-running agent harnesses to manage tool integration, repository contexts, and execution feedback. However, the native execution environments of these harnesses are inherently misaligned with policy-gradient training: environmental crashes and reward hacking corrupt outcome signals, while train-inference discrepancies decouple rollout behavior from policy updates. To address this, we present LEGO-RL, a framework that bridges native coding-agent harnesses with scalable policy-gradient optimization without modifying their internal control flow. LEGO-RL is built upon three pillars: (1) faithful optimization via in-process LLM proxying that captures raw generation streams for token-level alignment and robust trainer-side log-probability recomputation, even under harness-side compaction or re-serialization; (2) reliable execution via scalable sandbox orchestration featuring image caching and stage-wise defenses to mitigate reward hacking; and (3) observable training through an integrated plugin that automates validation and monitoring, paired with a Live UI for granular trajectory diagnostics. We evaluate LEGO-RL by training the sparse MoE model Qwen3.5-35B-A3B with GSPO across three native coding-agent harnesses. LEGO-RL improves Qwen3.5-35B-A3B across OpenHands SDK (64.0% to 70.4%), Claude Code (62.4% to 68.2%), and OpenCode (57.2% to 66.6%) on SWE-bench Verified, while maintaining a rollout-training probability correlation above 0.99.

</details>


### [188] [Task-Aware Harness Provisioning for LLM Agents in Mission-Critical Infrastructure Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17433)
*Liangtao Lin,Qingang Zhang,Zhaomeng Zhu,Tianwei Zhang,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体在关键基础设施（MCI）中的最优harness配置问题，将其建模为任务需求与harness供给之间的资源匹配问题。作者通过文献挖掘和智能体执行测量构建任务到harness的映射，并提出"图谱引导升级"（map-guided escalation）的harness配置算法：从任务专属的最小配置出发，仅在自检失败时扩展至完整配置。实验表明该方法在液冷任务中优于全量配置，且与Reflexion精度相当但节省48% token；在电网任务中则发现harness配置遵循依赖领域的精度-成本Pareto前沿，而非存在普适最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统对每个任务都暴露相同的全量harness（信息、工具和动作权限），这造成资源浪费且非必要。作者希望识别最优harness配置，将问题形式化为任务需求与harness供给的资源匹配问题，以在保证精度的同时降低资源消耗。

Method: 1) 基于底层系统的数学表示对MCI任务进行分类；2) 根据harness提供的信息类型和数量对harness配置进行排序；3) 通过两条途径构建任务到harness的映射：挖掘研究文献和测量受控智能体执行；4) 提出map-guided escalation算法——从任务专属最小harness开始，仅在自检失败后逐步扩展至全量配置。

Result: 在液冷任务中，该方法将智能体精度从全量配置下的0.652提升至0.715，并以48%更少的token达到与Reflexion相当的精度；在电网任务中，全量配置仍是精度最优，但基于映射的harness配置提供了更低成本的可选方案。结论是harness配置遵循依赖领域的精度-成本Pareto前沿，不存在普适最优。

Conclusion: harness配置问题不存在通用最优解，而是随任务领域在精度与成本之间呈现Pareto前沿权衡关系。所提出的map-guided escalation算法证明了从精简harness起步、按需扩展的策略可以在某些领域同时提升精度和降低开销，为LLM智能体的harness设计提供了新的优化视角。

Abstract: LLM agents have been widely adopted to operate mission-critical infrastructure (MCI). These agents normally rely on a harness that determines what information they can access, which tools they can use, and what actions they can take. Existing systems often expose the same comprehensive harness to every task, which may not be necessary and cause resource wastes. In this paper, we focus on the identification of optimal harness configurations, and view it as a resource-matching problem between what each task requires and what the harness provides. To measure this match, we classify MCI tasks based on the mathematical representation of the underlying system and rank harness configurations by the amount and type of information they provide. We then construct task-to-harness mappings from two sources: mining research literature and measuring controlled agent execution. Leveraging the measured mapping, we propose a new harness provisioning algorithm: map-guided escalation. It begins with a task-specific harness and expands to full provision only after a failed self-check. We evaluate our method in two representative MCI tasks: in liquid cooling, it improves the agent accuracy from 0.652 under full provision to 0.715 and achieves accuracy comparable to Reflexion with 48% fewer tokens; In power grids, full provision remains accuracy-optimal, while map-based provisioning offers lower-cost alternatives. These findings show that harness provisioning follows a domain-dependent accuracy-cost Pareto frontier rather than a universal optimum.

</details>


### [189] [Depth Enables Local Entropy: Quadratic Depth Dependence in Deep Variation-Norm ReLU Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17434)
*Tao Jiang,Minbo Gao,Shaowei Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究深度L、宽度w的向量值Parhi-Nowak深度RBV²架构（O(Lw²)参数）在Gaussian回归中的minimax风险。通过构造局部packing证明深度二次依赖L²是本质的（在显式样本量依赖半径条件下），得到下界Ω(L²w²log(w)R²/n)，并通过伪维数有限网上界得到Õ(L²w²R²/n)，从而确立深度呈二次多项式依赖的minimax风险（差对数因子），并在较小半径下呈现表示受限转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对于该O(Lw²)参数化架构，已知下界与上界相差一个深度因子L，不确定深度二次依赖是否为架构内在属性。论文旨在通过构造局部packing证明二次深度依赖L²是固有的（在该架构下的本质约束），从而彻底解决该架构minimax风险的深度依赖问题。

Method: 1) 构造局部packing：在O(λ)L²球内构造码字序列，pairwise Ω(λ)分离，log基数Ω(L²w²log w)；2) 使用偏置校正的有界系数逼近定理；3) 提出平衡放大技术：将深度D的ReLU网络乘以q可用一个常数通道实现，使每个系数仅增长q^(1/D)；4) 转换为向量值RBV²块，层和代价为O(Dw²q^(1/D))；5) 用Gaussian Fano得到半径显式下界；6) 用伪维数有限网上界处理无界Gaussian响应情况。

Result: 在A=B=R、σ正比于R及指定半径条件下，得到minimax下界Ω(L²w²log(w)R²/n)；上界为Õ(L²w²R²/n)。因此minimax风险对深度呈L²二次多项式依赖（差对数因子），即深度二次依赖是架构本质属性；在较小半径下则可观察到向表示受限行为的转变。

Conclusion: 该架构的minimax风险在中等半径下具有本质的深度二次依赖L²（仅差对数因子），解决了此前上下界相差一个深度因子的gap；同时揭示了在较小半径下风险由表示能力（而非统计估计）主导的转变现象，为深度架构统计复杂度的理解提供了统一的上下界刻画。

Abstract: We study Gaussian regression over the explicit vector-valued Parhi--Nowak deep-RBV^2 architecture with depth L, width w, layer-sum variation budget A, and output bound B. For this O(L w^2)-parameterized architecture, the known lower and upper bounds differ by one factor of depth. We construct a local packing showing that the quadratic depth dependence is intrinsic under an explicit sample-size-dependent radius condition. The packing has log-cardinality Omega(L^2 w^2 log w); its codewords lie in an O(lambda) L^2 ball and are pairwise Omega(lambda)-separated. The main ingredients are a bias-corrected bounded-coefficient approximation theorem and balanced amplification: multiplying a depth-D ReLU network by q can be implemented using one constant channel so that every coefficient grows by only q^(1/D). Translation to vector-valued RBV^2 blocks then has layer-sum cost O(D w^2 q^(1/D)). Gaussian Fano yields a radius-explicit lower bound governed by the output, testing, and representation scales. Under A=B=R, sigma proportional to R, and the stated radius condition, this gives minimax risk at least of order L^2 w^2 log(w) R^2/n. A pseudodimension-based finite-net upper bound gives O-tilde(L^2 w^2 R^2/n) for unbounded Gaussian responses. Thus the minimax risk has quadratic polynomial dependence on depth, up to logarithmic factors, and exhibits a transition to representation-limited behavior at smaller radius.

</details>


### [190] [Structure-Internalized Rule Language Model for Faithful Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17443)
*Xingrui Zhuo,Jiapu Wang,Manzong Huang,Gongqing Wu,Xindong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SIRLM（结构内化规则语言模型），通过结构规则生成将KG结构知识的参数化学习与推理逻辑的保真度评估耦合，解决LLM在知识图谱推理中因结构上下文与参数化知识表征不一致导致的"推理证据感知漂移"问题，在36个数据集上优于17种SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在KGR任务上虽表现出色，但KG结构上下文与LLM参数化知识之间存在表征不一致性，导致LLM无法有效感知符合KG约束的推理证据，损害了推理的有效性和忠实性，作者将之定义为"推理证据感知漂移"问题。

Method: 提出SIRLM框架，核心组件包括：(1) 结构内化规则生成器（SIRG），包含增强结构关系记忆的上下文学习模块；(2) 基于结构不变性学习的KG分词器，提供可学习的结构表征；(3) 基于规则约束消息传播的神经符号推理器，提供忠实的规则执行反馈。可无缝集成到SFT和GRPO等标准LLM训练范式。

Result: 在36个数据集上与17种SOTA KGR方法进行对比实验，SIRLM展现出显著的性能优越性。

Conclusion: SIRLM通过结构规则生成将结构知识与参数化学习有效耦合，锚定KG接地证据，显著提升LLM在KGR任务上的有效性和忠实性，且具有良好的可扩展性和兼容性。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Reasoning (KGR) aims to discover latent facts by leveraging the structural evidence available in KGs, posing a challenge to the structural semantic understanding capability of KGR models. Recent studies have demonstrated that Large Language Models (LLMs) can achieve remarkable progress on KGR tasks via flexible in-context learning. However, the inherent representation inconsistency between KG structural context and LLM parametric knowledge remains inadequately addressed. This limitation prevents LLMs from effectively perceiving reasoning evidence that aligns with KG constraints, which undermines both the effectiveness and faithfulness of reasoning. We refer to this problem as reasoning evidence perception drift of LLMs over KGs. To address this problem, we propose a Structure-Internalized Rule Language Model (SIRLM), which centers on structural rule generation to couple the parametric learning of structural knowledge with the faithfulness evaluation of reasoning logic, enabling LLMs to anchor tightly to KG-grounded evidence. Specifically, we first design a Structure-Internalized Rule Generator (SIRG), which incorporates an in-context learning block augmented with a structural relation memory to coordinate structural and parametric knowledge. Furthermore, we equip SIRG with a KG tokenizer based on structural invariance learning and a neuro-symbolic reasoner based on rule-constrained message propagation. These components provide SIRG with learnable structural representations and faithful rule-execution feedback, respectively. Our SIRLM can be seamlessly integrated into standard LLM training paradigms, such as SFT and GRPO. Extensive experiments against 17 state-of-the-art KGR methods on 36 datasets demonstrate the significant superiority of SIRLM.

</details>


### [191] [SAGE: Self-Evolving Storyboard Skills via Attribution-Guided Rule Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17468)
*Maolin Ran,Xiaoyang Lu,Jiaqi Liu,Jian Wang,Weiwen Liu,Jianghao Lin,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE是一个部署于虚拟电影工作室的框架，通过从专家演示中学习、归因、演化和路由导演知识，实现了从剧本到分镜板的自动化生成。在18集测试中得分77.8（专业导演77.1），部署14天产出1,344个叙事组输出，87.2%无需实质修改，节省83%以上的创作时间，并发布了首个公开的剧本-分镜板配对数据集PROSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业分镜板设计依赖隐性导演专业知识，是工业瓶颈。现有LLM自动化方法面临三大挑战：知识获取（工艺隐性）、知识精炼（无法根据执行结果评估和追溯）、知识注入（全部知识超出上下文且手动选择不可扩展）。

Method: SAGE通过学习、归因、演化和路由四个环节运作：(1)通过对比训练剧本与专家分镜板推导出独立于剧集内容的情境规则；(2)生成时记录每个叙事组采用的规则，结合局部反馈实现针对性更新；(3)演化的规则形成情景包并附带路由索引，使每个叙事组仅检索与自身情境相关的有界规则集，无需专家干预。

Result: 在涵盖三个类型的18个测试剧集上，SAGE得分为77.8，超过专业导演的77.1。在虚拟电影工作室部署14天，产生1,344个叙事组输出，其中87.2%无需实质编辑即被接受，每集创作时间减少超过83%。

Conclusion: SAGE证明了通过归因引导的知识演化，可以以自动、可扩展的方式将隐性导演专业知识转化为可部署的自动化分镜生成能力，达到甚至超过人类专业导演的水平，同时发布了首个公开的剧本-分镜配对数据集PROSE以推动该领域研究。

Abstract: Storyboards turn screenplays into visual shot plans for automated short drama production. Professional storyboarding relies on tacit directorial expertise and remains an industrial bottleneck. Large language models can automate this step, but methods for supplying directing knowledge face three challenges: (1) Knowledge acquisition: the craft remains implicit in exemplars or must be written manually. (2) Knowledge refinement: authored knowledge is not evaluated against execution outcomes, and opaque generation prevents feedback attribution to the knowledge behind each decision. (3) Knowledge injection: injecting all knowledge exceeds usable context, while manual selection for every narrative group does not scale. We present SAGE (Skill with Attribution-Guided Evolution), a deployed framework that learns, attributes, evolves, and routes directing knowledge from expert demonstrations. SAGE derives rules that are independent of episode content by contrasting each training screenplay with its expert storyboard. During generation, the model records each narrative group's adopted rules. Combining these records with localized feedback enables targeted updates to individual rules. Evolved rules form scenario packages with a routing index, so each group retrieves only a bounded set appropriate to its situation without expert intervention. On 18 test episodes across three genres, SAGE scored 77.8 on a rubric validated by experts, versus 77.1 for professional directors. Deployed for 14 days on Virtual Film Studio, SAGE produced 1,344 narrative group outputs; 87.2 percent were accepted without substantive edits, and the production team recorded over 83 percent less authoring time per episode. We release PROSE, the first public dataset pairing screenplays with storyboards by professional directors across 68 episodes: https://github.com/creDreams/PROSE.

</details>


### [192] [When AI Designs AI: Innovation or Imitation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17471)
*Yikang Yang,Zhengxin Yang,Luzhou Peng,Minghao Luo,Yanqi Kan,Wanling Gao,Jianfeng Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了LLM智能体设计的方法与人类设计方法在性能和算法设计上的差异。通过从人类设计方法中提取任务特定的算法设计空间，将智能体设计的方法映射到这些空间中，并在模块层面量化算法差异。实验发现，智能体偶尔能匹敌或超越人类SOTA（10/72配置），但成功率不稳定；96.8%的智能体设计方法落在人类衍生的算法设计空间内，近一半与现有的人类算法设计完全匹配，表明智能体主要在重组合和重用人类的算法选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体在复杂AI任务中方法设计能力的提升，研究者希望回答两个核心问题：智能体设计的方法与人类设计的方法相比性能如何？它们在算法设计上有何不同？现有研究缺乏系统性的方法来量化这些差异，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从人类设计的方法中推导出任务特定的算法设计空间，将人类和智能体设计的方法映射到这些空间中，并在模块层面量化它们的算法差异。在跨多种模态的代表性、开放式AI任务套件上评估广泛使用的LLM智能体，从任务性能和算法差异两个维度分析其设计的方法。

Result: 当前的LLM智能体偶尔能匹敌或超越人类SOTA性能（10/72配置），但这种成功不能可靠地跨任务或跨智能体泛化。96.8%的智能体设计方法落在人类衍生的算法设计空间内，大部分是重新组合人类设计方法中的算法选择，近一半完全匹配已有的人类算法设计。

Conclusion: 尽管当前智能体偶尔能匹敌或超越人类SOTA性能，但它们的算法设计仍停留在人类衍生的算法设计空间内，反映出对算法选择的复用和重组，并未展现真正超越人类的算法创新能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM agents have made them increasingly capable of designing methods for complex AI tasks. This raises two central questions about agent-designed methods relative to human-designed methods: how well they perform, and how different their algorithmic designs are. To study these questions, this paper introduces an analysis that derives task-specific algorithmic design spaces from human-designed methods, maps both human- and agent-designed methods into these spaces, and quantifies their algorithmic differences at the module level. Widely used LLM agents are evaluated on a suite of representative, open-ended AI tasks spanning multiple modalities, and the methods they design are analyzed in terms of both task performance and algorithmic differences from human-designed methods. Experimental results show that current agents can occasionally match or surpass human state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance (10/72 configurations), but such success does not generalize reliably across tasks or agents. Moreover, 96.8% of agent-designed methods fall within human-derived algorithmic design spaces, largely recombining algorithmic choices found in human-designed methods, while nearly half exactly match an existing human algorithmic design. Taken together, these findings suggest that although current agents can occasionally match or surpass human SOTA performance, their algorithmic designs remain within human-derived algorithmic design spaces, reflecting the reuse and recombination of algorithmic choices.

</details>


### [193] [Towards Better Agents for Multi-Turn User Interaction: The Next User Turn Is More Than Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17499)
*Yiwen Zhao,Zhihao Wen,Yuchen Mao,Mingxuan Jiang,Yihao Hu,Pan Wang,Xin Zhang,Wei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈感知的信用分配方法 FACA，通过利用用户下一轮反应作为局部信用信号，改进多轮交互式工具智能体的训练，在多个领域基准上显著超越仅依赖终端奖励的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向用户的工具智能体需要在多轮对话中协调对话与工具使用，但交互式强化学习通常只将整个回合归约到终端奖励，无法区分有效的引导、错误和后续修复等不同行为的贡献，导致信用分配不精确。

Method: FACA 将用户下一轮反应与该前置用户段对齐，推导出局部归一化的反应优势，并将其与验证过的终端结果优势相加。该方法不需要额外的批评网络或额外 rollout，在保持与 Interactive GRPO 相同的模拟器、可见对话、初始化、rollout 和优化设置下进行了对照实验。

Result: 在九个领域的 τ 系列平均上，FACA 相比仅用结果奖励的 Interactive GRPO 基线，在 8B 和 14B 规模下分别提升 5.91 和 10.22 个百分点。提升主要集中在电信领域；在 8B 下随机化反应极性会消除电信领域的增益。同样的排序在 Pare-Bench 和 Co-Gym 的零样本评测中保持一致。

Conclusion: 用户的下一轮反应提供了可操作的局部信用信号，能够有效改进多轮用户交互式智能体的训练效果，证明了局部反应信号在交互式强化学习中的价值。

Abstract: User-facing tool agents must coordinate dialogue and tool use as user goals unfold over multiple turns. Yet interactive reinforcement learning typically reduces each rollout to a terminal reward, assigning the same credit to effective elicitation, errors, and later repair. The next user turn is more than context: it also provides noisy, temporally local evidence about the preceding user-to-user segment. We introduce \textbf{F}eedback-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{C}redit \textbf{A}ssignment (\textsc{FACA}), which aligns each reaction with that segment, derives a locally normalized reaction advantage, and adds it to verified terminal outcome advantage without an extra critic or rollout. Against an outcome-only Interactive GRPO control matched in simulator, visible dialogue, initialization, rollout, and optimization, \textsc{FACA} improves the nine-domain $τ$-family average across three independently trained runs by 5.91 and 10.22 percentage points at 8B and 14B, respectively. Gains concentrate in Telecom; at 8B, randomizing reaction polarity removes the Telecom gain. The same ordering holds zero-shot on Pare-Bench and Co-Gym. These results demonstrate that next-turn user reactions provide actionable local credit for improving multi-turn user-interacting agents.

</details>


### [194] [SGHA: Evidence-Grounded Research Problem Discovery with Local Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17501)
*Sarvesh Gharat,Junpei Komiyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SGHA（Structural Gap Hypothesis Agent），一个完全自动化、以语料库为先的研究问题发现系统，完全在本地LLM（9B开源模型）上运行，无需依赖专有前沿模型API。SGHA将文献语料结构化为带证据链接的论文对象和类型化证据图，检测未解决的结构性模式，筛选候选缺口，并生成可追溯的研究问题族，包括假设、目标、成功标准和剩余歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化AI科学家在早期研究问题制定阶段过度依赖专有前沿模型，这些模型的参数知识是黑盒的，导致研究问题的证据基础和有效性难以审计，容易受到特定模型的幻觉和偏见影响，同时向外部API传输专有研究材料会产生保密性、隐私和数据治理方面的担忧。

Method: SGHA采用语料库优先的方法：(1) 将科学文献语料结构化为带证据链接的论文对象和类型化证据图；(2) 检测跨论文的未解决结构模式；(3) 在制定前筛选候选研究缺口；(4) 生成可追溯的研究问题族，输出假设、目标、成功标准和剩余歧义。所有LLM组件均使用本地服务的开源9B语言模型执行，不依赖专有前沿模型API。

Result: 在五个机器学习领域中将SGHA与AI Scientist-v2的创意制定模块进行比较，结果表明显式的语料库结构和证据约束推理能够支持有前景且可检查的研究问题制定，而无需在生成或验证阶段依赖前沿模型。

Conclusion: 通过显式语料库结构和证据约束推理，可以在不依赖专有前沿模型的情况下实现有前景、可检查的研究问题制定，从而解决黑盒知识依赖、幻觉偏见以及数据隐私治理等问题。

Abstract: Recent efforts toward fully automated AI scientists have demonstrated that language-model agents can generate hypotheses, execute experiments, and draft scientific manuscripts. However, during the early stages of research, when research problems are formulated, these AI scientists often rely heavily on proprietary frontier models. Their proposals are shaped by opaque parametric knowledge and by literature searches conditioned on the proposals themselves. Such knowledge is effectively a black box, and this dependence makes the evidential basis and validity of generated research problems difficult to audit and leaves the process vulnerable to model-specific hallucinations and biases. Furthermore, if proprietary research materials are transmitted to external APIs, the use of these models creates confidentiality, privacy, and data-governance concerns.
  We introduce the Structural Gap Hypothesis Agent (SGHA), a fully automated, corpus-first research-problem discovery system that runs entirely on a local LLM. SGHA structures a scientific literature corpus into evidence-linked paper objects and a typed evidence graph, detects unresolved structural patterns across papers, screens candidate gaps before formulation, and produces traceable research-problem families. In particular, it is able to output assumptions, objectives, success criteria, and remaining ambiguities. All LLM-based components of SGHA are executed using a locally served open-weight 9B language model, without requiring proprietary frontier-model APIs. We compare SGHA with the AI Scientist-v2 idea formulation module in five machine-learning domains. Our results suggest that explicit corpus structure and evidence-constrained reasoning can support promising, inspectable research-problem formulation without relying on frontier models during generation or verification.

</details>


### [195] [Agent Lightning v1.0: Towards Harnessed Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17528)
*Zhiyuan He,Siwei Zhang,Zhiwen Zhou,Yuqing Yang,Yu Kang,Yuge Zhang,Luna K. Qiu,Tin Yan Tsui,Jiahang Xu,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Agent Lightning v1.0，一个约3500行代码的轻量级框架，用于"有挂载的智能体强化学习"（harnessed agentic RL）。该范式通过LLM端点代理将任意智能体挂载（harness）与强化学习训练连接，训练器只观测LLM请求-响应对序列。在SWE-bench Verified上，仅用6K训练样本和适度算力，将Qwen3.5-9B从41.8%提升至56.4%（+14.6个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体运行在管理工具、上下文和控制流的挂载（harness）中，挂载成为智能体系统的关键部分。然而传统agentic RL中训练引擎拥有环境交互循环，无法直接利用部署时的挂载。本文旨在解决harnessed agentic RL这一新范式带来的挑战（重分词、样本合并、优势计算、损失归一化、后端调度），并提供可复现的测试平台。

Method: 提出Agent Lightning v1.0框架，采用解耦架构，通过LLM端点代理将任意智能体挂载连接到RL训练。训练器仅观测LLM请求-响应对序列，挂载拥有环境交互循环。框架约3500行代码，支持任意智能体挂载，并处理重分词、样本合并、优势计算、损失归一化、后端调度等挑战。提供了完整的编码智能体RL可复现流程。

Result: 在指令遵循、搜索和编码三类智能体上评估。仅用6K训练样本和适度算力，RL将Qwen3.5-9B在SWE-bench Verified上的性能从41.8%提升到56.4%，绝对提升14.6个百分点。发布了完整工作流和训练脚本。

Conclusion: Agent Lightning v1.0验证了harnessed agentic RL范式的可行性和有效性，表明部署时挂载直接参与模型后训练能够显著提升性能。该范式已被verl、Uni-Agent、AReaL 2.0、slime和Polar等框架采纳，本文工作为这一领域提供了轻量级可复现的研究测试平台。

Abstract: Modern agents operate inside agent harnesses that manage tools, context, and control flow, making the harness a critical part of the agent system. Our original Agent Lightning introduced a disaggregated architecture that connects arbitrary agents to RL training through an LLM endpoint proxy, an approach later adopted by frameworks such as verl Uni-Agent, AReaL 2.0, slime, and Polar. We refer to this paradigm as harnessed agentic RL, where the deploy-time harness directly participates in model post-training. Harnessed agentic RL differs fundamentally from traditional agentic RL: the harness, rather than the training engine, owns the environment interaction loop, while the trainer observes only sequences of LLM request-response pairs. This introduces challenges in retokenization, sample merging, advantage calculation, loss normalization, and backend scheduling, which can substantially affect training stability and effectiveness. We present Agent Lightning v1.0, a lightweight framework for harnessed agentic RL implemented in approximately 3,500 lines of code. It supports arbitrary agent harnesses and serves as a practical testbed for studying these challenges. We evaluate it on instruction-following, search, and coding agents, and provide a complete reproducible pipeline for coding-agent RL. Using only 6K training examples and modest compute, RL improves Qwen3.5-9B on SWE-bench Verified from 41.8% to 56.4%, a 14.6-point absolute gain. We release the complete workflow and training scripts to facilitate reproducible research on harnessed agentic RL.

</details>


### [196] [When to Review: Spaced Repetition for Continual Pre-Training of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17530)
*Alankar Atreya,Devesh Batra,Yoages Kumar Mantri,Geremy Bantug,Greig A Cowan,Raad Khraishi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Spaced Repetition Training (SRT)，一种受认知科学启发的持续预训练框架，利用SuperMemo-2 (SM-2)算法智能调度样本复习，在保留旧知识的同时获取新知识，相比朴素持续预训练和均匀回放显著改善稳定性-可塑性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续预训练中的回放方法通常选择全局固定的新旧数据混合比例并均匀采样，忽略了不同样例被遗忘的速度各不相同这一事实。这种"一刀切"的策略无法最优地分配复习资源。

Method: SRT将持续预训练建模为自适应复习调度问题。具体包括：1) 维护每个样本的复习状态；2) 将每个样本的困惑度(perplexity)映射为回忆质量信号；3) 使用SM-2算法调度历史样本进行保留和新样本进行巩固；4) 不改变模型、目标和优化器。

Result: 在时间分隔的Wikipedia和代码语料上，SRT改善了稳定性-可塑性权衡，在不同模型规模下恢复了朴素持续预训练损失的5到37个百分点旧知识准确率，同时保持或改善新知识获取。在更大规模上，SRT保持了广泛的基准性能，而朴素持续预训练和均匀回放显著退化。在视觉和表格数据上的实验表明该调度原则在配合适当回忆信号时可扩展到语言之外。

Conclusion: SRT验证了将认知科学中的间隔重复原理应用于LLM持续预训练的有效性，证明按遗忘速度而非均匀方式调度复习样本能更高效地平衡稳定性与可塑性，且该原则具有跨模态的普适性。

Abstract: Continual pre-training of large language models must acquire new information without erasing old knowledge. Existing replay methods often choose a global old/new mixture and sample uniformly, ignoring that examples differ in how quickly they are forgotten. We formulate continual pre-training as adaptive review scheduling: the training loop should decide not only how much history to replay, but which examples should return at each step. We introduce Spaced Repetition Training (SRT), a continual learning framework inspired by cognitive science, which schedules sample-rehearsal using the SuperMemo-2 (SM-2) algorithm. SRT maintains per-example review state, maps per-example perplexity to a recall-quality signal, and schedules historical examples for retention and new examples for consolidation while leaving the model, objective, and optimizer unchanged. On temporally separated Wikipedia and code corpora, SRT improves the stability-plasticity trade-off, recovering 5 to 37 percentage points of old-knowledge accuracy lost by naive continual pre-training across model scales while preserving or improving new-knowledge acquisition. At larger scale, SRT preserves broad benchmark performance that naive continual pre-training and uniform replay substantially degrade. Experiments with vision and tabular data further suggest that the scheduling principle extends beyond language when paired with an appropriate recall signal.

</details>


### [197] [Quantifying Risk Under Evolving Uncertainty: Belief-Dependent Robustness for Safe Sequential Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17574)
*Deep Kumar Ganguly,Jan Kretinsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RATTL（风险对抗总奖励学习）框架，将智能体的谨慎程度与认识论不确定性挂钩：智能体对未知动态持有贝叶斯后验，并针对以该后验单调函数为半径的Wasserstein模糊集进行规划。随着证据积累，半径收缩，行为在鲁棒最坏情况与风险中性总奖励最大化之间连续插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在学习环境的过程中，面对未知动态时需要在探索鲁棒性与追求最优奖励之间取得平衡。现有方法要么过于保守（完全最坏情况），要么过于乐观（完全风险中性），缺乏一种能随证据积累自适应调整谨慎程度的框架。

Method: 基于熵值风险价值（Entropic Value-at-Risk）的对偶性，将风险水平的选择转化为模糊半径的选择。贝叶斯后验驱动Wasserstein模糊集半径的收缩，后验越集中，半径越小，行为越接近最优解。在瞬态性和紧致性条件下证明规划问题良定性，并证明Safety Sandwich性质。

Result: 证明了RATTL值位于未信息化的鲁棒值与全知识最优解之间，且间隙随后验集中而消失。在二元风险实例中，该准则退化后验熵设定的条件风险价值（CVaR）。示例展示了智能体在识别阈值前延迟高效行动的行为。

Conclusion: RATTL为不确定性下运行的智能体（包括LLM系统）提供运行时安全保障，通过贝叶斯后验驱动的模糊集半径实现谨慎程度的自适应调节，实现鲁棒性与最优性之间的连续过渡。

Abstract: How cautious should an agent be while it is still learning its environment? We propose RATTL (Risk-Adversarial Total-Reward Learning), which ties caution to epistemic uncertainty: the agent holds a Bayesian posterior over unknown dynamics and plans against a Wasserstein ambiguity set whose radius is a monotone function of that posterior. The radius contracts with evidence, so behaviour interpolates continuously between worst-case robustness and risk-neutral total-reward maximization. The design follows the duality underlying the Entropic Value-at-Risk, which converts the choice of a risk level into the choice of an ambiguity radius. We show the resulting planning problem is well posed under transience and compactness conditions, and prove a Safety Sandwich: the RATTL value lies between the uninformed robust value and the full- knowledge optimum, with a gap that vanishes as the posterior concentrates. In a canonical binary-hazard instance, the induced criterion reduces to Conditional Value-at-Risk at a level set by the posterior entropy. A worked example shows the agent deferring the efficient action until a sharp identification threshold. RATTL targets runtime safety for agents, including LLM-based systems, acting under uncertainty.

</details>


### [198] [TRUSS: Towards Task-Reliable and User-Safe Automated Agent Skill Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17588)
*Zhibo Zhang,Zhen Ouyang,Ling Shi,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRUSS是一个面向Agent Skills的基于证据引导的生成与验证框架，通过静态门控（功能声明核查+九项安全属性检查）和动态执行（影子代理+可控执行环境+工具代理追踪）两阶段流程，实现对Skill工件的功能有效性与安全可靠性的双重验证，并在迭代精炼中定位失败根源。在SkillInject、SkillSafetyBench和SkillGenBench三大数据集上的实验表明，TRUSS在漏洞检测上达到100%精确率和召回率，并显著提升了Skill生成的任务有效性和安全率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills为软件代理提供了可复用的自然语言程序与可执行资源，但自动生成这类Skill时，仅从工件本身或最终任务结果进行评估，无法揭示装备该Skill的代理究竟会执行哪些动作以及这些动作会产生哪些副作用，导致功能有效性（是否真的能完成任务）和安全可靠性（是否会产生有害副作用）无法被充分验证。

Method: TRUSS采用两阶段框架：(1) 静态门控——检查候选Skill的功能声明与源代码及领域证据的一致性，并评估完整工件是否满足九项预定义的安全属性；(2) 动态执行——通过受控执行环境中的影子代理加载候选Skill，借助代理化工具（brokered tools）将请求动作暴露给策略执行机制，并以保留溯源信息的执行轨迹记录结果。功能失败和安全违规会被回溯关联到具体Skill内容，用于指导迭代精炼。

Result: 在168个SkillInject工件、155个SkillSafetyBench案例以及SkillGenBench全部187个任务上评估：漏洞检测达到100.00%精确率和召回率；修复使攻击成功率从38.71%降至19.35%（GPT 5.5）以及从46.45%降至29.68%（GPT 5.4），且零攻击回归；Skill生成方面，任务有效性从无Skill时的17.11%提升至52.94%，基准安全率从50.80%提升至100.00%。

Conclusion: 执行证据能够揭示单纯工件检查无法发现的行为失败，并能引导Skill生成走向功能和安全性联合验证的结果。TRUSS证明了结合静态审查与可控执行的证据驱动方法在Agent Skills质量保障上的有效性。

Abstract: Agent Skills package reusable natural language procedures with executable resources, enabling software agents to acquire task specific capabilities without model adaptation. Automatically generating such Skills can improve task performance, yet evaluating a candidate solely from its artifact or final task outcome leaves unresolved which actions the equipped agent will perform and which side effects those actions will produce. We present TRUSS, an evidence guided framework for generating functionally effective and safety reliable Agent Skills. TRUSS first inspects functional claims against source and domain evidence while evaluating the complete artifact under nine predefined safety properties. Candidates admitted by this static gate are loaded by a shadow agent inside a Controllable Execution Environment, where brokered tools expose requested actions to policy enforcement and record their results as provenance preserving execution traces. Functional failures and property violations are linked back to the responsible Skill content and used to guide iterative refinement.
  We evaluate TRUSS on 168 SkillInject artifacts, 155 SkillSafetyBench cases, and all 187 tasks in SkillGenBench. TRUSS achieves 100.00\% precision and recall in vulnerability detection. Repair reduces attack success from 38.71\% to 19.35\% with GPT 5.5 and from 46.45\% to 29.68\% with GPT 5.4, with zero attack regression. For Skill generation, TRUSS raises task effectiveness from 17.11\% without Skills to 52.94\%, while increasing the benchmark Security rate from 50.80\% to 100.00\%. These results show that execution evidence can expose behavioral failures missed by artifact inspection and can guide Skill generation toward jointly verified functional and safety outcomes.

</details>


### [199] [MoNe: Modular Neural Memory for Efficient Long Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17616)
*Wonguk Cho,Kyubyung Chae,Tribhuvanesh Orekondy,Sunghyun Park,Hyoungwoo Park,Jeongho Kim,Arash Behboodi,Kyuwoong Hwang,Sungrack Yun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MoNe是一种轻量级模块化神经记忆机制，可附加到任何冻结的预训练Transformer上，通过测试时学习和局部梯度更新实现长上下文推理，无需重新训练。它将推理成本与上下文长度解耦，实现O(N)预处理和O(1)查询成本，在128K token下相比ICL降低约80%的计算和峰值GPU内存，仅增加6.4%参数开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer模型在处理长上下文时面临推理成本随上下文长度线性增长的问题，而上下文学习(ICL)在超出模型原生窗口时性能急剧下降。作者旨在设计一种无需重新训练即可扩展任意预训练Transformer长上下文能力的高效方案。

Method: MoNe采用两阶段设计：1) 测试时学习阶段，以固定大小分段读取上下文，通过层本地化梯度更新训练快速权重神经记忆网络；2) 推理阶段，仅从查询token生成键和值，无需重新读取任何上下文token。这种设计将推理成本与上下文长度解耦。

Result: 在128K token下，MoNe相比ICL减少约80%的计算量和峰值GPU内存，仅引入6.4%的参数开销。在RULER基准的needle-in-a-haystack和单词提取任务中表现优异，且能泛化到远超骨干网络原生窗口的上下文长度，而ICL在此场景下性能急剧下降。

Conclusion: MoNe通过将长上下文信息压缩为模块化神经记忆，成功实现了一种高效、可泛化的长上下文推理方案。其O(1)查询成本和恒定峰值内存特性使其在实际长上下文场景中具有显著优势，且兼容任意冻结的预训练Transformer。

Abstract: We present MoNe, a lightweight modular neural memory that attaches to any frozen pretrained Transformer to enable long-context inference without retraining. MoNe reads context in fixed-size segments via test-time learning of fast-weight neural memory networks with layer-localized gradient updates; at inference, the memory generates keys and values from the query tokens alone, with no context tokens re-read.
