Whilst AI is increasingly being adopted across industries to automate tasks, including invoice categorisation, implementations built on in-house small language models (SLMs) can simultaneously reduce cost and improve data security, confidentiality, and interpretability. We investigate this approach by first analysing the pre-trained embedding geometry of a small sentence transformer (SBERT) and classic SLM (DeBERTa). The sentence-embedding space of this financial corpus is globally anisotropic but composed of locally isotropic clusters, extending prior token-level findings to sentence embeddings in a financial setting, and these clusters are strongly correlated with the vendor identity. SBERT fine-tuned on a single GPU reaches 0.96 accuracy on invoice classification, above both a zero-shot LLM and a vendor identity baseline, increasing performance for smaller, challenging categories and new clients. For this important generalisation problem, SBERT reaches 0.9 F1 with roughly 100 client-specific invoices, showing that an in-house SLM implementation is promising. Combining these results with geometric analysis shows that pre-trained embedding geometry is associated with classification performance and reveals a counterintuitive finding that a structured input that would help a human reader does not improve the SLM performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [A Framework for Using and Evaluating LLMs as Surrogate Experts in Security Surveys: Reliability, Bias, and Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16893)
*Despoina Giarimpampa,Roland Meier,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé,Vincent Lenders,Jacques Klein*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个方法论框架，用于评估大语言模型（LLMs）作为安全运营中心（SOC）专家调查受访者的替代或补充方案的可靠性。通过比较基于角色（persona-based）和聚合式LLM生成的回答与真实SOC专业人员的回答，研究发现LLM虽然内部一致性高，但存在方差减少、集中趋势偏差和观点同质化等问题，因此适合用于试点测试和假设生成，但不能替代真实专家调查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全研究中招募SOC领域专家进行调查研究十分困难，面临高工作负荷、职业倦怠和保密性约束，导致样本量小。LLM提供了一种大规模生成合成回答的替代方案，但缺乏关于其可靠性的方法学指导，研究者不清楚何时可以信任这类"替代受访者"。

Method: 提出一个评估LLM作为专家调查受访者的方法论框架，使用来自SOC专业人员的真实回答作为基准，在多个模型和提示设置下对比基于角色（persona-based）和聚合式（aggregate）LLM生成回答，从稳定性、模型间一致性和与人类回答的对齐程度三个维度进行测量。

Result: LLM生成的回答内部一致性好，但系统性地偏离真实专家回答，表现为方差减小、集中趋势偏差和意见同质化。

Conclusion: LLM可用于试点测试和假设生成，但不能替代真实专家调查；研究为安全研究社区提供了关于LLM增强型调查的适当使用场景和局限性的方法论证据与实践指导。

Abstract: Expert surveys are widely used in security research to study practitioner workows and decision-making, yet recruiting domain experts - especially in Security Operations Centres (SOCs), where analysts face high workload, burnout and confidentiality constraints - is difficult and often results in small samples. Large language models (LLMs) oer an appealing alternative by generating synthetic responses at scale, but little guidance exists on when such surrogate participants are reliable. We present a methodological framework for evaluating LLMs as substitutes or supplements to expert survey respondents. Using responses from SOC professionals, we compare persona-based and aggregate LLM-generated answers across multiple models and prompting settings. We measure stability, inter-model agreement and alignment with human responses. Our results show that although LLMs produce internally consistent answers, they systematically diverge from experts, exhibiting reduced variance, central tendency bias and homogenised opinions. This work contributes methodological evidence and practical guidance to the security research community on the appropriate use and limitations of LLM-generated survey responses. We conclude that LLMs are useful for piloting and hypothesis generation but not for replacing expert elicitation, and we discuss implications for researchers using LLM-augmented surveys.

</details>


### [235] [Governing Delegation to Generative Artificial Intelligence: Human Direction, Work-Related Orientation, and Modes of Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17624)
*Jorge Fábrega*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文基于Anthropic经济指数（AEI）2026年4-5月的月度聚合数据，区分了两种人类将认知任务委托给生成式AI的路径：指定委托（执行前通过指令、约束或标准限定任务）和迭代共同生产（执行过程中通过干预纠正或重定向临时输出）。研究发现工作导向的人机互动与更强的指定委托相关联，且迭代响应在不同使用模式（1P API vs Claude.ai）间存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将认知操作委托给生成式AI会重新分配执行权，但产生了一个治理问题：人类对任务的指导仍然存在。作者希望厘清人类方向在AI执行前后何时留下可观测痕迹，并检验工作导向的使用是否与特定的委托模式相关。

Method: 使用Anthropic经济指数（AEI）2026年4-5月的月度聚合单元格数据，区分两种使用模式（1P API直接流量和Claude.ai）。以level-0 O*NET任务为主要分析对象，估计当符合条件的记录将10个百分点从个人使用重新分配到工作相关使用后，指定委托和迭代共同生产两种画像如何变化。迭代比较限于两种模式下均观察且符合条件的1,411个节点-月对。

Result: 指定委托在1P API中增加2.76点（95% CI: [2.30, 3.22]），在Claude.ai中增加1.45点（95% CI: [0.93, 1.97]）。在共同支持上，迭代共同生产在1P API中变化为-0.30点，在Claude.ai中为0.15点，模式间差异为0.45点（95% CI: [0.15, 0.75]）。

Conclusion: 工作导向与更强的人类事前指导痕迹相关，且可观测的迭代响应在不同使用模式间存在显著差异。文章将关注点从"AI执行了多少"转向"人类方向在何时留下可观测的痕迹"。

Abstract: Delegating cognitive operations to generative artificial intelligence redistributes execution and raises a governance problem: where human direction of the task remains. We distinguish two routes. Specified delegation places that direction before execution, through instructions, constraints, or criteria that delimit the task. Iterative coproduction places it during production, through interventions that correct or redirect provisional outputs. To examine both routes, we use aggregate monthly cells from the Anthropic Economic Index for April and May 2026. The AEI distinguishes two modes of use: 1P API, which corresponds to direct traffic through Anthropic's API, and Claude.ai, which combines activity from Chat and Cowork. On this basis, we test whether a stronger work-related orientation of human-AI interaction is associated with more specified delegation within each mode and whether the increase in the iterative profile is greater in Claude.ai than in 1P API. The main analysis uses level-0 O*NET tasks and estimates how both profiles change when an eligible record reallocates ten percentage points from personal use to work-related use. The iterative comparison is restricted to 1,411 node-month pairs observed and eligible in both modes. Specified delegation increases by 2.76 points in 1P API (95% CI: [2.30, 3.22]) and by 1.45 in Claude.ai (95% CI: [0.93, 1.97]). On the common support, iterative coproduction changes by-0.30 points in 1P API and by 0.15 in Claude.ai, yielding a between-mode difference of 0.45 points (95% CI: [0.15, 0.75]). These findings show that work-related orien tation is associated with stronger traces of prior human direction and that the observable iterative response varies across modes of use. The article shifts attention from how much the AI executes to when human direction leaves observable traces.

</details>


### [236] [An Investigation of the NeurIPS and ICML 2025 Position Tracks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16894)
*Fan Yang,Wenkai Li,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文审计了NeurIPS 2025和ICML 2025 Position Paper Track的所有可访问投稿，发现约四分之三的投稿属于改革性批判（critique），而非方向设定性（direction-setting）工作。通过与公认的议程颠覆性论文（如AlexNet、Transformer等）对比，指出方向设定性工作通常提供新的构建物（artifact），如基准、协议、数据集等，而当前投稿池缺乏此类工作。作者提出四项CFP层面的干预建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Position Paper Track的设立旨在推动议程设定性工作，但其早期构成值得审计——需要了解该轨道是否真正吸引了方向设定性的研究，还是被批判性工作所主导。这种审计有助于确保该轨道实现其创建初衷。

Method: 作者对NeurIPS 2025和ICML 2025 Position Tracks的所有可访问投稿，在预设的评分标准（rubric）下进行审计，并将结果与公认的议程转移性ML论文（如AlexNet、Transformer、Concrete Problems in AI Safety等）组成的参考类进行比较。分析维度包括投稿的批判类型、与构建物（artifact）的耦合程度、以及证据深度与评审评分的关系。

Result: 四分之三的审计投稿批判现有基准、评估或方法论，这些论文在artifact-coupling标准上得分高，但证据深度与评审评分无预测关系。与参考类相比，议程转移性论文在artifact种类上不同——它们通常为领域提供新的可构建、可测试、可争论的东西（测量协议、基准提案、玩具实现、数据集卡、审计模板或可证伪的实验方案）。

Conclusion: Position Paper Track当前的投稿池被改革性批判主导，偏离了其议程设定性初衷。作者建议在不取代现有批判性工作的前提下，通过四项CFP层面的干预措施明确引导方向设定性工作的投稿。

Abstract: ML venues shape what kinds of research claims become legible to reviewers and what forms of evidence count as rigorous. The NeurIPS and ICML Position Paper Tracks were created for agenda-setting work, making their early composition worth auditing. \textbf{This paper argues that the publicly accessible 2025 reviewed pool is dominated by reformist critique, and that the track should explicitly solicit direction-setting work alongside, not in place of, the reformist critiques it already hosts well.} We audit every accessible submission to the NeurIPS 2025 and ICML 2025 Position Tracks under a pre-specified rubric, and compare the resulting pattern with a reference class of widely recognized agenda-shifting ML papers. Three-quarters of audited submissions critique an existing benchmark, evaluation, or methodology; these papers score highly on our artifact-coupling rubric, but evidentiary depth does not predict reviewer rating. The reference class (AlexNet, the Transformer, Concrete Problems in AI Safety, and others) differs from the accessible reviewed pool in \emph{artifact kind}: agenda-shifting papers typically gave the field something new to build on, test against, or contest, such as a measurement protocol, benchmark proposal, toy implementation, dataset card, audit template, or falsifiable experimental program. We close with four CFP-level interventions aimed at broadening the submission mix without displacing the critiques the track already hosts well.

</details>


### [237] [Orphan risks at the frontier of artificial intelligence: What diverging safety and compliance frameworks reveal about how AI companies choose the risks they prioritize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16895)
*Andrew D. Maynard*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文档记录了Anthropic、OpenAI、Google DeepMind和Meta在2023至2026年间发布的安全与合规文件，揭示前沿AI公司在自我制定的风险框架与监管机构选择的风险之间存在的"安全差异"，并提出四个决定哪些风险能在自主框架中存活的过滤器（可衡量性、严重性、可审计性和竞争成本），以及"孤儿风险"概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI公司虽然努力绘制其技术风险，但风险格局日益复杂，且这些公司往往同时持有多种不同的风险叙事。作者希望揭示这些公司如何选择管理哪些风险，以及这种选择背后隐藏的制度性偏见。

Method: 对比分析Anthropic、OpenAI、Google DeepMind和Meta在2023至2026年间发布的安全与合规文档，利用这些带时间戳和存档的记录作为机构风险选择的公共记录，识别出四个风险过滤器，并提出"安全差异"框架。

Result: 发现急性、可量化的风险在各类文档中普遍出现，而难以处理的风险（如有害操纵）只在法律强制披露时才被明确表述，在大多数自主选择的风险框架中却缺失。识别出可衡量性、严重性、可审计性和竞争成本四个过滤器决定了哪些风险得以保留。

Conclusion: 通过将风险重新定义为对价值的威胁，可以解释风险如何成为"孤儿风险"，指示未来可能出现的盲区，并为去孤儿化提供轻量级工具，以应对前沿AI安全机制目前尚未组织的风险。

Abstract: Companies developing some of the world's most powerful artificial intelligence systems are surprisingly diligent in how they map out the risks their technologies present. Yet the risk landscape that lies between emerging frontier models and their economically successful and societally beneficial deployment is becoming increasingly hard to navigate. Complicating this further, many frontier AI companies maintain more than one account of what could go wrong with their technologies. This paper documents the divergence between these accounts by comparing safety and compliance documents published by Anthropic, OpenAI, Google DeepMind and Meta between 2023 and 2026, and considers what the resulting record reveals about how these companies select the risks they manage. As these documents are timestamped and archived, they provide a valuable public record of institutional risk selection in progress. From this record the paper identifies four filters that determine which risks tend to survive in self-authored frameworks (measurability, severity, auditability and competitive cost) and introduces the "safety differential" as the gap between the risk landscape a company selects for itself, and the one regulators select for it. While acute, quantifiable risks appear across documents, less tractable risks such as harmful manipulation are articulated fluently where law compels disclosure, yet remain absent from most self-chosen frameworks. This is an exclusion that follows from how these institutions define risk. Drawing on scholarship on institutional risk selection and the framework of risk innovation, the paper shows how redefining risk as a threat to value can help explain how risks become "orphan risks," how it indicates where future blindsides may occur, and how it points to lightweight tools for de-orphaning risks that frontier AI's safety apparatuses are not currently organized to address.

</details>


### [238] [What If AI Carried Her Imagination? Black Girls as Creators in an AI Storytelling Weekend Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16896)
*Chun Li,Lauren Brown,Hubert Asare,Shawna Patterson,Dennis Henderson,Ericka Roland,tara Nkrumah,Angela E. B. Stewart*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个为10-12岁黑人女孩设计的七周AI故事创作项目，融合了非洲未来主义（Afrofuturism）和黑人女性主义思想，通过AI辅助的反叙事（counter-storytelling）培养参与者的AI素养和未来想象力。案例研究分析显示，参与者创作了根植于自身身份和日常经验的非洲未来主义叙事，并发展了提示工程、偏见批判和数据隐私意识等核心AI素养，证明了在非正式学习环境中整合非洲未来主义故事创作与生成式AI对吸引黑人女孩参与计算机科学教育具有显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学教育中黑人女孩等少数群体代表性严重不足，传统教学方式难以吸引她们参与。现有AI教育缺乏与文化身份相关的教学框架，需要探索更具包容性和文化相关性的策略来缩小数字鸿沟并促进教育公平。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，设计并实施了为期七个周末的AI故事创作项目，面向10-12岁黑人女孩。项目以非洲未来主义和黑人女性主义理论为基础，包含头脑风暴AI相关主题、开发角色与故事情节、进行协作小组展示等活动，并通过分析学习者的创作成果进行评估。

Result: 参与者成功创作了扎根于自身身份和日常经验的非洲未来主义叙事的作品，同时掌握了提示工程（prompt engineering）、偏见批判（bias critique）和数据隐私意识等核心AI素养。

Conclusion: 将非洲未来主义故事创作与生成式AI整合于非正式学习环境中，是吸引黑人女孩参与计算机科学教育的有效途径，为文化响应式AI教育提供了可行模型。

Abstract: This paper presents the design and outcomes of a seven-weekend AI storytelling program developed for Black girls aged 10-12. Grounded in Afrofuturism and Black feminist thought, the program adopted AI-enabled counter-storytelling, supported the development of foundational AI literacies, and fostered future-oriented imagination. Activities included brainstorming AI-related topics, developing character and story plots, and delivering collaborative group presentations. Drawing on the analysis of learners' artifacts from the case study, findings show that participants created Afrofuturist narratives rooted in their identities and everyday experiences. At the same time, they developed core AI literacies, including prompt engineering, bias critique, and awareness of data privacy. This program demonstrates that integrating Afrofuturist storytelling with generative AI in informal learning spaces can be a powerful approach for engaging Black girls in computer science education.

</details>


### [239] [Experiential Learning of Runtime Monitoring Using Pachinko](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16898)
*Miles Scharff,Maria Chemodanova,Mark Santolucito*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个嵌入式系统课程的课堂作业，通过构建交互式弹球游戏（Pachinko）来教授运行时监控。学生使用RTLola规范编写监视器，将其编译为C代码，并部署在双核ESP32上，与传感器和执行器控制逻辑协同运行。该作业在Barnard学院的创意嵌入式系统课程中实施，并设计了可复制的模板、硬件栈、代码库和评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在形式化方法教学中，传统的理论讲授往往难以激发学生兴趣。作者希望通过一个动手的、基于项目的创造性嵌入式系统构建任务，让学习者以实践的方式掌握运行时监控（runtime monitoring）和形式化时序逻辑（formal temporal logic），同时使教学资源可复用到其他课程中。

Method: 该作业的核心方法是：利用双核ESP32平台，让学生用RTLola规范语言编写监控器，将监控器编译为C代码，并部署到嵌入式系统中。监控器在游戏过程中实时记录弹球游戏事件，根据形式化时序逻辑规范触发声音、动画和电机行为。整个作业包含可复制的工作流，涵盖硬件栈、代码库和评估方案。

Result: 该作业已在Barnard学院COMS3930（创意嵌入式系统）课程中实际实施。结果表明形式化方法可以在动手、项目驱动的创造性课堂环境中有效教授，并且该作业的设计具有可移植性，适合嵌入式系统、创意计算或创客空间类课程复制使用。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了将形式化方法融入创造性嵌入式系统项目教学的可行性，通过弹球游戏这一有趣载体让抽象的形式化规范变得具体可感。作业的完整文档化设计使其具备跨课程、跨场景的可复制性，为形式化方法教学提供了一种新的实践导向思路。

Abstract: We present documentation of a classroom assignment that teaches runtime monitoring through a creative embedded systems build: an interactive Pachinko game. The assignment centers on a dual-core ESP32 workflow in which students write RTLola specifications for monitors, compile these monitors to C, and deploy them alongside sensor and actuator control logic. Pachinko game events are logged in real time and used to trigger sound, animation, and motor behavior according to formal temporal logic specifications.
  This work showcases how formal methods can be taught in a hands-on, project-based setting for learners in a creative and classroom-scale setting. We also discuss portability: the assignment template, hardware stack, code base, and assessment approach are designed and documented to be replicated in other embedded systems, creative computing, or makerspace-style courses. This assignment was given to the students of Creative Embedded Systems (COMS3930) at Barnard College.

</details>


### [240] [DOMtutor: Automated Autograding for Logic in Computer Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16899)
*Tobias Meggendorfer*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出将自动评分系统（autograder）应用于理论计算机科学课程（如逻辑学、自动机理论），并采用已成熟的DOMjudge系统（国际大学生程序设计竞赛使用的平台），以提供即时反馈和实践体验，弥补传统理论课程"讲授式+延迟反馈+纯概念练习"的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算机科学理论课程采用"灌输式"讲授和手工批改作业，反馈延迟且练习停留在概念层面（纸上草图），学生缺乏实践检验和即时反思的机会。自动评分系统虽已用于编程实践课，但很少被用于理论课程，形成应用空白。

Method: 作者提出两点核心策略：(i)将自动评分系统（autograder）引入并重点应用于理论课程（如命题逻辑、一阶逻辑、自动机理论）；(ii)直接采用已被广泛验证的DOMjudge平台——该平台是国际大学生程序设计竞赛（ICPC）等赛事使用的成熟系统，具备自动执行和评估代码的能力。

Result: 本文为概念性提议，尚未给出具体的实验或评估结果，重点在于论证将成熟竞赛级自动评分系统迁移到理论课程教学中的可行性与价值。

Conclusion: 理论课程同样可以从自动评分系统中获益，借助DOMjudge这类成熟平台，可在不增加开发成本的前提下为理论课程提供即时反馈和动手实践机会，进而提升学生的学习效果与反思能力。

Abstract: Teaching computer science at universities is often structured rather classically and theory oriented. The former refers to "transmission"-style lectures accompanied by exercises which are submitted and graded manually, providing delayed feedback (if any). The latter refers to exercises often posed at a conceptual level, requiring solution ideas to be sketched out on paper, but not put to the test in practice. By its nature, this is particularly true for subjects relating to theoretical computer science, such as courses on propositional or first-order logic or automata theory. Frameworks that automatically execute and evaluate code (also called autograders) are sometimes used to augment teaching. They provide (near) instant feedback and hands-on experience, prompting reflective analysis. However, their use usually is reserved for programming / practically oriented courses. We propose to (i) use autograders also (and especially) for theoretical courses and (ii) use the established DOMjudge system, which is used, among others, for the International Collegiate Programming Contest.

</details>


### [241] [The use of data from information systems in court proceedings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16901)
*Dobromira Bankova,Vladimir Dimitrov*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文基于保加利亚现行司法实践和新实体法律规制的背景，探讨司法机构如何收集、分析和评估数据作为证据的问题。通过具体案例分析，研究指出在新监管框架下，分析视角应从"作为信息单元的证据"转向"作为行为算法的证据"，这需要技术工具、方法论转变以及收集和评估聚合数字证据的充分准备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据在司法实践中作为证据的使用日益普遍，司法机构面临如何在法庭程序中有效收集和评估数据集的挑战。作者希望通过分析保加利亚的具体案例，揭示现有司法实践中的法律与实际挑战，并指出在新法规背景下证据分析范式需要转变的必要性。

Method: 本文采用案例分析的方法，基于保加利亚当前司法实践中的具体实例，同时结合新的实体法律规制框架，对数据作为法庭证据的收集、分析和评估过程进行考察与分析。

Result: 研究发现，在新监管框架下，证据分析视角需要从"证据作为信息单元"转向"证据作为行为算法"，这一转变不仅依赖技术工具，还需要方法论的调整以及对聚合数字证据收集和评估的充分准备。

Conclusion: 数据的司法使用正经历范式转变，司法机构需要从方法论层面适应聚合数字证据带来的新挑战，而不能仅停留在技术工具层面的升级。

Abstract: This paper examines data in the context of how the judiciary collects, analyses, and evaluates it as evidence, based on examples from current judicial practice in Bulgaria and within the context of the new substantive legal regulations. It explores the legal and practical challenges related to the use of data sets as evidence in court proceedings through the analysis of specific cases. In light of the new regulatory framework, the research points out that the analytical perspective should shift from "evidence as an information unit" towards "evidence as a behavioural algorithm", requiring not only technological tools but also a methodological shift and adequate preparation for collecting and assessing aggregated digital evidence.

</details>


### [242] [Advancing Health Equity through Multi-Level Fairness in Health Informatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16902)
*Nick Souligne,Vignesh Subbian*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文评估了健康信息学中多层级公平性技术（结合多种偏见缓解步骤）的现状，重点关注其对公平医疗结果的影响，并分析MINIMAR和TRIPOD等报告标准在提升模型透明度和解决健康差异中的作用，最后提出改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在医疗领域的整合日益增加，但公平性、透明度和健康公平面临严峻挑战。多层级公平性技术有潜力减少不同患者群体的偏见，但其健康公平结果尚未被充分探索。

Method: 通过系统审视现有文献，评估多层级公平性技术的实施现状及其对公平医疗结果的影响，同时分析报告标准（如MINIMAR和TRIPOD）在模型透明度中的作用，并识别实施和报告方面的关键缺口。

Result: 发现多层级公平性技术的实施存在关键缺口，且健康公平影响的报告不够一致；MINIMAR和TRIPOD等报告标准为机器学习模型报告提供了有价值的基准，但在捕获公平和公平结果方面仍有改进空间。

Conclusion: 建议改进报告透明度、推广多层级公平性技术的广泛应用，并在未来研究工作中明确优先考虑健康公平。

Abstract: The increasing integration of machine learning in healthcare has highlighted critical challenges related to fairness, transparency, and health equity. Specifically, the use of multi-level fairness techniques, which combine multiple bias mitigation steps or techniques, show promise for reducing biases across different patient demographics, yet this approach remains underexplored in terms of its health equity outcomes. In this paper, we assess the current landscape of multi-level fairness in health informatics by focusing on its impact on equitable healthcare outcomes and evaluating how transparency and reporting standards contribute to these advancements. Through an examination of the existing literature, we identify key gaps in both the implementation of multi-level fairness techniques and the consistent reporting of health equity impacts. Furthermore, we analyze the role of reporting standards, including MINIMAR and TRIPOD, in improving model transparency and ensuring that machine learning models in healthcare address health disparities. These standards offer valuable benchmarks for reporting on ML models, yet we identify key opportunities for enhancing how these reports capture fairness and equity outcomes. The paper concludes by providing recommendations that focus on improving transparency in reporting, advocating for the broader adoption of multi-level fairness techniques, and ensuring that health equity is explicitly prioritized in future research efforts.

</details>


### [243] [AI, Brain Death Detection, and Islamic Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16903)
*Muhammad Aurangzeb Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了机器学习系统在检测神经损伤患者隐性意识方面的部署，从临床医学、AI伦理和伊斯兰法理学的交叉视角展开分析，提出应通过伊斯兰法认识论中的三个基础概念（bayyina明确证据、yaqin认识论确定性、以及关于灵魂的神学不可知论）来应对从二元临床判决向概率性、时间粒度的神经状态估计的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI意识检测系统在神经损伤患者中的应用提出了深刻的伦理和法律挑战，特别是在伊斯兰法理学框架下。现有的二元临床判决方式与AI提供的概率性、时间粒度的神经状态估计之间存在根本性差异，需要新的认识论框架来应对。

Method: 本文采用跨学科分析方法：(1)综述AI意识检测的技术文献；(2)将技术现状映射到伊斯兰脑死亡学术讨论的背景下；(3)提出基于伊斯兰法律认识论三个基础概念（bayyina、yaqin和对灵魂的神学不可知论）的分析框架；(4)识别关键挑战并讨论对AI代理决策系统的影响。

Result: 本文识别了AI意识检测从二元判决向概率性、时间粒度估计转变所带来的关键挑战，并展示了伊斯兰法认识论概念如何为这一新兴领域提供理解框架，同时指出了其对AI代理决策系统的潜在影响。

Conclusion: AI意识检测系统需要超越简单的二元判断，而伊斯兰法认识论中的bayyina、yaqin和关于灵魂的神学不可知论为处理这种临床-伦理-法律交叉挑战提供了有价值的框架，对AI代理决策系统的设计和部署具有重要意义。

Abstract: The deployment of machine learning systems capable of detecting covert consciousness in neurologically injured patients creates a profound challenge at the intersection of clinical medicine, AI ethics, and Islamic jurisprudence. We argue that the shift from binary clinical verdicts to probabilistic, temporally granular neural-state estimates should be addressed through three foundational constructs in Islamic legal epistemology: bayyina (clear evidentiary proof), yaqin (epistemic certainty), and the theologically mandated agnosticism about there (soul). We survey the current technical literature on AI-based consciousness detection, map it onto the landscape of Islamic brain death scholarship, and identifykey challenges. We also discuss its implications for AI surrogate decision systems.

</details>


### [244] [Understanding Computing Identity Development Through Mentorship and Epistemic Network Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16904)
*Behdokht Kiafar,Roghayeh Leila Barmaki*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究采用定量人种学方法，通过Epistemic Network Analysis (ENA)分析37名计算机相关领域参与者的开放式调查回答，发现获得导师支持的参与者其计算机身份各维度（兴趣、能力、认可、归属感）连接更紧密，而未获导师支持的参与者其叙述中自我怀疑和冒充者综合征联系更强，两组间存在显著差异（Cohen's d = 1.72）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机身份认同对学生的参与度、坚持性和归属感至关重要，但传统调查量表难以捕捉身份发展的动态和复杂结构。现有研究对计算机身份的表达方式及其与导师支持的关系缺乏深入理解，需要新的分析方法来揭示身份认同的内在结构。

Method: 采用定量人种学（Quantitative Ethnography）方法，使用Epistemic Network Analysis (ENA)对37名参与者的开放式调查回答进行建模，分析六个身份相关构念（认可、兴趣、能力、归属感、自我怀疑、冒充者综合征）之间的共现模式，并比较有导师支持与无导师支持两组参与者的身份叙述结构差异。

Result: 有导师支持的参与者在兴趣、能力、认可和归属感之间表现出更强的连接，形成更整合和支持性的身份结构；无导师支持的参与者则在自我怀疑和冒充者综合征之间表现出更强的连接。两样本t检验显示两组在X轴上的ENA得分存在统计学显著差异，效应量大（Cohen's d = 1.72）。

Conclusion: 导师支持与计算机身份结构的差异相关，可能有助于个体将兴趣、能力、认可和归属感在计算机领域内联系起来，减少自我怀疑和冒充者综合征在身份叙述中的紧密关联。

Abstract: Computing identity plays an important role in students' participation, persistence, and sense of belonging in computing, yet identity development can be difficult to capture through survey measures alone. This study examines how computing identity is expressed in open-ended survey responses from 37 participants in computing-related fields. Using a Quantitative Ethnography approach, we applied Epistemic Network Analysis (ENA) to model co-occurrence patterns among six identity-related constructs: recognition, interest, competence, sense of belonging, self-doubt, and imposter syndrome. We compared the structure of computing identity narratives between participants who reported mentorship support and those who did not. Findings showed that participants with mentorship support had stronger connections among interest, competence, recognition, and sense of belonging, suggesting a more integrated and supportive identity structure. In contrast, participants without mentorship support showed stronger connections involving self-doubt and imposter syndrome, indicating that uncertainty and feelings of not belonging were more closely connected in their narratives. A two-sample t-test comparing ENA scores showed a statistically significant difference between the two groups along the X-axis, with a large effect size (Cohen's d = 1.72). These findings suggest that mentorship is associated with differences in the structure of computing identity and may help individuals connect their interests, abilities, recognition, and belonging within computing.

</details>


### [245] [The politics of postmortem privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16905)
*Mauricio Figueroa*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文揭示"死后隐私"概念的内在张力与不稳定，将其归纳为"死后隐私的政治"，通过跨大西洋分歧、欧洲数据保护内部矛盾以及全球南方后殖民/后威权语境三个分析视角，探讨死后隐私如何在不同的社会中被差异化地建构为协商记忆与尊严的生产性机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然死后隐私的存在已日益被认可（如保护逝者在数字空间中的存在），但其内在不稳定性——适用范围、正当性基础、以及不同司法管辖区的差异表达——远未得到充分关注。作者希望填补这一空白，揭示该概念内部的冲突与张力。

Method: 本文组织并整合现有法律学术文献，将其与更广泛的文化、社会、历史和政治观察进行对话，通过三个分析场所展开论述：欧美之间的跨大西洋分歧、欧洲数据保护治理中的内部张力、以及全球南方的后殖民与后威权语境。

Result: 文章表明，死后隐私并非统一的普遍概念，而是在不同政治与法律语境中被"组装"为不同的形态；现有研究已触及前两个分析维度，但全球南方语境（后殖民/后威权）应获得更多关注与纳入。死后隐私作为社会协商记忆与尊严的生产性注册机制，在逝者数据治理和信息流动中扮演重要角色。

Conclusion: 死后隐私是一个内在多元且政治上高度建构的概念，其承认与实施因司法辖区而异。将全球南方的后殖民与后威权视角纳入死后隐私讨论，有助于深化我们对隐私、记忆与尊严之间关系的理解，并推动更全面的数据治理理论构建。

Abstract: While the existence of postmortem privacy is increasingly acknowledged (such as the protection of the presence of deceased within digital spaces), far less attention has been paid to its internal instability: its scope (the extent of its application), justificatory foundations (why do we protect the deceased in the first place), and uneven articulation across jurisdictions (for example, some jurisdictions may tolerate or endorse practices that may be contestable in a different jurisdiction). This piece unearths the internal diversity of the concept by illuminating specific points of tension and conflict that the notion of postmortem privacy evokes. These points of tension are collectively refer to as the politics of postmortem privacy. To do so, this paper organises existing contributions of legal scholarship, placing them in dialogue with broader cultural, social, historical and political observations to illustrate the politics of postmortem privacy through three different loci of analysis: the transatlantic divide between European and American approaches, intra-European tensions within data protection governance, and postcolonial and post-authoritarian contexts in the Global South. While existing literature has glimpsed toward the former two, this piece contends that the latter deserves greater attention and inclusion in the debates around privacy and the dead. The piece explains, in continuity with existing scholarship, how postmortem privacy is assembled differently as a productive register through which societies negotiate memory and dignity, which play a great role in the governance of data of the dead and information flows.

</details>


### [246] [Effective Personalized AI Tutors via LLM-Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16907)
*Angel Tsai-Hsuan Chung,Botong Zhang,Ling-Chieh Kung,Hamsa Bastani,Osbert Bastani*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实地实验证明，将GenAI聊天机器人导师与强化学习算法结合，利用学生-聊天机器人互动信号自适应排序练习题，可显著提高学生无辅助期末考试成绩（提高0.15个标准差，相当于6-9个月的学习量），且增益主要由学生参与度提高驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GenAI教育平台大多依赖反应式聊天机器人被动回答学生问题，缺乏主动引导学生学习的能力，其教育效果可能未得到充分释放。

Method: 设计了一个将精心设计的GenAI聊天机器人与强化学习算法紧密集成的辅导平台。强化学习算法利用学生与聊天机器人互动的丰富信号，自适应选择适当难度的练习题。与台北市政府和美国在台协会合作，在十所高中开展为期五个月的Python教学部署，并将学生随机分配到固定练习题序列和自适应序列两组进行对比实验。

Result: 自适应排序使无辅助期末考试成绩提高0.15个标准差（相当于6-9个月的学习量）；中介分析表明增益主要由学生参与度提高驱动。

Conclusion: 研究提供了大规模实地证据，表明学生与聊天机器人的互动信号可用于主动优化和个性化学生学习，为GenAI教育平台的设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) is rapidly reshaping education by unlocking the potential for personalized tutoring. Yet, emerging platforms largely focus on GenAI chatbot tutors that reactively answer student questions. We hypothesize that the efficacy of GenAI chatbot tutors can be substantially improved by proactively guiding student learning. To test this, we design a novel tutoring platform that tightly integrates a carefully-designed GenAI chatbot with a reinforcement learning algorithm for sequencing practice problems. Critically, this algorithm leverages rich signals from student-chatbot interactions to adaptively select practice problems of an appropriate difficulty level. In partnership with the Taipei City Government and American Institute in Taiwan, we deployed our tutoring platform in conjunction with a five-month course to teach Python to students across ten high schools. We randomized students between a fixed practice problem sequence and our adaptive sequencing algorithm. We find that adaptive sequencing increased unassisted final exam performance by 0.15 standard deviations (equivalent to 6-9 months of schooling by some estimates); mediation analysis suggests that gains were driven by increased engagement. Our work provides large-scale field evidence that student-chatbot interactions provide valuable signals for proactively optimizing and personalizing student learning.

</details>


### [247] [When Personalization Becomes Bias: Structural and Discursive Religious Framing in AI-Generated Financial Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16909)
*Muhammad Salar Khan,Hamza Umer,Hasan Mahmud,Sandra Rothenberg*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过432次模拟顾问-客户互动，覆盖16种宗教身份配对和三种家庭财务决策，系统性地证明了ChatGPT、Gemini和Grok三个大语言模型在金融建议中普遍存在宗教偏见（仅12-18%的建议无偏见），并揭示了偏见通过宗教锚定、文化信号不均和语气调节等语言机制实现的结构性与话语性双重维度偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益融入金融咨询系统，但其在复制宗教偏见方面的作用尚未得到充分研究。现有研究对LLM金融建议中的宗教偏见证�据不足，亟需系统性实证研究填补这一空白。

Method: 采用混合方法：对三个LLM（ChatGPT、Gemini、Grok）进行432次模拟顾问-客户互动实验，涵盖16种宗教身份配对（基督教、伊斯兰教、印度教、非宗教）和三种金融决策（股票投资、购房、人寿保险）。结合回归分析和反思性主题分析，同时考察结构性和话语性偏见。

Result: 无偏见建议仅占12-18%；Gemini的偏见程度持续高于Grok，ChatGPT与Grok在统计上相当；宗教对称配对几乎总会触发显性宗教框架，非宗教客户常收到以顾问为中心的宗教诉求；股票投资引发更多金融技术性回应，而人寿保险触发更强的宗教语言。

Conclusion: 研究提出了将模型训练与设计中的结构性偏见和语言表达中的话语性偏见相关联的双维度框架，表明AI建议会针对身份线索进行语言适应，揭示了个性化与中立性之间的管理困境，并对企业、金融机构和监管机构确保AI中介建议的中立性、文化敏感性和信任提出了重要启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into financial advisory systems, yet their role in reproducing religious bias remains underexamined. This study provides systematic mixed-methods evidence of such bias across three LLMs (ChatGPT, Gemini, and Grok) using 432 simulated advisor-client interactions spanning 16 religious identity pairings (Christian, Muslim, Hindu, and non-religious) and three core household financial decisions: stock investment, house purchase, and life insurance. Combining regression and reflexive thematic analyses, we identify structural biases across models and decision contexts and the discursive mechanisms through which they are linguistically enacted. Unbiased advice appeared in only 12-18% of cases. Gemini consistently produced more bias than Grok, while ChatGPT's outputs were statistically comparable to Grok's. Religiously symmetric advisor-client pairings almost always triggered explicit religious framing, and non-religious clients often received advisor-centered religious appeals. Qualitative findings show that bias is linguistically manifested through religious anchoring, uneven cultural signaling, and tone modulation, varying by model and financial scenario. Stock investment prompts produced more financially technical responses, whereas life insurance advice triggered stronger religious language. The study develops a dual-dimensional framework linking structural bias rooted in model training and design with discursive bias expressed through language, advancing understanding of algorithmic bias in LLM-generated financial advice. It also shows that such advice adapts linguistically to identity cues, revealing a managerial dilemma between personalization and neutrality. Finally, it highlights implications for businesses, financial institutions, and regulators seeking to ensure neutrality, cultural sensitivity, and trust in AI-mediated advice.

</details>


### [248] [Education-centered critical policy analysis of AI: Ghana's AI strategy as a case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16910)
*Matthew Nyaaba,Vida Awinime Bugri,Eric Kojo Majialuwe,Bismark Nyaaba Akanzire,Ibrahim Nantomah,Felicia Boateng,Patrick Kyeremeh,Benjamin Quarshie,Ellen Kwarteng,Macharious Nabang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究开发并应用了"以教育为中心的AI政策框架"，对加纳《国家人工智能战略（2025-2035）》进行批判性定性政策文档分析，发现该战略在AI素养、青年技能、劳动力准备等方面雄心勃勃，但在教师能动性、课程与评估细节、学习者保护、参与式治理等学校层面实施方面存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各国AI战略虽然日益主导治理、劳动力发展和创新，但学界对它们如何将教育框定为具有教学、文化、伦理和实施需求的部门知之甚少。本研究旨在填补这一空白，聚焦加纳这一案例，考察其国家AI战略中的教育维度。

Method: 采用批判性定性政策文档分析方法，开发并应用了包含六个维度的"以教育为中心的AI政策框架"对加纳国家AI战略进行分析，六个维度分别是：政策目的、教师能动性与专业学习、课程与评估、语言与文化、负责任AI与学习者保护、参与与实施治理。

Result: 加纳战略在教育方面雄心勃勃且及时，尤其强调AI素养、青年技能、职业技术教育与培训（TVET）、劳动力准备、农村覆盖、本地语言数据、包容性和负责任AI治理。但教育议程更多侧重于国家层面的AI准备，而学校层面的实施仍不足：教师能动性、职前教师教育、课程递进、评估指导、AI披露、多语言教学法、文化响应式AI使用、以儿童为中心的保护措施和参与式治理均欠发达。研究还发现文档透明度与连贯性问题，包括AI风格视觉内容未加披露，以及愿景使命图与其文字说明不匹配。

Conclusion: 加纳需要一项部门特定的、以教育为中心的AI政策与实施路径，将劳动力准备与教师培训、课程改革、评估重塑、学习者保护、基础设施、本地语言教学、文化响应式教学法、本地响应的AI工具及参与式治理相连接。

Abstract: National AI strategies increasingly guide governance, workforce development, innovation, and competitiveness, but less is known about how they frame education as a sector with pedagogical, cultural, ethical, and implementation demands. This study develops and applies an Education-Centered AI Policy Framework to analyze Ghana's National Artificial Intelligence Strategy, 2025-2035. Using critical qualitative policy document analysis, we examined the strategy through six components: policy purpose, teacher agency and professional learning, curriculum and assessment, language and culture, responsible AI and learner protection, and participation and implementation governance. Findings show that Ghana's strategy is ambitious and timely, especially in its emphasis on AI literacy, youth skills, TVET, workforce readiness, rural outreach, local language data, inclusion, and responsible AI governance. However, the education agenda is stronger on national AI readiness than on school-level implementation. Teacher agency, pre-service teacher education, curriculum progression, assessment guidance, AI disclosure, multilingual pedagogy, culturally responsive AI use, child-centered safeguards, and participatory governance remain underdeveloped. We also identify document-level concerns about transparency and coherence, including apparent AI-styled visual content without visible disclosure and a mismatch between a vision and mission figure and its textual explanation. We argue that Ghana needs a sector-specific, education-centered AI policy and implementation pathway that connects workforce readiness with teacher preparation, curriculum reform, assessment redesign, learner protection, infrastructure, local language instruction, culturally responsive pedagogy, locally responsive AI tools, and participatory governance.

</details>


### [249] [What Makes a Fairness Gap Actionable? Statistical Actionability for Responsible AI Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16912)
*Hairu Fan,Shiyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"统计可行动性"(Statistical Actionability)框架，将公平性部署重新定义为基于证据的决策问题，综合考虑差异幅度、统计可靠性、子群充分性和部署环境，将证据状态映射为四种建议：缓解、收集更多数据、监控或不立即行动。在模拟实验中将平均决策成本降低了19.2%，并能区分具有相似公平差距但不确定性不同的审计案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性审计只能检测差异，但无法判断差异何时需要干预。已有公平性方法虽能量化差异和不确定性，却缺乏将累积证据转化为具体行动的指导，存在"检测到差异"与"决定是否干预"之间的决策鸿沟。

Method: 提出统计可行动性(Statistical Actionability)框架：(1) 整合公平性证据的四个维度——差异幅度、统计可靠性、子群充分性和部署环境；(2) 将证据状态映射为四种部署建议（缓解、收集更多数据、监控、不立即行动）；(3) 通过校准的部署规则在不同统计环境下泛化。

Result: 在受控模拟中，该方法相比代表性基线实现了最低决策成本，相对基于差距的干预降低19.2%的平均决策成本，同时减少误报和漏检偏差。校准部署规则在五个可迁移性场景中的四个保持在与目标oracle相差2%以内。基准公平性审计分析表明，该框架能区分具有相似公平差距但不确定性和子群支持度不同的审计案例。

Conclusion: 统计可行动性在公平性评估与部署干预之间建立了统计决策层，使负责任AI系统能够基于累积证据而非仅凭差异幅度采取行动，为公平性部署决策提供了系统化、可解释的决策框架。

Abstract: Algorithmic fairness audits can detect disparities, but they do not determine when those disparities warrant intervention. Deployment decisions also depend on the reliability of the evidence, subgroup support, and deployment context. Existing fairness methods quantify disparities and uncertainty, yet provide limited guidance for translating accumulated evidence into action. We introduce Statistical Actionability, a statistical construct that recasts fairness deployment as an evidence-based decision problem. The framework integrates fairness evidence regarding disparity magnitude, statistical reliability, subgroup adequacy, and deployment context, and maps the resulting evidence state to one of four recommendations: mitigate, collect more data, monitor, or take no immediate action. In controlled simulations, Statistical Actionability achieved the lowest decision cost among representative baselines, reducing average decision cost by 19.2% relative to gap-based intervention while simultaneously reducing both false alarms and missed bias. A calibrated deployment rule generalized across heterogeneous statistical environments, remaining within 2% of the target oracle in four of five transportability regimes. Analyses of benchmark fairness audits further demonstrated that the framework distinguished audits with similar observed fairness gaps but different levels of uncertainty and subgroup support, yielding interpretable deployment recommendations. Statistical Actionability therefore establishes a statistical decision layer between fairness evaluation and deployment intervention, enabling responsible AI systems to act on accumulated evidence rather than disparity magnitude alone.

</details>


### [250] [Which CS1 Students Will Fail? Identifying Digital Markers from Learning Analytics in Computer Systems and Architecture Using Weighted Academic Momentum and Interaction Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16914)
*Lighton Phiri,Mutune Chaibela,Ivy Chisha,David Pungwa,Danny Siabbaba,Bydon Simukoko*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究利用来自撒哈拉以南非洲一所大型公立大学四个学期（2017-2021年，N=284）的数据，结合传统和数字标记（人口统计、调查问卷、Moodle交互日志和持续评估成绩），通过逻辑回归和SMOTE+ENN重采样构建了用于预测CS1课程失败的早期预警系统。最佳特征子集为"Base + Demo + LMS"，在测试集上实现74.7%准确率、0.742宏F1和0.800 AUC，在默认阈值下能识别87%的挂科学生（假阳性率41%），SHAP分析确认加权学业动量是最强预测因子。该模型可在学期第五周部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字学习平台产生丰富的行为痕迹（数字标记），有望早期识别学习困难的学生。本文旨在探究传统与数字标记的组合能否在CS1课程中充分有效地预测学生挂科，以便及时干预，特别是在教育资源有限的撒哈拉以南非洲地区。

Method: 采用混合方法：首先通过利益相关者启发式方法识别十个候选因素；然后将这些因素操作化为涵盖人口统计、自我报告调查、Moodle交互日志和持续评估分数的综合特征集；使用逻辑回归配合5折交叉验证和SMOTE+ENN重采样进行系统性消融研究；最后通过SHAP分析进行模型可解释性分析。

Result: 最佳特征子集为"Base + Demo + LMS"（加权学业动量、基本人口统计和LMS活动二元指示器）。逻辑回归在保留测试集上达到74.7%准确率、0.742宏F1和0.800 AUC。在0.5默认阈值下，模型识别出87%的挂科学生，假阳性率为41%。SHAP分析确认加权学业动量是最强预测因子，其次是与LMS参与的交互作用。

Conclusion: 简单数字标记即可在学期第五周构建实用的早期预警系统。研究贡献包括：多源数据集和利益相关者引导的方法论、量化特征组贡献的消融研究，以及可直接部署的可解释高召回率模型。该工作证明了在资源受限环境中通过低成本指标实现学业风险早期识别的可行性。

Abstract: Digital learning platforms generate rich behavioural traces (digital markers) that offer the potential to identify struggling students early. This paper investigates whether a combination of traditional and digital markers can predict failure in a first-year CS1 course (Computer Systems and Architecture) with sufficient recall to enable timely intervention. Using data from four cohorts (2017-2021, N=284) at a large public university in sub-Saharan Africa, we conducted a mixed-methods stakeholder elicitation to identify ten candidate factors. These were operationalised into a comprehensive feature set spanning demographics, self-reported surveys, Moodle interaction logs, and continuous assessment scores. A systematic ablation study using logistic regression with 5-fold cross-validation and SMOTE+ENN resampling revealed that the most predictive feature subset was Base + Demo + LMS: weighted academic momentum (M = 0.1Q1 + 0.15Q2 + 0.2Q3 + 0.55T1), basic demographics (gender, sponsorship, COVID-19 cohort), and a binary indicator of any LMS activity. On a held-out test set, logistic regression achieved 74.7% accuracy, 0.742 macro F1, and an AUC of 0.800. At the default threshold of 0.5, the model identified 87% of failing students (recall = 0.87) with a 41% false positive rate. SHAP analysis confirmed that weighted academic momentum is the strongest predictor, followed by its interaction with LMS engagement. These results demonstrate that simple digital markers can power a practical early-warning system by the fifth week of the semester. Our main contributions are: (1) a multi-source dataset and a stakeholder-guided methodology; (2) an ablation study quantifying feature group contributions; and (3) an interpretable, high-recall model ready for deployment.

</details>


### [251] [WIP: LLM Odyssey: A Game-Based Platform for Teaching LLM Engineering Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16924)
*Priyamvada Tripathi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: LLM Odyssey是一个开源的、基于浏览器的严肃游戏平台，包含13个交互式游戏，用于教授大语言模型（LLM）工程概念（如词元化、Transformer架构、提示工程、RAG和生产部署）。该平台通过三个与Bloom修订版分类法对齐的学习层级（认知核心、系统锻造厂、铸造竞技场）提供结构化的教学路径，并融入了五种来自文献的教学策略。平台已在加拿大大学2026年冬季学期部署初评，并设计了正式的混合方法评估协议（N=50）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM工程概念在计算机科学课程中代表性不足，而现有的交互式工具只涵盖单个概念，缺乏教学脚手架和结构化学习路径，无法系统性地教授LLM工程知识。

Method: 构建包含13个交互式游戏的浏览器平台，设计三个学习层级（认知核心7个基础游戏、系统锻造厂5个生产工程游戏、铸造竞技场顶点挑战），每个游戏融入五种教学策略：即时形成性反馈、基于最近发展区的脚手架提示、基于心流理论的渐进难度、降低认知负荷的示范示例、源自生产实践的真实场景。采用混合方法评估协议，包括前后知识测试、验证问卷、参与度分析和访谈。

Result: 平台已在2026年冬季学期部署于加拿大学院进行初步审查，反馈确认了功能需求，并确定自适应难度为未来开发优先事项。设计了正式的混合方法评估协议（N=50）用于未来评估研究。

Conclusion: LLM Odyssey通过游戏化方式系统性地填补了LLM工程教育的空白，其教学设计基于成熟的教学理论，且通过公开平台和记录在案的评估协议支持可复现的后续研究。

Abstract: This work-in-progress (WIP) innovative practice category paper presents LLM Odyssey, an open source, browser-based serious gaming platform comprising 13 interactive games for teaching Large Language Model (LLM) engineering concepts. Topics such as tokenization, transformer architecture, prompt engineering, retrieval augmented generation (RAG), and production deployment are underrepresented in computer science curricula. Existing interactive tools address individual concepts but lack pedagogical scaffolding or structured learning pathways. LLM Odyssey addresses this gap through three learning tiers aligned with Bloom's revised taxonomy: Cognitive Core (7 foundational games), Systems Forge (5 production engineering games), and Foundry Arena (capstone challenges). Each game incorporates five pedagogical strategies drawn from the literature: immediate formative feedback, scaffolded hints grounded in the Zone of Proximal Development, progressive difficulty informed by flow theory, worked examples to manage cognitive load, and authentic scenarios drawn from production practice. The platform was deployed in Winter 2026 semester at a Canadian college for an initial review. Feedback confirmed functional requirements and identified adaptive difficulty as a priority for future development. A formal mixed methods evaluation protocol (N=50) has been designed, comprising pre and post knowledge tests, validated surveys, engagement analytics, and interviews, and is documented here to enable future evaluation studies with the publicly available platform.

</details>


### [252] [Without journalists, there is no journalism: the social dimension of generative artificial intelligence in the media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17017)
*Simón Peña-Fernández,Koldobika Meso-Ayerdi,Ainara Larrondo-Ureta,Javier Díaz-Noci*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对过去二十年间媒体领域人工智能应用的实证研究进行了系统性回顾，识别出其带来的主要社会与认识论挑战。研究发现：媒体面临技术平台依赖与编辑独立性之间的张力；记者在职业威胁感与摆脱日常琐事、提升内容质量之间摇摆；受众对机器生成文本的质量和可信度感知差异不大，但可读性上仍偏好人类作者。研究主张以社会性视角而非技术中心主义视角来看待AI在新闻业的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能技术正在系统性地改变媒体行业及其从业者的工作方式，但学界对其带来的社会与认识论影响缺乏系统梳理。作者旨在通过系统性文献回顾，整合过去二十年的实证研究成果，识别AI在媒体中应用的核心挑战，并呼吁从社会性视角审视这一问题。

Method: 采用系统性文献回顾方法（systematic review），聚焦过去二十年（约2000-2020年代）关于AI在媒体中应用的实证研究，从媒体机构、记者和受众三个维度分析其面临的挑战与影响。

Result: 研究发现三大核心挑战：（1）媒体层面：对技术平台的依赖加剧与编辑独立性维护之间的冲突；（2）记者层面：面临失业威胁和象征资本流失的焦虑，同时获得摆脱例行任务的解放感，从而可专注于高质量内容生产；（3）受众层面：对自动化文本的质量和可信度感知与人工文本差异不大，但可读性上人类作者仍具优势。

Conclusion: AI在新闻业这一高度人性化领域的应用不能仅从技术中心主义或决定论的角度理解，需要采取社会性研究路径。未来研究应聚焦于AI如何影响个体和记者、如何服务于职业正当目的与社会福祉，以及如何弥合AI使用带来的数字鸿沟。

Abstract: The implementation of artificial intelligence techniques and tools in the media will systematically and continuously alter their work and that of their professionals during the coming decades. To this end, this article carries out a systematic review of the research conducted on the implementation of AI in the media over the last two decades, particularly empirical research, to identify the main social and epistemological challenges posed by its adoption. For the media, increased dependence on technological platforms and the defense of their editorial independence will be the main challenges. Journalists, in turn, are torn between the perceived threat to their jobs and the loss of their symbolic capital as intermediaries between reality and audiences, and a liberation from routine tasks that subsequently allows them to produce higher quality content. Meanwhile, audiences do not seem to perceive a great difference in the quality and credibility of automated texts, although the ease with which texts are read still favors human authorship. In short, beyond technocentric or deterministic approaches, the use of AI in a specifically human field such as journalism requires a social approach in which the appropriation of innovations by audiences and the impact it has on them is one of the keys to its development. Therefore, the study of AI in the media should focus on analyzing how it can affect individuals and journalists, how it can be used for the proper purposes of the profession and social good, and how to close the gaps that its use can cause.

</details>


### [253] [Wasted large language models: A life cycle thinking approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17055)
*Erik Johannes Husom,Maria Emine Nylund,Ophelia Prillard*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出将大语言模型（LLMs）视为可产生废弃物的产品，借鉴欧盟废物框架指令中的废物层级（预防、再利用、回收、恢复和处置）来系统性地减少LLMs的环境影响，特别强调预防不必要的使用是降低碳足迹的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs的开发和使用的碳足迹日益增大，尽管提高能效的努力并未转化为整体能耗的降低，反而因杰文斯悖论（效率提升导致使用增加）而加剧了消耗。因此需要额外的措施来解决这一问题。

Method: 采用生命周期思维，将LLMs视为可能成为废物的产品，应用欧盟废物框架指令中的废物层级五级措施（预防、再利用、回收、恢复和处置），逐一审视这些措施如何应用于LLMs以减少其环境影响的思路与方法。

Result: 废物层级应用于LLMs后显示：预防废物是降低环境影响的核心（主要因为减少训练新模型的需求）；预防可通过现有的再利用、回收和恢复方法实现；处置既有助于节能也对投入训练LLMs的资源保持谨慎态度有重要意义；防止不必要的LLM使用具有巨大的气候影响降低潜力。

Conclusion: 废物层级为减少LLMs环境影响提供了新的系统性思考框架，强调"预防"是最高优先级策略，特别是减少不必要的使用，这对降低气候影响具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are machine learning (ML) models that have an increasingly large carbon footprint through their development and use. Efforts to increase the energy efficiency of these models have not translated into reduced consumption due to rebound effects such as Jevons Paradox - that increased efficiency drives increased use. There is therefore a need for additional measures to solve this problem.
  We suggest that one possible way forward is to use life cycle thinking, and view LLMs as products that can become waste. With this perspective, we investigate the potential of the waste hierarchy from the EU's Waste Framework Directive, which suggests five different measures for how to manage waste: prevention, reuse, recycling, recovery, and disposal. We examine how these measures can inform and motivate new types of thinking and approaches to reducing LLM waste and their environmental impact in general.
  Applying the waste hierarchy to LLMs highlights that preventing waste is essential for reducing the models' environmental impact, mainly because it reduces the need for training new models. Prevention can be achieved through many existing methods for reusing, "recycling", and "recovering" LLMs. Additionally, disposal can be important both for saving energy and for keeping a considerate attitude to the resources being spent on training LLMs. We also call to attention that prevention of unnecessary use of LLMs carry huge potential for lowering the climate impact of the models.

</details>


### [254] [Language Models Reproduce Human Reductionist Bias and Decision Inconsistency in Neurodevelopmental Disorders Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17105)
*Maciej Wodziński,Joanna Wodzińska,Kacper Dudzic,Marcin Moskalewicz*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合方法的人机审计框架，用于评估大语言模型（LLMs）在心理健康决策中的一致性、认知启发式易感性、智识谦逊以及概念操作化。通过对比35名人类专家（18名医生和17名心理学家）与7个LLM，发现两组在功能水平评级与支持资格决策间均无显著关联，且都未表现出对锚定和代表性启发式的显著易感性。LLMs报告了更高的智识谦逊，但与其决策一致性无关。LLMs和医生比心理学家更少授予支持，且对"基本生活需求"概念有不同解释（偏向生物生存和自我照顾，而非沟通和社会需求）。研究认为评估高回报场景中的AI不仅需要衡量准确性、一致性或对认知偏差的抵抗力，还需批判性审视AI所操作化的神经多样性概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs越来越多地参与复杂的心理健康决策（这些决策不仅依赖事实证据，还涉及价值判断），需要系统地评估其在这些高回报场景中的表现。现有评估往往只关注准确性、一致性或认知偏差抵抗力，而忽略了AI所操作化的概念框架和价值内涵，尤其是在神经发育障碍的支持分配判断中。

Method: 采用混合方法的人机审计框架，同时检验决策一致性、对认知启发式（锚定和代表性）的易感性、声明式智识谦逊，以及支持分配判断中操作化的概念。比较对象为35名人类专家（18名医生和17名心理学家）与7个LLM，通过实验操纵和统计分析（包括U检验、效应量计算）进行对比。

Result: 1) 两组的功能水平评级与支持资格决策均无显著关联，显示描述性评估与最终评价性判断间存在不一致；2) 两组均未表现出对锚定和代表性启发式的显著易感性；3) LLMs报告的智识谦逊显著更高（r=.62），但与决策一致性或功能评估无关；4) LLMs和医生比心理学家更少授予支持；5) LLMs对"基本生活需求"的解释偏向生物生存和自我照顾，而非沟通和社会需求。

Conclusion: 尽管LLMs表现出高水平的智识谦逊，它们仍复制了医疗决策中固有的还原主义解释框架和知识。评估高回报场景中的AI需要超越准确性、一致性或认知偏差抗性的传统指标，必须批判性审视AI所操作化的神经多样性概念及其隐含的价值取向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly supporting complex mental-health decisions, which depend not only on factual evidence but also value-laden interpretations. We introduce a mixed-methods human-LLM auditing framework examining decision consistency, susceptibility to cognitive heuristics, declarative intellectual humility, and the concepts operationalized in support-allocation judgments of neurodevelopmental disorders. Comparing 35 humans (18 physicians and 17 psychologists) with seven LLMs, we show that in both groups, ratings of patients' functional level were not significantly associated with support-eligibility decisions, indicating an inconsistency between descriptive assessments and final evaluative judgments. Specifically, we find that neither group showed significant susceptibility to experimental manipulations targeting anchoring and representativeness heuristics. LLMs reported higher intellectual humility than experts (U = 241, p < .001, r = .62; LLMs: M = 41.43, SD = 1.99; experts: M = 29.03, SD = 8.05), but it was unrelated to decision consistency or functional assessment. While LLMs and physicians granted support less frequently than psychologists (U = 180.50, p = .003, r = .34), they also interpreted a concept of "basic life needs" differently, primarily as biological survival and self-care, and not communicative and social needs. These findings suggest that despite expressing high levels of intellectual humility, LLMs reproduce a reductionist interpretive framework and knowledge embedded in medical decision-making. More broadly, we argue that evaluating AI in high-stakes contexts requires not only measuring accuracy, agreement, or resistance to cognitive bias, but also critical examination of the concepts of neurodiversity that AI systems operationalize.

</details>


### [255] [Ready for What? Rethinking AI and Robotics Preparedness for Adoption and Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17520)
*Peng Wang,Naomi Adel,Amy E. Morgan,Folayo Aina,Demos Parapanos,Vikas Mackevicius,Teslim Olayiwola Salahudeen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过982名参与者对17项AI和机器人挑战的15,200次评估，运用重复卡片式调查分离了人与人之间的稳定差异和同一人内部的挑战特异性偏差，发现：同一受访者将某挑战评为比平时更复杂1分时，其准备度降低约0.21分；而普遍认为挑战更复杂的人并不会系统性报告更低准备度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速AI和机器人技术采用需要了解各社区的准备情况，但对不同利益相关者群体取平均值会掩盖同一人在评估不同挑战时出现的关系。需要区分稳定的人际差异和挑战特异性的内部偏差，以提供更精确的政策建议。

Method: 采用重复卡片式调查设计，982名参与者对17项AI和机器人挑战在1-5量表上评估其重要性、复杂性和准备度。由于参与者评估多个挑战，研究设计能够将稳定的个体间差异（between-person）与挑战特异的个体内偏差（within-person）分离开来分析。

Result: 同一受访者中，挑战复杂度比平时高1分与准备度降低0.21分相关（p<0.001）；而普遍认为挑战更复杂的人并不系统性报告更低准备度（p=0.29）。重要性正向关联准备度，但异常高复杂度会削弱这种对齐。这些关系因挑战家族而异，专业背景仍与调整后的准备度相关。对应用型卡片，信心、信任及相关感知为准备度提供了额外信息，包括对未见参与者的预测。

Conclusion: 对政策制定者和组织而言，跨利益相关者取平均值会隐藏挑战特有的障碍。准备度评估应同时保留利益相关者群体间的差异以及同一利益相关者跨挑战的变化。有效的采用与素养策略不仅要问"谁看起来准备好了"，还要问"哪些挑战他们觉得特别困难"以及"可能的约束涉及实施、能力、保证还是资源"。

Abstract: Efforts to accelerate AI and robotics adoption require evidence about where communities are ready to act and where support is still needed. Yet averages across stakeholder groups can obscure relationships that emerge when the same person evaluates different challenges. We analyse a repeated card-based survey in which 982 participants provided 15,200 evaluations of 17 AI and robotics challenges. Each challenge was rated on 1-5 measures of significance, complexity and readiness, where readiness refers to perceived community preparedness and available resources rather than personal competence or realised adoption. Because participants evaluated multiple challenges, the design separates stable between-person differences from challenge-specific within-person deviations. Within the same respondent, a challenge rated one point more complex than usual is associated with about 0.21 points lower readiness (p less than 0.001). By contrast, respondents who generally rate challenges as more complex do not systematically report lower readiness (p=0.29). Significance is positively associated with readiness, while unusually high complexity modestly weakens this alignment. These relationships vary across challenge families, and professional background remains associated with adjusted preparedness. On applied cards, confidence, trust and related perceptions add substantial information about readiness, including for held-out participants. For policymakers and organisations, averaging across stakeholders can hide challenge-specific barriers. Readiness assessments should preserve both differences between stakeholder groups and variation within the same stakeholders across challenges. Effective adoption and literacy strategies should ask not only who appears ready, but which challenges they find unusually difficult and whether the likely constraint concerns implementation, capability, assurance or resources.

</details>


### [256] [Too cheap to matter: over abundant microchips, and what we can learn from them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17541)
*Adrian Friday,Fieke Jansen,Gauthier Roussilhe,Srinjoy Mitra*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了价格低于1美元的"超廉价微芯片"如何成为无处不在的"智能材料"，被集成并一次性嵌入日常物品中。这些占每年销售超400亿颗芯片绝大多数的芯片完全被忽视，且随着新工艺节点推出，老节点仍在持续生产。作者通过ICT4S研讨会的发现，呼吁LOCO社区反思这一系统，发展技能以创造持久技术并减少电子废弃物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最新IC芯片驱动数据中心和AI革命备受关注，但那些廉价、一次性、已完全隐身于日常物品中的微芯片却被忽视。它们占芯片销售量的绝大多数，却因过度封装和快速淘汰而加剧了电子垃圾问题，其真实成本未被充分认识。

Method: 作者在ICT4S会议上举办研讨会，深入探究这些超廉价微芯片设备的真实成本（包括环境、经济和社会层面），并基于研讨会讨论成果提出挑战与行动方向。

Result: 研讨会揭示了这些"隐形"微芯片的真实成本，包括不必要的"智能化"带来的加速过时和电子废弃物累积；同时向LOCO社区提出了具体挑战，主张抵抗这一系统并发展可持续技术技能。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁LOCO（低碳计算）社区关注那些被忽视的廉价微芯片，主动反对"为智能而智能"的无意义封装文化，并培养能创造持久、可维护技术的能力，以遏制不断增长的电子废弃物危机。

Abstract: Ultra-cheap microchips (<$1) are so abundant they've become a 'smart material' integrated and disposable in everyday things. Hidden in our everyday products, we have entirely lost sight of them, yet they account for the vast majority of the >400 billion pieces sold each year. As new technology nodes are released, older ones (from as far back as the 1980s) continue to be produced. These microchips do not exist on their own; they are packaged into every possible item to bring 'smartness', necessary or not; this simultaneously increases their obsolescence. While the latest ICs power our data centres and AI revolution that draws our attention, what about technology so disposable that it has become entirely invisible? We report on our workshop at ICT4S exploring these devices' true costs, and pose challenges to the LOCO community to push back on this system, and develop the skills necessary to create lasting technology and avoid further e-Waste.

</details>


### [257] [Advancing Inclusivity in Cybersecurity Education: Integrating Intersectionality to Enhance Student Engagement in Australian Higher Education Curriculums Strategies, Barriers, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17758)
*Nalin A. G. Arachchilage,Asangi Jayatilaka,Senuri Wijenayake,David Herbert,Kaie Maennel,Nicole Herbert,Claudia Szabo,Gabrielle Murray,Gary Thomas*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过对澳大利亚15位网络安全课程学术人员的半结构化访谈，探讨了女性、性别多元及文化和语言多元（CALD）群体在网络安全教育中的视角缺失问题，识别出四个关键障碍和四个亟需支持的领域，为课程改革提供了重要指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澳大利亚女性、性别多元群体和CALD社区因语言障碍、缺乏网络安全教育和社交孤立等因素更容易遭受网络钓鱼等网络犯罪；同时，高等教育网络安全课程过度偏重技术能力，忽视交叉性（intersectionality）和以用户为中心的思维，导致该领域缺乏多元视角，难以应对复杂的网络安全挑战。

Method: 对来自澳大利亚各州多所大学的15位经验丰富的网络安全课程教学与协调学术人员进行半结构化访谈，围绕三个核心问题展开：当前教学中如何处理女性、性别多元和CALD视角；将这些视角纳入课程面临的障碍；未来需要的工作和支持。

Result: 研究发现澳大利亚高等教育网络安全课程中缺乏系统性的交叉性视角整合方法，具体识别出四个关键障碍以及四个需要支持和未来努力的领域，以解决这一问题。

Conclusion: 研究结果为网络安全教育的课程开发提供了宝贵的洞见，强调了将性别包容性和交叉性视角系统化融入课程设计的必要性，以培养更具多元视角、更能应对复杂网络安全挑战的专业人才。

Abstract: Australian women, gender-diverse individuals, and culturally and linguistically diverse (CALD) communities are often more susceptible to phishing and other forms of cybercrimes due to factors such as language barriers, limited access to cybersecurity education, and social isolation. These communities encounter substantial obstacles both entering and progressing in the cybersecurity field. In Australia, the Higher Education sector still leans heavily on a largely uniform cybersecurity curriculum, focusing heavily on technical proficiency, overlooking the vital impact of intersectionality and user-centered thinking for boosting student engagement and learning. Without gender inclusivity and proper consideration of intersectionality forms such as CALD, the workforce is deprived of the varied perspectives necessary to tackle today's intricate cybersecurity issues. In this study, we conducted semi-structured interviews with 15 experienced academics teaching and coordinating cyber security programs from a diverse range of Australian universities, covering all states, to explore their perspectives on: i) current strategies for addressing the women, gender-diverse and CALD perspective in cyber security education in the Australian HE sector; ii) barriers to incorporate women, gender-diverse and CALD perspective in cybersecurity curriculums in higher education; iii) future work and support that is needed. Our research highlights a lack of systematic methods for integrating intersectional perspectives into cybersecurity curriculums. In particular, we identified four key barriers and four areas where support and future efforts are needed to address this issue. Our findings offer vital insights that can substantially guide curriculum development in cybersecurity education.

</details>


### [258] [Quo Vadis? Scientific Discovery in the Age of Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17970)
*Petr O. Jedlicka*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文审视了AI在科学发现中日益增长的作用，调查了AI能力的快速崛起及其在科学中的扩散证据，提出了AI系统的类型学分类，并综述了各科学领域的最新成就，同时指出当前系统的技术、认知和制度局限及风险，最后探讨了人类与机器认知劳动分工的深层问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在推理、抽象、规划和长时程任务执行等能力上的快速提升，AI正日益融入科学研究过程，需要对这一趋势进行系统性的审视与评估，以理解其影响、局限和风险。

Method: 本文采用多层次的综述方法：(1) 调查AI能力的最新进展；(2) 运用科学计量学证据分析AI在科学中的扩散；(3) 提出一个从专用科学AI到科学AI助手/智能体再到混合实验系统的类型学框架；(4) 基于该框架对数学、物理、化学、生命科学及行为与社会科学进行选择性综述。

Result: 文章揭示了AI在多个科学领域取得的显著成就，但同时论证了当前系统仍受技术、认知和制度层面的重要限制所约束，且其广泛应用正在引入短期和长期风险。

Conclusion: AI在科学中的推进引发了关于人类研究者与机器之间认知劳动分工的更深层问题，需要在推动AI科学应用的同时审慎考量其局限性、风险以及人机协作范式的重塑。

Abstract: This paper examines the growing role of AI in scientific discovery. It first surveys the rapid rise of AI capabilities, especially in reasoning, abstraction, planning, and long-horizon task execution, before turning to scientometric evidence of AI's diffusion across the sciences. It then proposes a typology of AI systems used in research, ranging from specialized scientific AI through scientific AI assistants and agents to hybrid experimental systems that combine computation and physical experimentation. On this basis, it offers a selective overview of recent achievements in mathematics and computer science, physics, chemistry, the life sciences, and the behavioural and social sciences. It argues that, despite these advances, current systems remain constrained by important technical, epistemic, and institutional limitations, and that their growing use introduces both near-term and longer-term risks. The conclusion further suggests that the advancement of AI in science raises broader questions concerning the division of cognitive labour between human researchers and machines.

</details>


### [259] [Traceable Trust for action-ready artificial intelligence in bioscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17997)
*Huayu Xin,Yizhi Cai,Mukilan Deivarajan Suresh,Gavin Michael Farrell,Iwona Gajda,Charlie Harrison,Conor Houghton,Mato Lagator,Yang Lu,Virginia Portillo,Reyer Zwiggelaar,Sebastian Lobentanzer*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"可追溯信任"(Traceable Trust)框架，作为生物科学中AI输出到实验室行动这一关键决策节点的评估与设计框架，通过六个核心问题确保AI驱动的科研决策是可审查、可问责的，并通过三个案例研究加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正成为生物科学工作基础设施的一部分，其输出（结构预测、蛋白质设计、变异排序等）越来越频繁地指导实验室实际决策。但从AI输出到实验室行动的这一转换环节缺乏明确、可审查的流程，这成为确保研究可信度的关键瓶颈，亟需一个系统的信任评估框架。

Method: 提出Traceable Trust框架，围绕六个关键问题构建：(1)什么证据支撑该输出；(2)声称了何种能力；(3)授权了何种代理权；(4)什么阈值授权行动；(5)谁能覆盖/否决该决策；(6)结果如何反馈到后续决策。并通过三个跨生态资源、项目设计和实验室行动的案例研究来验证框架的适用性。

Result: 三个案例研究展示了该框架在不同层面（生态系统资源、项目设计、实验室行动）的适用性，表明信任可以在AI输出开始影响科学工作的节点被系统性地记录和文档化。

Conclusion: 从AI输出到实验室行动的决策边界是可信研究的关键关口，应当遵循明确、可审查的流程。Traceable Trust提供了一个与之相称的评估与设计框架，使生物科学中的AI使用更具可追溯性和问责性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is becoming part of the working infrastructure of the biosciences. AI models can predict biomolecular structures, design proteins, rank variants, annotate images, recommend strains and optimise experimental conditions. We argue that the decision to use an AI output to guide laboratory action is a key juncture for trustworthy research and should follow a defined, reviewable process. We propose Traceable Trust as a proportionate assessment-and-design framework for this output-to-action boundary. It asks what evidence supports the output, what capability is being claimed, what agency has been delegated, what threshold authorises action, who can override it and how outcomes inform later decisions. We illustrate the framework through three case studies spanning ecosystem resources, project design and laboratory action. Together, the cases show how trust can be documented where AI outputs begin to shape scientific work.

</details>


<div id='econ.EM'></div>

# econ.EM [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [The Limits of Experimental Design: Covariate Balance Beyond Low Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18057)
*Max Cytrynbaum*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究实验设计在有限样本中逼近半参数效率界的速度，证明了一个不可能性定理：在弱条件下，除非协变量维度 d ≪ log n，否则没有任何设计能在光滑结果模型类上一致地逼近方差界。基于此，作者提出基于差异最小化（discrepancy minimization）的新设计，通过控制限制复杂度非参数函数类上的不平衡来实现快速逼近，并允许 d ≪ n 个协变量。仿真实验表明，在12个已发表实验中，新设计在所有情形下均优于配对随机化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实验设计（如完全随机化、分层随机化等）旨在逼近半参数效率界，但作者发现有限样本下这一目标可能在大协变量维度下根本不可实现，需要新的设计框架。

Method: 1) 证明不可能性定理：在光滑结果模型类上，除非 d ≪ log n，否则无法一致逼近方差界；2) 提出基于差异最小化的新设计，将目标从控制所有光滑函数的不平衡改为控制限制复杂度非参数函数类的不平衡；3) 在加性非参数设定下实现 d ≪ n 协变量的快速速率；4) 与匹配方法结合以抵御未建模的结果变异。

Result: 新设计在加性非参数设定下允许 d ≪ n 个协变量并实现快速收敛率；在12个已发表实验的校准仿真中，新设计在所有情况下均相比配对随机化降低了方差。

Conclusion: 传统实验设计无法在高维协变量下均匀逼近半参数效率界，而基于差异最小化的设计通过降低目标复杂度，在有限样本中实现了更快的逼近速率和更好的实际表现。

Abstract: We study how fast experimental designs can approach the semiparametric efficiency bound in finite samples, as measured by the excess variance of unadjusted treatment effect estimation. We prove an impossibility theorem: under weak conditions, no design can approach the variance bound uniformly over smooth outcome models unless covariate dimension $d \ll \log n$. Even in experiments with thousands of units, this permits only a handful of covariates. Motivated by this, we propose new designs based on discrepancy minimization that instead attempt to control imbalances over restricted-complexity nonparametric function classes. Such designs achieve fast rates to their corresponding restricted efficiency targets, permitting $d \ll n$ covariates in an additive nonparametric specification. They can also be combined with matching to protect against unmodeled outcome variation. In simulations calibrated to 12 published experiments, our designs reduce variance relative to matched pairs randomization in every empirical setting.

</details>

### [261] [A generic nonparametric value-at-risk estimator for high dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17481)
*Siyuan Sun*

Main category: q-fin.RM

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非参数化的VaR+CVaR算法，适用于任意数量的头寸，通过避免高维空间中的精度退化，直接在历史数据中保留所有高维关系。在500个投资组合（49种期货）的盲测中，99%置信度VaR的超额损失率中位数约为1.0%，表现精准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VaR估计存在两个内在难题：一是过去历史不能直接映射到未来，但所有预测又基于历史；二是VaR估计本质上等同于在高维空间中建模"所有头寸同时亏损"的单一角落，传统方法在高维下精度会急剧退化。

Method: 提出非参数化VaR+CVaR算法，仅使用在高维条件下不损失精度的数学方法，直接将历史数据以所有高维关系原样纳入，不做任何操作或降维处理。

Result: 在500个随机投资组合（涵盖VIX、股指、国债、利率、能源、金属、畜牧、农产品、软商品等49种不同到期日的流动性期货）上进行盲测，99%置信度日VaR的超额损失率中位数在1.0±0.1%之间，68%的投资组合在1.0±0.3%以内，95%的组合在1.0±0.5%以内。

Conclusion: 该算法在高维环境下依然保持高度准确的VaR估计，验证其有效性和稳健性，可应用于任意数量的底层头寸。

Abstract: We present in this article a non-parametric value-at-risk (VaR+CVaR) algorithm that remains accurate for an arbitrarily large number of underlying positions. The algorithm solves the two inherent problems of VaR estimation. First, past history is not directly applicable to the future, but all predictions of the future are based on the past. Second, VaR estimation is equivalent to modeling a single corner of a high-dimensional space (the corner where all bets lose simultaneously). The algorithm only uses mathematical methods that strictly do not degrade in accuracy at high-dimensions. Historical data are then directly incorporated with all high-dimensional relationships present, without manipulation. We test the algorithm with an ensemble of 500 portfolios with random positions across 49 distinct liquid futures of different expiries (VIX, equity indexes, gov. bonds, rates, energy, metals, livestock, agriculture, and softs). All VaR estimations are performed strictly blind to the future. The median portfolio rate of loss exceeding the 99% confidence daily VaR estimate is between $1.0\pm0.1$% depending on algorithm input parameters. 68% of portfolios have a rate of loss exceeding 99% VaR between $1.0\pm0.3$%, and 95% of portfolios between $1.0\pm0.5$%.

</details>


### [262] [Communicating Credit Risk with Large Language Models: Evaluation of Explanations from Standard and Alternative Data-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17715)
*Sahab Zandi,Noah Kostesku,Christophe Mues,María Óskarsdóttir,Cristián Bravo*

Main category: q-fin.RM

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型（LLM）能否作为解释层，将信用风险模型（XGBoost、GNN）的技术性事后解释（SHAP、GNNExplainer）转化为面向不同利益相关者的风险叙述文本。使用Freddie Mac贷款数据构建三条管道，并通过自动评估和人工研究（对比专业人士与非专业人士）验证解释质量，发现证据表征是制约解释质量的关键因素而非模型本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用决策是高风险任务，模型输出必须既准确又可解释以支持合规决策。虽然XGBoost和GNN等现代信用风险模型提升了预测性能，但其解释对利益相关者而言过于技术化，造成沟通鸿沟，进而影响审批、拒贷及公平性判断。需要弥合技术解释与业务沟通之间的差距。

Method: 使用Freddie Mac单户贷款数据构建三条管道：（1）标准表格管道（XGBoost + SHAP）；（2）纯网络管道（GNN + GNNExplainer）；（3）双模态管道（结合表格与网络数据）。采用三种LLM配置生成叙述：小型微调LLM（Gemma 3 4B）、大型微调LLM（DeepSeek R1 70B）、零样本商业LLM（Gemini 2.5）。通过自动化检查评估所有管道，并对双模态解释进行人工研究，比较信用风险专业人士与非专业人士在八个决策相关维度上的评估。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）管道对证据基础评分方差的贡献大于语言模型，说明解释质量的约束瓶颈是证据表征而非模型；（2）解释叙述能可靠地指出影响因素，但在表述影响方向时可靠性较低，这可能对不利行动沟通产生重大影响；（3）专业人士比非专业人士采用更严格的证据标准。

Conclusion: 研究为风险模型的治理提供启示，包括部署考量和领域对齐LLM在受监管信用环境中的价值。建议在将LLM用作信用决策解释层时，优先优化证据表征质量，并关注影响方向表述的可靠性问题。

Abstract: Credit decisioning is a high-stakes task in which model outputs must be accurate and explainable to support compliant decisions. Although modern credit risk models such as eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) and Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) improve predictive performance, their explanations are often too technical for stakeholders creating communication gaps that can shape approvals, denials, and fairness judgments. We examine whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can serve as explanation layers that translate post-hoc explanation artefacts into stakeholder-appropriate risk narratives. Using Freddie Mac single-family loan-level data, we develop three pipelines: standard tabular (XGBoost + SHAP), and two with alternative data, a pure network-based (GNN + GNNExplainer), and a bimodal one (combining tabular and network data). We generate narratives with three LLM configurations: a small fine-tuned LLM (Gemma 3 4B), a large fine-tuned LLM (DeepSeek R1 70B), and a zero-shot commercial LLM (Gemini 2.5). Explanation quality is evaluated through automated checks across all pipelines and a human study of bimodal explanations comparing credit risk professionals and non-professionals on eight decision-relevant dimensions. We have three main findings. First, the pipeline accounts for higher variance in evidence-grounding scores than the language model, meaning that the binding constraint on explanation quality is the evidence representation, not the model used. Second, the explanation narratives reliably name the influential factors but are less reliable when stating the direction of influence, which may be consequential for adverse-action communication. Finally, professionals apply stricter evidentiary standards than non-professionals. We discuss implications for the governance of risk models, including deployment considerations and the value of domain-aligned LLMs in regulated credit settings.

</details>

### [263] [Potential of ChatGPT in predicting stock market trends based on Twitter Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.06273)
*Ummara Mumtaz,Summaya Mumtaz*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了ChatGPT利用社交媒体推文和情感分析预测股票市场走势的能力，针对微软和谷歌两家科技巨头，发现ChatGPT的评估与次日股票表现之间存在正相关关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ChatGPT的兴起带来了AI领域的显著变革，其出色的对话能力和语言理解能力使其具有跨领域的应用潜力。研究者希望验证ChatGPT能否利用Twitter等平台上的海量情感数据，为股票趋势提供有价值的预测，从而拓展生成式AI在金融预测领域的应用边界。

Method: 研究者让ChatGPT分析社交媒体推文的情感倾向（正面、负面或中性），并将这些情感评估与微软和谷歌两家科技公司的股票走势进行关联分析，以检验推文情感对次日股价的预测能力。

Result: 研究发现ChatGPT的情感评估与两家科技公司次日的股票表现之间存在正向关联，表明ChatGPT的推文情感分析能够为股价走势提供有意义的预测信号。

Conclusion: 该研究丰富了人们对ChatGPT跨领域适应性的认识，强调了AI在塑造金融市场预测方面日益增长的重要性。

Abstract: The rise of ChatGPT has brought a notable shift to the AI sector, with its exceptional conversational skills and deep grasp of language. Recognizing its value across different areas, our study investigates ChatGPT's capacity to predict stock market movements using only social media tweets and sentiment analysis. We aim to see if ChatGPT can tap into the vast sentiment data on platforms like Twitter to offer insightful predictions about stock trends. We focus on determining if a tweet has a positive, negative, or neutral effect on two big tech giants Microsoft and Google's stock value. Our findings highlight a positive link between ChatGPT's evaluations and the following days stock results for both tech companies. This research enriches our view on ChatGPT's adaptability and emphasizes the growing importance of AI in shaping financial market forecasts.

</details>
