### [264] [Bounds for multi-horizon stochastic optimization with application to power generation and transmission expansion planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17121)
*Giovanni Micheli,V Varagapriya,Francesca Maggioni,Guzin Bayraksan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了多时域随机优化问题（特别是电力发输电扩展规划）中求解最优值下界的计算高效方法，通过对场景树进行系统性分割（仅运营层、仅战略层、或两者同时），并设计两种重组方式获得有效下界，形成单调不等式链逼近原问题最优值。数值实验表明所提方法在计算效率上具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多时域随机优化问题同时包含战略（长期）和运营（短期）两个时间尺度的序贯决策，在不确定性跨越多个时间层时，直接求解在计算上不可行。因此需要开发高效的定界技术来近似原问题的最优值。

Method: 基于场景树分割的定界方法：系统性地对（i）仅运营场景树、（ii）仅战略场景树、或（iii）两类场景树同时进行分割，并设计了两种重组方式来获得有效下界。每种方法均生成一条单调递增的不等式链，从下方逼近原问题的最优值。其中一种重组方法在运营层和同时分割下产生显著更少的子组数量，从而大幅降低计算开销。

Result: 在多时域混合整数发输电扩展规划问题上的数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性，展示了不同分割与重组策略下的计算效率；其中一种重组策略在运营层和同时分割场景下子组数量显著减少，带来了可观的计算节省。

Conclusion: 该研究为多时域随机优化问题提供了一套系统且计算高效的定界框架，特别是通过精心的场景树分割与重组策略设计，能在保证有效下界的同时显著降低计算负担，对大规模电力系统规划具有实用价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates computationally efficient methods for deriving bounds on the optimal value of multi-horizon stochastic optimization problems, with a particular focus on applications in power generation and transmission expansion planning. Multi-horizon stochastic programs capture sequential decision-making under uncertainty across multiple time scales---e.g., strategic (long-term) and operational (short-term)---jointly. Due to their inherent complexity, especially when uncertainties span several time horizons, solving these problems directly becomes computationally prohibitive. To address this, the paper develops and analyzes various novel bounding techniques, based on the dissection of scenario trees. We investigate systematically dissecting (i) only the operational, (ii) only the strategic, or (iii) both scenario trees simultaneously, and we devise two ways to recombine them to obtain valid bounds. Each method leads to a monotonic chain of inequalities that approximate the optimal value of the original problem from below. One of these methods results in a significantly smaller number of subgroups to recombine in the operational and simultaneous dissections, leading to substantial computational savings. Numerical results on a multi-horizon mixed-integer generation and transmission expansion planning problem show the efficiency of the proposed approach through different dissection and recombination strategies.

</details>


### [265] [Self-Consistent Adjoint Policy Iteration for Constrained Dynamic Portfolio Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17808)
*Jeonggyu Huh,Yeoneung Kim,Seungwon Jeong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于仿真的策略迭代方法，用于处理具有可预测收益和凸约束的连续时间投资组合选择问题。外层步骤在部署后重新评估固定潜在OL-BPTT伴随变量并求解带约束的更新，通过移位伴随消项控制伴随与HJB哈密顿-梯度之间的差异。对于CRRA投资组合，精确HJB策略迭代可识别最优约化价值因子，而总体OL-BPTT迭代在占用测度相对误差条件下全局收敛。定理匹配的审计给出最大95%上限端点为0.074（阈值要求0.75）。在三因子、五十资产设计中，当前策略重新评估在两种评估法则下均优于匹配的池化细化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间投资组合选择问题在实际中面临可预测收益和凸约束的挑战，传统方法难以高效求解。作者希望通过仿真驱动的策略迭代方法，将深度学习（OL-BPTT伴随方法）与经典HJB策略迭代理论相结合，在保证收敛性和理论可靠性的同时提升实际求解效率。

Method: 提出仿真化策略迭代框架：外层步骤在策略部署后重新评估固定潜在OL-BPTT伴随，并求解带凸约束的更新；采用移位伴随消项技术控制伴随与HJB哈密顿-梯度之间的差异；针对CRRA投资组合给出精确HJB策略迭代和总体OL-BPTT迭代两种收敛分析路径。

Result: 在理论层面证明了CRRA情形下精确HJB策略迭代能识别最优约化价值因子，总体OL-BPTT迭代在占用测度相对误差条件下全局收敛。数值实验中，定理匹配的审计最大95%上限端点为0.074，远低于0.75阈值；在三因子五十资产设计中，当前策略重新评估方法在两个评估法则下均优于匹配的池化细化方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明仿真化策略迭代能够将基于神经网络的OL-BPTT方法与经典HJB策略迭代理论有效融合，在凸约束连续时间投资组合问题中同时实现理论收敛保证和实际优异表现，为高维动态投资组合优化提供了可行且可靠的求解框架。

Abstract: We develop simulation-based policy iteration for continuous-time portfolio choice with predictable returns and convex constraints. Each outer step re-evaluates a fixed-latent OL-BPTT adjoint after deployment and solves the constrained update. Shifted-adjoint cancellation controls the adjoint--HJB Hamiltonian-gradient discrepancy by the policy-improvement residual. For CRRA portfolios, exact HJB policy iteration identifies the optimal reduced value factor, while population OL-BPTT iteration converges globally under an occupation-measure relative-error condition. A theorem-matched audit yields a maximal 95% upper endpoint of 0.074 against the required 0.75 threshold. In a three-factor, fifty-asset design, current-policy re-evaluation outperforms matched pooled refinement under both evaluation laws.

</details>


### [266] [Feedback approaches for set-point stabilization of interacting particle systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17222)
*Daniel Jannik Happ,Birgit Jacob,Claudia Totzeck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类受Cucker-Smale模型启发的交互粒子系统的渐近镇定问题，目标是将粒子系统稳定到指定的零速度构型。作者没有手动求解IDA-PBC匹配方程来设计反馈，而是采用瞬时滚动时域控制算法推导反馈，并证明该反馈保持了端口-哈密顿结构，可在IDA-PBC框架下解释。通过闭环哈密顿量的能量平衡证明了粒子速度和相互作用力在离散与连续时间下的收敛性；在控制算子满射的条件下，进一步证明了粒子位置收敛到指定构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，对交互粒子系统进行渐近镇定需要手动求解IDA-PBC匹配方程，这一过程繁琐且困难。作者希望探索一种替代方法，通过滚动时域控制算法自动推导反馈，从而简化控制器设计过程，同时保持系统的端口-哈密顿结构以获得物理意义和稳定性分析工具。

Method: 采用瞬时滚动时域（instantaneous rolling horizon）控制算法来设计反馈控制器，而非手动求解IDA-PBC匹配方程。该方法利用了端口-哈密顿系统的结构特性，使闭环系统保持端口-哈密顿结构，从而能够在IDA-PBC框架下进行解释和分析。

Result: 1）推导出的反馈控制器保持了交互粒子系统的端口-哈密顿结构；2）利用闭环哈密顿量的能量平衡，证明了粒子速度和相互作用力在离散时间和连续时间下的收敛性；3）在控制算子为满射的条件下，进一步证明了粒子位置收敛到指定构型。

Conclusion: 滚动时域控制方法为交互粒子系统的渐近镇定提供了一种有效的替代方案，避免了手动求解IDA-PBC匹配方程的复杂性，同时保留了端口-哈密顿结构的优势。该方法能保证速度和相互作用力的收敛，且在控制算子满射时还能保证位置收敛。

Abstract: We explore the problem of asymptotically stabilizing a class of interacting particle systems to a prescribed particle configuration with zero velocity. The class of interacting particle systems is motivated by the Cucker-Smale model on the mean-field level. Rather than designing a feedback by manually solving an IDA-PBC matching equation, we derive a feedback using an instantaneous rolling horizon control algorithm. We show that this feedback preserves the port-Hamiltonian structure of the interacting particle system and therefore allows for an interpretation in the IDA-PBC framework. Additionally, the energy balance of the closed-loop Hamiltonian provides an important tool to prove convergence of the particles' velocities and interaction forces in discrete and continuous time. If the control operator is surjective, we additionally prove convergence of the particle positions to the prescribed configuration.

</details>


### [267] [Maximum Tsallis Entropy Distributions for Robust and Efficient Sparse Learning from Correlated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17244)
*Kai Yang,Masoud Asgharian,Celia M. T. Greenwood*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出使用qGaussian分布（源于Tsallis熵最大化）替代传统高斯分布，以应对统计稀疏学习中相关性和异质性数据的建模挑战，并结合Hager-Zhang共轭梯度算法开发了数值稳定高效的稀疏统计学习优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯分布假设在统计稀疏学习中存在明显局限，特别是在处理相关性和异质性数据时缺乏鲁棒性，难以应对异常值和潜在的分布假设偏差。这一限制在生物统计领域尤为突出，如遗传学和纵向研究中普遍存在的相关观测与异质性数据。

Method: 1）通过Tsallis熵最大化重新推导多元概率密度函数，用于相关数据的建模；2）提出一个新的框架，将寻找流平衡点的数值方法适配到统计稀疏学习中常见的复合优化问题；3）将该框架应用于Hager-Zhang共轭梯度算法，开发了一个数值稳定且高效的稀疏统计学习算法。

Result: qGaussian分布基于Tsallis熵最大化原理，被证明是高斯分布方法的一个可行且灵活的替代方案，能够有效处理相关性和异质性数据，并在稀疏统计学习中展现出数值稳定性和高效性。

Conclusion: 本文不仅推进了统计分布和优化技术的理论理解，还为实际数据分析（特别是生物统计领域）提供了新的方法和思路。

Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Gaussian distribution assumptions in statistical sparse learning, particularly in modeling correlated and heterogeneous data. Conventional Gaussian models often lack robustness towards outliers and underlying distribution assumptions. To overcome these limitations, we propose the use of the $q$Gaussian distribution, derived from Tsallis entropy maximization, as a robust alternative. This is notably relevant in biostatistics, where the presence of correlated observations and heterogeneity, such as in genetic and longitudinal studies, is prevalent. Our contributions include modeling of correlated data through the re-derived multivariate probability density function from Tsallis entropy maximization, thereby addressing the limitations inherent in conventional Gaussian models. Furthermore, we introduce a novel framework that adapts numerical methods designed to find equilibria in flows to tackle composite optimization problems prevalent in statistical sparse learning. Applying this framework to the Hager-Zhang conjugate gradient algorithm \cite{Hager2005}, we develop a numerically stable and efficient algorithm for sparse statistical learning. The $q$Gaussian distribution, informed by the principle of maximizing Tsallis entropy, presents a viable and flexible alternative to Gaussian-based methods. This paper not only contributes to the theoretical understanding of statistical distributions and optimization techniques, but also paves the way for practical data analysis.

</details>


### [268] [Mirror Polyak and a Primal-Dual Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17252)
*Frederik Kunstner,Ryan D'Orazio,Victor S. Portella,Adrien Taylor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了将Polyak步长推广到镜像下降（mirror descent）的变体——"mirror Polyak"方法，该方法基于Bregman投影（源自Kiwiel 1997），能够自适应相对平滑性、Lipschitz连续性和强凸性条件，并进一步通过凸对偶的凸起公式化来避免需要知道最优值的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶方法通常需要依赖目标函数正则性条件的特定步长，Polyak步长作为经典替代方案只需知道最优值即可自适应各种正则性条件。然而许多优化问题更适合非欧几里得几何下的镜像下降，但现有的Polyak步长推广依赖范数而非纯相对几何，排除了镜像下降的许多应用场景。

Method: 作者重新审视了Kiwiel（1997）提出的基于Bregman投影的Polyak步长变体（称为mirror Polyak），该方法已知具有渐近收敛性但收敛速率未知。作者证明了mirror Polyak享有与欧几里得版本相似的保证，能自动适应相对的平滑性、Lipschitz连续性和强凸性。此外，文章提出了基于凸对偶的凸起公式化方法（提升问题的最优值恰好为零），利用问题结构的自然镜像映射，避免了需要预先知道最优值的问题。

Result: mirror Polyak方法在非欧几里得几何下获得了与欧几里得Polyak步长相似的收敛保证，并能够在结构化优化问题（如正则化线性回归和逻辑回归）中避免对最优值的先验知识需求。将mirror Polyak应用于提升后的凸对偶问题，在最坏情况下的保证与原问题已知最优值时的Polyak步长相同。

Conclusion: mirror Polyak方法成功地将Polyak步长的自适应性扩展到镜像下降框架，克服了之前推广方法依赖范数而非相对几何的局限，并通过对偶提升技巧解决了实际中最优值未知的困难，同时保持了对正则性条件的自适应能力。

Abstract: First-order methods typically require a specific step-size that depends on the regularity conditions of the objective function, such as the smoothness, Lipschitz continuity, or strong convexity constants. The Polyak step-size is a classical alternative for subgradient descent on convex functions that only uses knowledge of the optimal value of the objective function and automatically adapts to the above-mentioned regimes. However, many optimization problems are better described by non-Euclidean geometries and are more amenable to mirror descent. Extending this adaptivity to mirror descent is subtle. Some existing generalizations of the Polyak step-size rely on norms instead of purely on relative geometry, excluding many of the use cases of mirror descent. In this work, we revisit a variant of the Polyak step-size based on Bregman projections due to Kiwiel (1997), which we call mirror Polyak. This method is known to converge asymptotically, but its convergence rate is not known. We show that mirror Polyak enjoys guarantees similar to its Euclidean counterpart, automatically adapting to relative notions of smoothness, Lipschitz continuity, or strong convexity. We then leverage mirror Polyak to avoid having to know the optimal value in some structured optimization problems such as regularized linear and logistic regression. We propose a lifted formulation based on convex duality with optimal value exactly zero and a natural mirror map given by the problem's structure. Mirror Polyak applied to the lifted problem enjoys the same worst-case guarantees as the Polyak step-size in the original problem if we knew the optimal value.

</details>


### [269] [A note on the complexity of random subspace model-based methods for derivative-free optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17307)
*Coralia Cartis,Lindon Roberts*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明在基于随机子空间模型的导数自由优化算法中，通过适当的重新缩放使用Johnson-Lindenstrauss变换（JLTs），相比未缩放的JLTs可以获得改进的最坏情况评估复杂度界。该改进不需要修改原始算法或复杂度分析，并且在维度依赖性方面匹配了基于模型的DFO中已知的最佳界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导数自由优化（DFO）算法在实际应用中非常重要，但随机子空间模型下的评估复杂度界可以通过选择合适的随机投影矩阵来优化。之前的算法虽然使用了JLTs，但未达到最优的复杂度界，作者希望通过重新缩放来改进而不改变算法本身。

Method: 在Cartis & Roberts提出的随机子空间模型DFO算法框架中，对Johnson-Lindenstrauss变换应用适当的重新缩放（rescaling），从而在保持原算法结构不变的前提下获得改进的最坏情况评估复杂度界。

Result: 采用重新缩放的JLTs后，算法获得了比未缩放JLTs更优的最坏情况评估复杂度界，该界在维度依赖方面与Scheinberg & Chaudhry使用Haar矩阵取得的已知最佳界相匹配。

Conclusion: 仅仅通过选择合适的重新缩放策略来使用JLTs，就能在不修改算法或分析的前提下达到模型基于DFO的最佳已知复杂度界，这是一个纯理论层面的改进，具有简洁性和通用性。

Abstract: We demonstrate that, with a suitable rescaling, using Johnson-Lindenstrauss transforms (JLTs) in the random subspace model-based derivative-free optimization (DFO) algorithm from [Cartis & Roberts, Math. Prog. 199 (2023)] achieves an improved worst-case evaluation complexity bound compared to 'unscaled' JLTs. This improved bound does not require any modification to the original algorithm or complexity analysis, and matches the best-known bound in terms of dimension dependence for model-based DFO (e.g. achieved by [Scheinberg & Chaudhry, ICM 2026] using Haar matrices in a similar framework).

</details>


### [270] [Operator-based data embedding for data-driven control of continuous-time systems from noisy data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17518)
*Masashi Wakaiki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的状态反馈增益设计方法，用于连续时间系统的镇定、H2控制与H∞控制，该方法基于算子数据嵌入来刻画与噪声数据一致的所有系统集合，并给出数据信息性的充要条件（以LMI形式），同时提供连续时间信号重构误差的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统连续时间系统的控制器设计依赖于精确的系统模型，然而在实际中系统往往未知或不精确，只能获取被噪声污染的采样数据。需要一个从噪声数据出发直接设计状态反馈控制器的数据驱动方法，同时需要处理过程噪声、测量噪声和输入扰动带来的不确定性。

Method: 1) 使用算子数据嵌入（operator-based data embedding）刻画与噪声数据一致的所有可能系统集合；2) 针对特定噪声类别推导数据信息性（data informativity）的必要与充分条件，并将其转化为线性矩阵不等式（LMI）；3) 从LMI的解构造反馈增益；4) 为连续时间信号的离散采样重构误差提供上界分析，以便直接从噪声采样数据设计控制器。

Result: 获得了数据信息性的必要且充分条件（以LMI形式表达），并成功从LMI解构造出能够实现镇定、H2控制与H∞控制的反馈增益。同时得到了连续时间信号重构误差的理论上界，保证了从噪声采样数据设计控制器的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为连续时间系统在噪声数据下的数据驱动控制设计提供了完整的理论框架，不仅覆盖了镇定、H2和H∞三类控制目标，还通过算子数据嵌入和LMI工具保证了条件的可检验性和控制器的可构造性。

Abstract: We propose a data-driven method for designing state-feedback gains that achieve stabilization, $H_2$-control, and $H_\infty$-control for continuous-time systems. The state-input data are assumed to be corrupted by process noise, measurement noise, and input disturbances. We first characterize the set of all systems consistent with the noisy data using operator-based data embedding. This characterization yields necessary and sufficient conditions for data informativity under a certain class of noise. These conditions are formulated as linear matrix inequalities, and the feedback gains are constructed from their solutions. To enable direct controller design from noisy sampled data for continuous-time systems, we also obtain an upper bound on the reconstruction error of continuous-time signals.

</details>


### [271] [Mixed-integer programming formulations for optimal reconfiguration of supply chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17667)
*Lavinia M. P. Ghilardi,Olga Walz,Steffen Klosterhalfen,Calvin Tsay*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文开发了混合整数规划（MIP）公式，用于在供应链面临能源转型等外部因素引发的结构性变化时，识别最优的供应链重构方案，同时保持工业运营和盈利能力。通过提出产品和空间邻域概念来限制可行搜索空间，实现快速启发式求解，并在农业废弃物案例中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供应链作为生产和交付高价值产品的互联网络，正日益受到能源转型和其他外部因素的影响而产生结构性变化。需要一种系统化的方法来识别最优的供应链重构方案，以在应对这些变化的同时保持工业运营的连续性和盈利能力。

Method: 开发混合整数规划（MIP）公式来寻找最优供应链重构方案；提出产品邻域和空间邻域两个概念来限制可行搜索空间，从而实现快速启发式求解；该限制方法结合了结构信息和基于产品的信息，支持探索和定义多种重构场景。

Result: 通过农业废弃物案例研究展示了该方法的能力，能够快速产生高质量的解决方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的MIP框架结合邻域限制策略，能够有效地在复杂供应链重组问题中快速获得高质量解，为应对能源转型等外部变化下的供应链重构提供了可行的方法论。

Abstract: Supply chains are interconnected networks of processes and operations producing and delivering high-value products. These chains are increasingly subjected to structural changes from the energy transition and other external factors. To address this, this work develops mixed-integer programming formulations to identify optimal reconfigurations that preserve industrial operations and profitability. We propose products and spatial neighborhoods to restrict the feasible search space and enable fast heuristic solutions. Furthermore, this restriction combines structural and product-based information, thus allowing to explore and define multiple reconfiguration scenarios. We demonstrate the approach using an agricultural waste case study, showing its ability to quickly produce good quality solutions.

</details>


### [272] [Resource Extraction from a Stochastic Population with Harvesting Quotas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17778)
*Erik Ekström,Dante Mata,Harto Saarinen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个遍历型资源开采问题，其中收获配额通过当前生物量的函数来限制收获率。在扩散动力学背景下，文章给出了最优收获策略为阈值型（threshold type）的条件。与无限制的奇异控制情形相比，最优阈值可能为0，即始终以最大可能速率进行收获。此外，文章还证明了当收获率趋于无穷时，解收敛到相应的奇异控制问题的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际资源管理（如渔业、林业）中，收获配额是常见的政策工具，但以往的研究多集中于无限制的奇异控制问题。本文旨在填补有配额限制情形下的最优收获策略研究的空白，并考察其与无限制情形的联系。

Method: 采用遍历型随机控制框架，在扩散动力学模型中加入依赖当前生物量的收获率限制函数，推导最优收获策略的结构性质（阈值类型），并通过渐近分析研究收获率趋于无穷时与奇异控制问题的收敛关系。

Result: 给出了最优收获策略为阈值型的充分条件；发现与无限制奇异控制情形不同，最优阈值可能为0（即始终以最大速率收获）；证明了收获率趋于无穷时解收敛到相应的奇异控制问题解。

Conclusion: 配额的引入显著改变了最优策略的结构，可能使收敛被完全忽略（阈值退化为0）；同时，有配额限制的问题在极限意义下与经典奇异控制问题建立了一致性联系。

Abstract: We study a resource extraction problem of ergodic type, where harvesting quotas are imposed that restrict the harvesting rate by a function of the current biomass. In a setting with diffusive dynamics, we provide conditions under which the optimal harvesting strategy is of threshold type. In contrast with the unrestricted case with singular controls, the optimal threshold may be 0, which corresponds to harvesting at the maximal possible rate at all times. Furthermore, we establish the convergence of the solution to the corresponding singular control problem as the harvesting rate tends to infinity.

</details>


### [273] [Isoperimetric problem for quasi-Finsler metrics and shapes of least aerodynamic resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17783)
*Lev Lokutsievskiy,Alexander Plakhov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究牛顿空气动力学框架下的一个二维模型：一个凸体在稀薄介质中向前运动并缓慢振荡，给定振荡规律，目标是找到固定面积下阻力最小的物体形状。通过将问题转化为拟Finsler度量的等周问题来求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在稀薄介质中运动且伴有振荡的凸体的最优形状设计问题，这是牛顿空气动力学中的一个经典优化问题，对工程应用具有理论指导意义。

Method: 将最小阻力问题转化为关于拟Finsler度量的等周问题；引入两个振荡小量准则；通过该准则判断最优体的奇异点在边界前部或后部的出现条件；在均匀振荡情形下求解精确的最优形状。

Result: 证明了最优体的奇异点出现在边界前（后）部的充要条件是前（后）者准则成立；边界其余部分是光滑的；在均匀振荡情形下找到了精确的最优形状并显式构造了若干形状。

Conclusion: 该工作为带振荡约束的牛顿最小阻力问题提供了完整的数学刻画，给出了最优体的奇异结构判据以及均匀振荡情形下的精确解。

Abstract: We consider the following model in the framework of Newtonian aerodynamics: a 2D convex body moves forward and slowly oscillates in a rarefied medium on the plane. The law of oscillations is given. The problem is to find a body of fixed area that has the smallest resistance. We solve this problem by reducing it to an isoperimetric problem for quasi-Finsler metrics. Further, we introduce two criteria of smallness of oscillations. We show that the optimal body has singularity at the front (back) part of its boundary iff the former (latter) criterion is satisfied. The rest of the boundary is smooth. Finally, we find exact optimal shapes in the case of uniform oscillations and construct several shapes explicitly.

</details>


### [274] [Optimal W-infinity Control of Prandtl-Ishlinskii Hysteresis Model via Weak Derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17791)
*Daniel Neri Cardoso,Petrus Emmanuel Oliveira Gomes Brant Abreu,Guilherme Vianna Raffo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向含Prandtl-Ishlinskii磁滞动态系统的鲁棒最优W∞控制器。通过加权Sobolev空间Wm,p,Γ，利用弱导数严谨处理磁滞的非可微与输入非仿射特性，将Pr-Ishlinskii算子重铸为作用于输入速率的界内不确定性，进而借助线性矩阵不等式设计鲁棒最优控制器，并保证W3,2,Γ稳定性及W∞增益界。压电致动器数值实验验证了渐近跟踪与磁滞/外部扰动衰减性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁滞现象广泛存在于压电、磁致伸缩等智能材料致动器中，其非光滑、非可微及输入非仿射特性给传统控制器设计带来严峻挑战，需要一种能够在数学上严谨处理这些难点的鲁棒最优控制方法。

Method: 采用加权Sobolev空间Wm,p,Γ框架，以弱导数替代经典导数来处理磁滞的非可微性；将Prandtl-Ishlinskii算子重新表述为带界的不确定性乘子作用于输入导数，从而将问题转化为线性矩阵不等式可解的鲁棒最优控制问题，并建立W3,2,Γ稳定性分析与W∞增益指标。

Result: 在压电致动器模型上的数值研究验证了所提控制器的有效性，实现了渐近跟踪，并通过简单直接的实现方式有效衰减了磁滞效应与外部扰动。

Conclusion: 该工作为含磁滞动态系统提供了一种理论上严谨且实现简便的鲁棒最优控制方案，兼具稳定性保证、最优性能与扰动抑制能力，为智能材料致动器的高精度控制提供了可行途径。

Abstract: This work proposes a novel robust optimal W-infinity controller for dynamic systems with Prandtl-Ishlinskii hysteresis. By utilizing weighted Sobolev spaces Wm,p,Gamma, the approach uses weak derivatives to rigorously handle the non-differentiable, input non-affine nature of hysteresis. This formulation recasts the Prandtl-Ishlinskii operator as a bounded uncertainty multiplying the input rate, enabling robust optimal controller design via linear matrix inequalities, while guaranteeing W3,2,Gamma-stability with a W-infinity-gain bound. A numerical study on a piezoelectric actuator model validates its effectiveness, demonstrating asymptotic tracking and the attenuation of both hysteresis and external disturbances through a straightforward implementation.

</details>


### [275] [A geometric reformulation of the bilevel parameter optimization problem to a single level non-linear programming problem with applications to phase equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17806)
*Stefan C. Endres,Lutz Mädler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何重构方法，将相包络生成中的双层参数估计优化问题转化为单层问题，使得全局最优解可证明地求解，并适用于包括非光滑、非连续模型在内的最广泛類別的热力学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相平衡问题在化工中至关重要，但生成相包络需要求解计算昂贵的双层优化问题，这限制了复杂热力学模型的可靠拟合与精确建模。

Method: 通过几何重构将双层优化问题重新表述为更易求解的单层问题，同时保留约束条件（如正确相分裂次数、排除虚假相、在不稳定区域保证稳定性），并提出一种对任意黑箱热力学模型均可证明收敛的算法，仅需Gibbs自由能表达式，无需导数。

Result: 使用专门的全局优化求解器时，单层问题的解可证明为双层问题的全局最优解；所提算法对最广泛类别的非光滑、非连续模型均保证收敛。

Conclusion: 该方法使从业者能够可靠高效地拟合数学上复杂的热力学模型，有望实现此前难以处理的计算热力学高精度严格建模。

Abstract: Phase equilibrium problems are central to chemical engineering, underpinning tasks ranging from separation process design to the development of thermodynamic models. A particularly challenging computational task is the generation of phase envelopes: rigorously fitting thermodynamic models to real world data with well behaved predictions requires solving a computationally expensive bilevel optimization problem. We present a geometric reformulation of this parameter estimation problem that restates the bilevel program as a single level problem that is significantly easier to solve. The solution of the single level problem is proven to be the globally optimal solution of the bilevel problem when specialized global optimization solvers are used. In addition, the method retains the constraints that guarantee a well behaved fitted model, such as enforcing the correct number of phase splits, excluding spurious phases, and ensuring stability in regions of instability. This allows the practitioner to reliably and efficiently fit mathematically complex thermodynamic models to data, and potentially enables highly accurate and rigorous modelling of problems in computational thermodynamics that were previously intractable. Finally, an algorithm is presented that is proven to converge for any black box thermodynamic model. Only an expression of the Gibbs free energy is required, no derivatives are needed, and convergence is guaranteed for the broadest class of non-smooth, non-continuous models.

</details>


### [276] [A single loop method for quadratic minmax optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17830)
*Stefano Cipolla,Oliver Stein,Alain Zemkoho*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有耦合内层约束的二次极小极大问题，提出了一种基于对数障碍函数的不可行内点型单循环方法来计算一类平稳点，并证明了非退化平稳点是算法沿中心路径的吸引点，在特殊情形下算法具有多项式复杂度，数值实验表明其在平稳点质量上优于现有算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有耦合内层约束的二次极小极大问题，现有的计算方法在处理平稳点方面存在不足。作者希望证明所计算的平稳点在非退化条件下具有局部最优性意义，并提出一种有效的算法来求解该类平稳点，以弥补现有算法在平稳点质量上的缺陷。

Method: 首先证明非退化平稳点在适当条件下是问题的局部最优点；然后基于对数障碍函数构建一个不可行内点型单循环方法（不显式区分外层与内层问题），并证明非退化平稳点是算法沿设计中心路径移动时的吸引点；在内层可行集独立于外层变量的特殊情形下，证明了算法具有多项式复杂度。

Result: 数值实验在合成数据以及一类最小费用流问题上展示了所提方法的性能，结果表明该方法在计算平稳点的质量上优于现有文献中的算法。

Conclusion: 该文提出了一种新的内点型单循环方法用于求解带耦合内层约束的二次极小极大问题，理论上保证了平稳点的有意义的局部最优性和算法的收敛性，实践中展现出优于现有算法的性能。

Abstract: We consider a quadratic minmax problem with coupled inner constraints and propose a method to compute a class of stationary points. To motivate the need to compute such stationary points, we first show that they are meaningful, in the sense that they can be locally optimal for our problem under suitable{non-degeneracy} conditions. Then based on a suitable log barrier function, we build an infeasible interior point-type {single loop method} (which does not explicitly distinguish between the outer and inner problem) and prove that a non-degenerate stationary point is an attraction point as the algorithm moves along the designed central path. We show in particular that our method is polynomial in the special case where the inner feasible set of our constrained minmax problem is independent from outer variables. Our numerical experiments, on both synthetic data and a class of min-cost flow problems, showcase the behavior of our method and how it outperforms existing algorithms from the literature in terms of the quality of the computed stationary points.

</details>


### [277] [Exact and Heuristic Methods for $Γ$-Robust Min-Max Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17876)
*Yasmine Beck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文总结了作者博士论文中的研究成果，针对混合整数线性最小-最大（min-max）双层优化问题，在Γ-鲁棒框架下处理不确定数据，提出了精确和启发式求解方法，并在560个背包拦截问题实例上进行了计算评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层优化在现实中广泛存在但因其嵌套结构而本质难解，若同时考虑混合整数约束和数据不确定性，求解难度进一步加大。现有方法在此类复杂场景下往往效率不足，亟需更有效的求解方案。

Method: 针对混合整数线性min-max问题，在Γ-鲁棒不确定集下建模，分别设计了精确求解算法和启发式算法来处理该类双层问题，并将方法应用于背包拦截问题（knapsack interdiction problem）进行验证。

Result: 在560个背包拦截问题实例上的计算研究表明：所提出的启发式方法能够显著缩小最优性间隙（对相当一部分实例），并且在实际运行中往往优于现有的启发式和精确基准方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的启发式方法在Γ-鲁棒混合整数双层优化问题上表现出良好的实际性能，能够有效平衡求解质量与计算效率，为求解此类复杂问题提供了切实可行的方案。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization is a powerful tool for modeling hierarchical decision-making processes, which arise in various real-world applications. Due to their nested structure, however, bilevel problems are intrinsically hard to solve, even if all variables are continuous and all parameters of the problem are exactly known. Further challenges arise if mixed-integer aspects and problems under uncertainty are considered. In this article, we summarize selected results from the author's dissertation. We study mixed-integer linear min-max problems with a $Γ$-robust treatment of uncertain data, for which we present exact and heuristic solution approaches. The performance of the methods is assessed in a computational study on 560 instances of the knapsack interdiction problem. Our results show that the heuristic closes the optimality gap for a significant portion of the considered instances and often practically outperforms both heuristic and exact benchmark approaches.

</details>


### [278] [Stochastic Robust Linear W-infinity Control via Dynamic Output Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17910)
*Daniel Neri Cardoso*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对线性Itô扩散的鲁棒W-infinity最优控制框架，采用加权Sobolev空间性能度量，通过期望状态的弱导数处理样本路径不可微问题，并基于LMI半定规划实现动态输出反馈综合，保证均方最终有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Itô扩散的样本路径不可微，传统控制框架难以直接应用；需要一种新的性能度量来恰当刻画随机系统的鲁棒控制性能，同时保证稳定性与扰动衰减。

Method: 采用加权Sobolev空间性能度量，利用期望状态的弱导数替代传统导数；建立基于LMI的半定规划进行动态输出反馈综合；通过严格的稳定性分析保证均方最终有界性并最小化最终界。

Result: 数值示例表明，所提出的方法能有效实现扰动衰减，并具有快速的瞬态性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的W-infinity框架为线性Itô扩散系统提供了一种鲁棒且实用的控制综合方法，在稳定性保证与性能优化之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: This paper introduces a robust W-infinity optimal control framework for linear Itô diffusions using a weighted Sobolev-space performance measure. Because the sample paths of Itô diffusions are nondifferentiable, the formulation leverages the weak derivative of the expected state. An LMI-based semidefinite program is developed for dynamic output-feedback synthesis, and a rigorous stability analysis guarantees mean-square ultimate boundedness with minimized ultimate bound. A numerical example demonstrates that the proposed approach provides effective disturbance attenuation with fast transient performance.

</details>


### [279] [Global Optimization of Flexible District Heating Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18046)
*Marc E. Pfetsch,Lea Rehlich,Florian Steinke,Stefan Ulbrich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了区域供热网络的全局优化运行问题，针对单时间步引入多种加速空间分支定界算法的新方法（排除循环流、对称性利用、温度混合约束缩减、新启发式与分支规则），使求解实例数翻倍、运行时间减半；针对多时间步提出时间分解方法，在合理假设下可证明最优，求解速度极快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域供热网络是实现低碳供热的核心工具，但面临可再生能源、热能存储和网状拓扑带来的异构性与时变性挑战。现有方法难以高效求解基于非线性网络模型的全局优化问题，尤其是多时间步耦合存储的复杂场景。

Method: 1) 建立基于稳态但真实非线性网络模型的多时间步全局优化问题；2) 对单时间步的空间分支定界算法引入四种新方法：排除循环流、供应/回水网络间对称性利用、温度混合约束缩减、新的原始启发式和分支规则；3) 对多时间步（由存储耦合）采用时间分解方法，并在实际合理假设下证明其最优性。

Result: 在生成基准实例上，新方法使求解实例数量翻倍以上、运行时间减半以上；在真实世界基准实例上，算法能在合理时间内提供有质量保证的解。对于多时间步，时间分解方法在小规模示例网络上不足一秒即可求得最优解，而完整求解时间耦合问题在一小时内不可行。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法组件显著提升了区域供热网络单时间步优化问题的求解性能，时间分解方法有效解决了多时间步耦合存储的可扩展性瓶颈，为实际工程应用提供了高效且质量有保证的求解方案。

Abstract: District heating networks are a central tool to achieve low-carbon heat supplies. In this realm, they face the challenge of dealing with increasingly heterogeneous, partially time-varying renewable sources, thermal storage, and meshed topologies. This paper examines global optimization of the operation of such district heating networks over multiple time steps, based on a stationary, yet realistical nonlinear network model.
  To accelerate the solution performance of a spatial branch-and-bound algorithm for one time step, the following new methodological ingredients are introduced: exclusion of cyclic flow, symmetry exploitation between supply and return networks, reduction of temperature mixing constraints, novel primal heuristics and branching rules. The proposed methods are evaluated on a set of generated and real-world benchmark network instances with cycles and several suppliers. On the generated benchmark instances, using these methods more than doubles the number of solved instances and more than halves the runtime. For the real-world benchmark instances, the resulting algorithm produces solutions with guaranteed quality in reasonable run time.
  For multiple time steps that are coupled by a storage, a time decomposition approach is investigated. Under assumptions that are reasonable in practice, this approach is shown to yield an optimal solution. On a small example network, this decomposition is able to compute optimal solutions in less than a second, while solving the complete time-coupled problem is not possible within one hour.

</details>
